[1] Tracking Representations in LLM Reasoning Traces for Iterated Games cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.GTPDF

Chaipat Tirapongprasert, Rajarshi Das, Parijat Dube

TL;DR: 论文研究LLM智能体在不完全信息迭代博弈中的决策过程，利用机制可解释性技术追踪其链式思维推理轨迹与模型内部计算的关系。作者发现模型会在CoT中形成并不断修正对博弈的内部表示，但一旦过早形成错误信念，后续很少能够纠正，这种失败同时体现在显式推理文本和层激活中。

Details

Motivation: 现有LLM智能体广泛使用链式思维，但外部化推理轨迹与模型真实计算之间的关系尚不清楚，尤其缺乏对多智能体战略推理错误来源的诊断。

Result: 摘要未给出定量benchmark结果，主要提供机制可解释性定性发现：模型形成并迭代修正博弈内部表示，错误信念一旦过早确立则难以恢复，且该失败在推理轨迹和层激活中均可观察到。

Insight: 创新点在于将机制可解释性用于分析LLM在迭代不完全信息博弈中的战略推理，追踪CoT中的内部表示演化，并定位早期错误承诺导致不可恢复失败的机制表现；客观上看，这为多智能体系统战略推理错误提供了更聚焦的诊断视角。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents achieve state-of-the-art performance across domains through chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning. Despite their ubiquitous use, little is understood about the relationship between the externalized trace and the model’s actual computation. We address this by investigating how LLM agents make decisions in iterative games with incomplete information. Using a series of mechanistic interpretability techniques, we find that the model forms and iteratively revises an internal representation of the game throughout the CoT. Once the model prematurely commits to an incorrect belief, it rarely recovers. This failure is manifested in both the literal trace and the layer activations. Ultimately, as multi-agent systems are more prevalent, our work opens the door to a more focused diagnosis of their strategic reasoning errors. For illustrative purposes, we offer an interactive visualizer of our methodology on selected sample traces at https://interp-self.github.io/gametrace/

[2] Beyond Replacement: Levenshtein Editing for Diffusion Language Models cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Shuaidi Wang, Zhuocheng Gong, Xuejie Liu, Jianguo Li, Yu Zhang

TL;DR: 论文提出 LED（Levenshtein Editing for Diffusion Language Models），一个针对扩散语言模型的后训练框架，将现有只允许替换已解码 token 的“仅替换编辑”（ROE）扩展为包含插入与删除的 Levenshtein 式序列精修。方法依赖两个训练设计：用最长公共子序列（LCS）对齐在线构造编辑监督，以及多阶段训练让模型接触并修正自身早期错误。在 16B 的 LLaDA2.1-Mini Base 上，该方法在十二个基准上普遍提升，并显著改善大 block size 下的鲁棒性与生成多样性。

Details

Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型解码大多把已解码 token 视为固定，近期方法虽允许替换 token（ROE），但面对缺失或多出 token 造成的序列位移效率很低，且当错误散布在多个位置时难以纠正，因此需要更强大的自纠正编辑机制。

Result: 在 16B 的 LLaDA2.1-Mini Base 上，置信度引导解码时十二个基准平均分从 54.86 提升至 57.10，同时每次前向的 token 数从 6.14 提升至 7.05；无置信度引导解码时平均分从 27.52 大幅提升至 47.19；block size 为 128 时平均分从 36.97 提升至 51.34，并在大 k 的 pass@k 上优于无编辑的阈值解码。

Insight: 创新点在于把编辑操作从“替换”扩展到插入与删除，实现 Levenshtein 式精修；用 LCS 对齐在线重建编辑监督，无需人工标注即可学习插入/删除；并用多阶段训练让模型暴露于自身错误以学会自我纠正。从客观角度看，将编辑距离与序列对齐思想引入扩散语言模型的解码后训练是自然且有效的思路，且不依赖置信度引导也能带来大幅提升，揭示了 DLM 的多样性潜力。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) offer a flexible alternative to autoregressive models, enabling any-order infilling. While most existing decoding methods treat decoded tokens as fixed, some recent models allow previously decoded tokens to be revised only by replacing them with alternative tokens, a paradigm we refer to as replacement-only editing (ROE). Although ROE can correct individual tokens, it is inefficient for sequence shifts caused by missing or extra tokens and struggles when errors occur across many positions. To address these limitations, we propose LED (Levenshtein Editing for Diffusion Language Models), a post-training framework that extends ROE with insertion and deletion, enabling Levenshtein-style refinement of generated sequences. It uses two training designs. First, it reconstructs edit supervision on the fly during supervised fine-tuning using longest-common-subsequence alignment, enabling insertion and deletion learning without manually annotated labels. Second, it adopts multi-stage training, where the state from one stage becomes the input to the next, exposing the model to its earlier errors and enabling it to correct them. When applied to the 16B-parameter LLaDA2.1-Mini Base, LED raises the average score across twelve benchmarks from 54.86 to 57.10 under confidence-guided decoding while increasing tokens per forward from 6.14 to 7.05, whereas under confidence-free decoding it raises the average score from 27.52 to 47.19. We further show that LED achieves higher pass@k at large k than thresholded decoding without editing, demonstrating improved generation diversity and revealing the diversity potential of DLMs without confidence guidance. LED also improves robustness to large block sizes, raising the average score from 36.97 to 51.34 at block size 128. Together, these results suggest that LED supports more robust self-correction in DLMs.

[3] A Hybrid RAG Framework for Deceptive Review Detection: Integrating RoBERTa-LoRA, FAISS-Based Retrieval, and LLM Reasoning cs.CLPDF

Andrei Kostin, José Ángel Diaz-Garcia, Karel Gutiérrez-Batista

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于虚假评论检测的混合RAG框架，将RoBERTa-LoRA分类、基于FAISS的语义向量检索和LLM推理结合，实现自动识别垃圾/欺骗性评论。系统在平衡和严重不平衡的真实场景中评估，展示跨域迁移能力和证据支撑的可解释性。

Details

Motivation: 在线虚假评论和欺骗性意见会带来误导信息与欺诈风险，论文旨在构建能自动区分垃圾评论并提升跨域鲁棒性与可解释性的检测系统。

Result: 实验显示仅5个epoch微调即可实现较强域迁移，跨域评估F1达0.90，超过OpenAI Detector（F1=0.82），并与域内专用SOTA模型fakeRoBERTa（F1=0.97）具有竞争力；同时在平衡和严重不平衡真实场景中保持相近整体检测性能，并取得统计显著的精确率提升。

Insight: 创新点在于将RoBERTa-LoRA分类、FAISS语义检索与LLM最终推理集成到RAG式虚假评论检测流程中，兼顾跨域迁移、证据支撑的可解释性和不平衡场景评估；客观看，核心贡献更偏向多模块系统整合与面向真实复杂场景的实证验证。

Abstract: The growth of deceptive online opinions poses a critical risk of misinformation and fraud. This paper proposes a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system to automatically distinguish spam reviews. The architecture integrates RoBERTa with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) classification, FAISS semantic vector retrieval, and a Large Language Model (LLM) for final reasoning. The system was evaluated across balanced and severely imbalanced real-world scenarios. Results demonstrate that five epochs of fine-tuning enable robust domain transfer, achieving an F1-score of 0.90 in cross-domain evaluation, outperforming baselines (OpenAI Detector,F1 = 0.82) and demonstrating competitive performance against specialized in-domain SOTA models (fakeRoBERTa,F1 = 0.97). The proposed architecture provides evidence-backed explainability and statistically significant precision gains while maintaining comparable overall detection performance.

[4] Grading Executable Legal Rules Against Independent Records: Transitional Provisions and Korean SME Status cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Jinhyung Bae

TL;DR: 论文提出 LexTemporis，用独立真实记录而非评分者自己对法条的理解来评估由 LLM 编写的可执行法律规则。以韩国上市公司 10,901 个公司-年度的 SME 状态披露为基准，并纳入适用规则的所有版本，考察过渡条款等对判定结果的影响。研究发现，按评分者自身解读打分可能高估法律程序的效果，而独立记录能更客观地检验规则是否正确。

Details

Motivation: 现有法律可执行程序常由人阅读同一法条后生成的答案来评分，这会奖励与评分者相同的解读，而非真正符合法律和事实。论文希望解决法律规则自动执行评估中的偏差问题，改用实际发生的独立记录作为金标准。

Result: 在韩国 SME 状态披露数据上，一个过渡条款使程序与披露记录的一致率从 83-86% 提升到 89-90%；在 189 个解读分歧的公司-年度中，有 130 个支持作者对争议条款的解读。四个 LLM 在不同法条选择下编写规则，预注册比较未发现某种条件在四个模型上全面更优；探索性分析显示，按作者解读评分会缩小某些模型的优势，并在作者解读错误处显著高估表现。

Insight: 创新点在于用独立真实记录评估可执行法律规则，并强调主法条之外的过渡条款对日期相关判定至关重要。客观来看，该工作揭示了 LLM 生成法律程序评估中“与评分者同读法条”的偏差，为法律 NLP 和程序化规则评测提供了更可靠的基准思路。

Abstract: Laws are increasingly turned into programs that decide cases, such as whether a firm was a small or medium enterprise (SME) on a given date, and large language models (LLMs) now write them. The programs are usually graded against answers written by someone who read the same law, so a program can score well by sharing the grader’s reading rather than by getting the law right. LexTemporis grades them instead against records of what actually happened: 10,901 firm-years of SME status disclosed by Korean listed companies, with every version of the governing rules. An appendix adds comparison cases from US income tax and German hospital minimum volumes. The date changes outcomes mainly through provisions outside the main statutory text: one transitional provision raises agreement with the disclosures from 83-86% to 89-90%. The disclosures also test the author’s reading of a contested provision, favouring it in 130 of 189 firm-years where readings diverge. Four LLMs wrote the rules, given different selections of the law. A pre-registered comparison finds no condition better across all four models. In exploratory analyses, supplying the right provisions looks better graded against the author’s reading than against the records: the advantage shrinks for three models (for one, to between a quarter and three-fifths of its size), and per firm-year it is several times larger where the author’s reading is wrong, partly by construction, since there reproducing that reading more faithfully must lose. Grading legal programs against the grader’s own reading can overstate how well they work.

[5] MIRAGE: A Framework for Multi-hop Reasoning under Dynamic Knowledge Injection cs.CLPDF

Heyang Xu, HanWen Hao, Haifeng Wang, Haoyi Zhou, Jianxin Li

TL;DR: 论文研究动态知识注入下的多跳问答，提出首个覆盖冲突知识（K2）与增量新知识（K3）的基准 NEXUS，并设计免训练框架 MIRAGE；其核心组件通过相关性感知双路径推理与基于 P(True) 的置信度路由，缓解模型在每一跳选择错误知识源的问题，并用模板引导校正器修正残余结构偏差。

Details

Motivation: 现有多跳问答知识更新研究多关注简单事实编辑，忽略了更现实的知识注入场景：模型需同时处理与参数记忆冲突的更新和全新事实，常因信任错误知识源导致跳级答案错误。

Result: 在四个 LLM 系列和两个基准上实验，MIRAGE 一致提升最终答案准确率与跳级推理准确率，并在该挑战性设定下达到新的 SOTA。

Insight: 创新点在于提出覆盖冲突与增量知识的多跳推理基准 NEXUS，以及免训练的 MIRAGE 框架；客观看，其可借鉴之处是用双路径推理显式区分参数知识与注入知识，并通过置信度路由动态选择可信来源，辅以轻量校正器提升多跳推理鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multi-hop Question Answering (MQA) requires large language models (LLMs) to retrieve and synthesize multiple pieces of evidence into a coherent reasoning path, which is essential for complex tasks. However, prior work on knowledge updating for MQA has largely focused on simple fact edits, overlooking a more challenging and realistic setting—\emph{Knowledge Injection}—in which the model must simultaneously accommodate both conflicting updates and entirely novel information. Under this setting, a hop-level answer is often wrong simply because the model trusts the wrong knowledge source: it clings to outdated parametric memory when the injected knowledge conflicts with it (K2), and it cannot answer when the required fact is entirely new (K3). To investigate this, we introduce NEXUS, the first benchmark tailored for multi-hop reasoning under Knowledge Injection, which explicitly covers both conflicting (K2) and incremental (K3) knowledge. We further propose MIRAGE, an efficient, training-free framework whose core component, the Correlation-aware Dual-path Reasoner (CDR), resolves such per-hop source-trust errors via P(True)-based confidence routing, complemented by a lightweight template-guided corrector for residual structural deviations. Extensive experiments across four LLM families and two benchmarks demonstrate that MIRAGE consistently improves both final-answer accuracy and hop-level reasoning accuracy, establishing a new state of the art in this challenging setting.

[6] MECI: Multi-Element Collaborative Interaction for Multimodal Entity Linking cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Jie Peng, Yongxue Shan, Yongfu Zha, Xiaodong Wang

TL;DR: 论文提出 MECI 框架用于多模态实体链接（MEL），通过多视图专家网络、层级多模态交互模块和基于大语言模型的生成式语义精炼重排序，缓解多模态上下文中实体链接的“元素主导”问题，从而更准确地将提及链接到知识库实体。

Details

Motivation: 多模态实体链接需要捕获提及-实体间的语义一致性以及跨模态互补关联，但现有方法依赖耦合交互或粗粒度全局聚合，容易导致元素主导，难以充分建模多层级对应关系。

Result: 在 WikiDiverse、RichpediaMEL 和 WikiMEL 三个数据集上，MECI 均优于现有 SOTA 基线，Hits@1 分别提升 1.95%、7.30% 和 2.31%。

Insight: 创新点主要包括：用“分而治之”的多视图专家网络解耦特征学习，并以 KL 引导路由控制专家专业化与协作；设计动态模态感知加权的层级多模态交互模块融合多粒度证据；引入大语言模型进行零样本生成式语义精炼与重排序。

Abstract: Multimodal Entity Linking (MEL) aims to disambiguate mentions in multimodal contexts by grounding them to specific entities in a knowledge base. A pivotal challenge in MEL is capturing multi-level correspondences: the semantic consistency between mention-entity pairs and the complementary correlations across modalities. However, existing methods often suffer from element dominance due to their reliance on coupled interactions or coarse global aggregations. In response, we propose the Multi-Element Collaborative Interaction (MECI) framework. First, to capture multi-element mention-entity correspondences, we develop a Multi-view Experts Network that leverages a “divide-and-conquer” strategy for decoupled feature learning to mitigate element dominance, supported by a KL-guided routing mechanism that governs expert specialization and collaboration. Furthermore, to model cross-modal complementary correlations, we propose a Hierarchical Multimodal Interaction Module, where a dynamic modality-aware weighting network refines interactions across hierarchical semantic levels, thereby integrating multi-granular evidence to counteract element dominance. Finally, we incorporate a generative semantic refinement stage that utilizes large language models for zero-shot re-ranking. Extensive experiments on WikiDiverse, RichpediaMEL, and WikiMEL show that MECI consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, improving Hits@1 by 1.95%, 7.30%, and 2.31%, respectively.

[7] ProbMedTOD: A Bayesian Network Guided Task-Oriented Dialogue System for Patient History Taking cs.CLPDF

Vishal Vivek Saley, Bhavesh Gurnani, Dinesh Raghu, Mausam

TL;DR: 论文提出ProbMedTOD，一个贝叶斯网络引导的任务导向对话系统，用于自动进行患者病史采集。它通过三阶段（本体构建、结构预测、参数估计）从少量临床笔记中自动合成临床贝叶斯网络，并与小语言模型集成，利用贝叶斯网络代理实时维护诊断后验分布，策略代理选择诊断信息量大的问题。

Details

Motivation: 小语言模型在病史采集中缺乏显式的审慎推理（系统2）机制，难以随时间维护和更新诊断不确定性；而临床贝叶斯网络虽能提供可解释的概率信念跟踪，但因依赖专家构建结构、依赖和参数而难以获得。

Result: 在DDXPlus和MIMIC-IV Notes数据集上，ProbMedTOD优于仅用大语言模型和基于检索的基线，尤其对小模型提升显著。

Insight: 创新点在于自动从少量临床笔记合成临床贝叶斯网络的三阶段方法，以及将贝叶斯网络与小语言模型集成，实现多轮对话中的概率推理和可审计的信念跟踪；客观看，结合符号概率模型与神经语言模型来弥补小模型推理能力不足是值得借鉴的思路。

Abstract: Patient history taking is a strategic, multi-turn process that refines diagnostic hypotheses through iterative questioning, mirroring clinical reasoning where each inquiry updates beliefs over candidate diagnoses. Deploying AI in this setting is constrained by privacy, cost, and latency, making Small Language Models (SLMs) a more desirable backbone compared to larger LLMs. However, SLMs capture only the fast, intuitive “System 1” side of this process and lack explicit mechanisms for the deliberative “System 2” reasoning needed to maintain and update diagnostic uncertainty over time. Bayesian Networks (BayesNets) offer a natural complement, providing an interpretable framework for auditable probabilistic belief tracking. Yet, clinical BayesNets are difficult to construct due to their reliance on expert curated structure, dependencies, and parameters. We introduce ProbMedTOD, which automatically synthesizes a clinical BayesNet from a small number of clinical notes in three stages: ontology building, structure prediction, and parameter estimation. ProbMedTOD then integrates the resulting BayesNet with SLMs, enabling probabilistic reasoning in multi-turn dialogues. A BayesNet Agent maintains real-time posterior distributions over diagnoses, while a Policy Agent picks diagnostically informative questions. Experiments on DDXPlus and MIMIC-IV Notes show that ProbMedTOD outperforms LLM-only and retrieval-based baselines, with especially strong gains for smaller models.

[8] Late Entry in Looped Language Models cs.CLPDF

Ziqi Jin, Jiaxi Li, Weiwen Xu, Aixin Sun

TL;DR: 论文研究循环语言模型（looped LM）中的“晚进入”解码策略：新token跳过前若干次迭代，直接基于已缓存的深层上下文执行最后K次pass，并通过轻量微调消除进入边界处的隐藏状态范数不匹配；进一步提出Wake验证，将候选块验证、已提交token的完整T-pass执行和输出GEMM中的状态投影统一到单个静态CUDA图，实现从贪婪精确到有损非回滚的多种解码模式。

Details

Motivation: 传统早退出解码在初始pass后停止且未完成缓存，导致历史token的KV缓存只保留浅层深度；作者希望利用深层缓存上下文替代被跳过的token局部迭代，降低循环模型深度带来的串行解码延迟。

Result: 在Ouro-2.6B（T=4,K=2）上，tau=0时Wake相对贪婪精确解码获得5.2倍加速，GSM8K上比K=4块解码快26%；tau=inf时获6.8倍加速，并在同速下比训练过的早退出高16.9分。Huginn-3.5B上T扩展到64时，tau=0加速达8.4倍，tau=inf达10.6-12.5倍。

Insight: 创新点在于提出与早退出相反的晚进入策略，并用深层缓存上下文补偿跳过的迭代；同时设计Wake验证把精确/容差门控/有损非回滚验证统一进静态CUDA图，在关键路径上只验证最后K个pass，同时并行完成已提交token的剩余pass。客观看，其价值在于把循环深度转化为可调的解码时延-质量折中，并给出可落地的系统实现。

Abstract: Looped language models deepen reasoning at a compact parameter count by iterating a shared layer stack T times per token, turning recurrence depth directly into sequential decoding latency. Conventional early-exit decoding halts after initial passes without cache completion, truncating cached key-value states of past tokens to shallow depth. We study the opposite strategy, late entry, where new tokens skip initial passes and enter directly into the final K < T passes over a deep context cache. Once lightweight fine-tuning resolves hidden-state norm mismatch at the entry boundary, deep cached context substitutes for skipped token-local passes, allowing late entry to outperform matched early exit across model families and pass budgets. To turn this asymmetry into wall-clock speedups without stalling to build deep caches, candidate blocks verify through only the final K passes on the critical path while previously committed tokens complete passes 1 -> T alongside subsequent steps at zero extra serial calls. Projecting these finished T-pass wake states inside the existing output GEMM (Wake verification, governed by logit-margin tolerance tau in [0, inf]) unifies greedy-exact T-pass decoding (tau = 0, matching K = T block decoding within bf16 reduction order), margin-gated verification (tau > 0), and non-rollback lossy late entry (tau = inf) inside a single static CUDA graph. On Ouro-2.6B (T = 4), K = 2 Wake reaches 5.2x greedy-exact speedup at tau = 0 (+26% over K = 4 block decoding on GSM8K) and 6.8x at tau = inf (+16.9 points over trained early exit at matched speed). Scaling test-time recurrence across T in {8, 16, 32, 64} on Huginn-3.5B cuts K = T/4 rollbacks 21.5x with depth, driving greedy-exact (tau = 0) speedups to 6.1x (T = 32) and 8.4x (T = 64, +153% over K = 64 block) and tau = inf speedups to 10.6 to 12.5x.

[9] Large Language Models or AI Agents for English to Arabic Translation? An assessment on WikiEn-Ar190 cs.CLPDF

Fatima Zahra Iguenfer, Yousra Chtouki

TL;DR: 论文研究英译阿（低资源语言）任务中，独立大语言模型与带自评估/改进的自主AI Agent流程哪种表现更好，并构建了WikiEn-Ar190数据集（190篇、18领域、双母语者校验）。实验以GPT-5-mini为对象，比较单独翻译与“翻译+自评估+改进”流程，发现后者在保持语义质量的同时提升词汇准确性。

Details

Motivation: 阿拉伯语等低资源语言的机器翻译质量仍有限，作者希望评估自主推理/AI Agent流程能否优于单独LLM翻译，并为英阿翻译提供更可靠的评测数据与改进框架。

Result: 单独GPT-5-mini翻译流畅，COMET达0.8649，人工充分性、流畅性和风格平均4.53/5；加入自评估与改进后，词汇准确性提升28.76%（BLEU 22.83），语义质量基本保持。

Insight: 创新点在于构建领域多样且经母语者校验的WikiEn-Ar190评测集，并验证“翻译-自评估-改进”的Agent式流程能提升低资源语言翻译一致性；客观上提供了可复用的低资源翻译评测与改进框架。

Abstract: This research investigates whether a large language model or an autonomous AI Agent performs better at translating from English to Arabic, which is considered a low-resource language. The authors introduce WikiEn-Ar190, a new dataset of 190 carefully selected Wikipedia articles from 18 different areas, which were created and verified by two native Arabic speakers to ensure that they accurately reflect both meaning and style. Using this dataset, the study evaluates the translation quality of GPT-5-mini both as an independent neural translator and when used within a more complex process that includes self-assessment and improvement. As a standalone model, GPT-5-mini produced very fluent translations , achieving a COMET score of 0.8649 and receiving high human scores for adequacy, fluency, and style (averaging 4.53 out of 5). When used in a system that combined translation with self-assessment, it improved lexical accuracy by 28.76% (BLEU score of 22.83) while maintaining a similar level of semantic quality. These findings show that methods involving autonomous reasoning can improve translation consistency for language pairs with limited resources. Beyond the results, the approach offers a framework for evaluating and improving translations, potentially using them as assisting tools to expand the representation of low resource languages, such as Arabic, on the web.

[10] Chain-of-Thought Hidden States Reveal Task-Solving Directions for Direct Answers cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Houman Mehrafarin, Ioannis Konstas

TL;DR: 论文通过给CoT隐藏状态标注为“可恢复”或“失败”，发现模型对两类状态有可区分编码，并从直接答案提示的表示空间中为每个问题构建一个引导方向。在生成前将直接提示的最后隐藏状态朝该方向旋转一次，就能以远少于CoT的token获得更高准确率，说明CoT并非达到该准确率的最短路径。

Details

Motivation: CoT虽能提升大模型推理表现，但并非总优于直接回答，且常需生成数百token；论文希望理解CoT内部隐藏状态的作用，并寻找更短、更直接的正确答案引导方式。

Result: 在25个任务上，该方法将LLaMA 3.1 8B直接提示的平均准确率从46%提升到85%（CoT为69%），将Qwen 2.5 7B从52%提升到86%（CoT为77%），且仅使用约十分之一的CoT token。

Insight: 创新点在于用隐藏状态的可恢复性分析CoT，并发现直接提示表示空间中存在可按问题构建的任务解决方向；通过单次隐藏状态旋转实现无需长链推理的性能提升，也为未来训练模型对齐内部状态提供了可解释的控制信号。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting improves the performance of Large Language Models by eliciting intermediate reasoning steps. However, CoT is far from perfect: on some tasks, models perform worse than with direct-answer prompting, and on others they generate a few hundred tokens to reach the correct answer. We start by labelling each CoT hidden state as recovering if patching it into the direct prompt yields the correct answer, and failed otherwise. A linear probe shows that the model encodes these two classes differently. We then show that the representation space of the direct prompt holds a direction for each question that leads the model, in a short generation, to the correct answer more often than CoT does. Specifically, we rotate the direct prompt’s last hidden state once, before generation, towards a direction built for each question from its own recovering and failed states. Across 25 tasks, these directions raise the direct prompt’s mean accuracy from $46%$ to $85%$ on LLaMA 3.1 8B (CoT: $69%$) and from $52%$ to $86%$ on Qwen 2.5 7B (CoT: $77%$), with a tenth of CoT’s tokens. CoT is thus not the shortest route to this accuracy, and these directions can be used in future work to train models to align their states with them.

[11] Piccolo: Co-Evolving Decision Models and Data through Recursive Self-Improvement cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.CVPDF

Zhenqing Ling, Qirui Jiao, Daoyuan Chen, Cheng Chen, Xinji Mai

TL;DR: 论文提出 Piccolo 系列多模态决策模型（0.8B 到 27B），通过智能体数据工程与递归自我改进（RSI）让模型能力与训练数据协同演化。模型对文本与视觉判断、智能体框架、数据工作流和交互游戏中的候选答案预测概率，并在多个评测上取得领先成绩。

Details

Motivation: 关注通用决策模型如何通过数据驱动的方式持续自我提升，解决模型能力与训练数据难以协同进化、数据选择与反馈 refinement 成本高的问题。

Result: Piccolo-27B 以 75.59 分登顶 Decision Index Vision Full 排行榜，Piccolo-9B 和 4B 分别达到 68.35 和 67.46；三者在 Private benchmark 各自模型规模类别中均排名第一。

Insight: 创新点在于用研究智能体自动分析模型行为、构造数据、做实验并反馈优化，形成模型与数据递归共演化；语义切片和共享研究记忆支持跨模型族协作。论文还给出反直觉发现：骨干网络与训练配方协同适配比模型新旧更重要，定向数据策展可让小模型媲美大模型，连续 loss 下降可能偏离甚至损害离散决策准确率。

Abstract: We present Piccolo, a family of multimodal decision models (0.8B to 27B) that coevolves model capabilities and training data via Agentic Data Engineering and recursive self-improvement (RSI). The models predict probabilities over candidate answers for general-purpose text and visual judgments, agent harnesses, data workflows, and interactive games. Piccolo-27B tops the Decision Index Vision Full leaderboard with a score of 75.59, while Piccolo-9B and Piccolo-4B achieve 68.35 and 67.46, respectively. All three rank first on the Private benchmark in their respective model-size categories. Research agents analyze model behavior, construct training data, conduct experiments, and use the results to refine subsequent proposals. Semantic slices and a shared research memory connect these actions across the model family’s development. With training recipes comprising hundreds of thousands of examples, data selection and feedback-driven refinement are central to the self-improvement process. Our large-scale RSI exploration yields three counter-intuitive insights for decision-model development: (1) Backbone-recipe co-adaptation outweighs raw model recency: earlier backbones can surpass newer generations on visual tasks under identical recipes; (2) Targeted data curation enables 4B-9B models to rival 27B baselines on specialized workflows; and (3) Continuous loss reduction can decouple from and even degrade discrete decision accuracy. Together with the released weights and tools from the Data-Juicer and AgentScope ecosystems, Piccolo provides an actionable blueprint for building frontier decision models and scaling data-driven RSI.

[12] CurvDelta: Low-Curvature Delta Modeling For Surgical Learning and Unlearning cs.CLPDF

Zeguan Xiao, Zhuocheng Liang, Yun Chen, Yong Wang, Guanhua Chen

TL;DR: 论文提出 CurvDelta 框架，将通用能力保持约束嵌入 LLM 参数更新的参数化中，通过围绕适配前模型分析保持损失的局部几何，构造低曲率更新空间，使参数更新几乎不改变保持损失。为进一步提升效率，作者提出低秩变体 CurvLoRA，并统一用于能力学习与选择性遗忘任务。

Details

Motivation: 大语言模型在适配到目标任务时，往往会出现目标能力提升但通用能力退化的问题，核心挑战是在适配过程中保持原有能力。论文希望把能力保持约束直接融入参数更新建模，而不是仅在训练目标中做正则化。

Result: 论文在数学推理、代码生成和知识遗忘任务上评估方法。数学推理和代码生成中，方法在下游性能有竞争力的同时更好地保持通用能力，取得更优的获取-保持权衡；在 TOFU 和 MUSE 的知识遗忘任务上，相比基线更好地平衡定向遗忘与知识保持，保持性能接近从头重训练参考。

Insight: 创新点在于从保持约束的局部几何出发构造低曲率参数更新空间，使更新对保持损失近似不变，并用 K-FAC 与因子化投影使其可扩展到 LLM；同时提出同一保持几何下的低秩变体 CurvLoRA。客观看，其可借鉴之处是将“能力保持”从损失项转化为参数更新空间的几何约束，并统一支持学习与遗忘两类适配任务。

Abstract: A central challenge in adapting large language models (LLMs) is capability preservation: an adaptation can achieve its target while degrading general capabilities. We introduce CurvDelta, a scalable framework that incorporates the preservation constraint into the parameterization of the model update. By analyzing the local geometry of the preservation constraint around the pre-adaptation model, CurvDelta constructs a low-curvature space in which parameter updates leave the preservation loss nearly invariant. We make its construction practical for LLMs through Kronecker-factored approximate curvature (K-FAC) and a factorized projection. We also introduce CurvLoRA, a low-rank variant within the same preservation geometry, designed for more efficient adaptation. Both methods apply to preservation-constrained adaptation tasks such as capability learning and selective unlearning. We evaluate our methods on mathematical reasoning, code generation, and knowledge unlearning. For mathematical reasoning and code generation, our approaches achieve competitive downstream performance while better preserving general capabilities, offering a favorable acquisition–retention trade-off relative to the baselines. For knowledge unlearning, our main results on TOFU and MUSE show a better balance between targeted forgetting and knowledge retention than the baselines, with retention performance close to the from-scratch retraining reference.

[13] OCLDA-GS: Object-Centric Local Deformation-Aware Gaussian Streaming cs.CVPDF

Ashim Dahal, Nikesh Subedi, Rabab Abdelfattah, Nick Rahimi

TL;DR: 论文提出 OCLDA-GS，一个以对象为中心的流式 3D Gaussian Splatting 框架，用于多视角视频的动态场景在线重建与自由视角视频。方法动态重分配对象和运动感知的高斯锚点，并利用对象级刚体变换与逐锚点局部形变校正来提升大运动对象和小形变区域的建模质量。实验表明其在多个动态多视角视频数据集上优于现有基线，并提升了细化时间步之间的稳定性。

Details

Motivation: 现有高效流式 3DGS 方法通常选择锚点高斯的子集并进行邻域插值，容易忽略对象特定运动，并在对象边界附近产生不一致。论文希望解决动态流式重建中对象运动建模不足和对象边界不精确的问题。

Result: 在 N3DV、Meeting Room 和 Google Immersive 三个动态多视角视频数据集上，匹配细化预算时相较 Instant Gaussian Stream (IGS)，PSNR 分别提升 0.169 dB、0.202 dB 和 0.199 dB；同时将细化时间步之间的平均 PSNR 下降在 N3DV 和 Meeting Room 上分别减少 9.2% 和 13.8%。

Insight: 创新点在于以对象为中心动态重分配对象与运动感知的高斯锚点，将高斯邻域限制在对象组内以改善边界，并融合对象级刚体变换估计与逐锚点局部形变校正。客观来看，其可借鉴之处是将对象级运动先验、局部形变补偿和流式锚点资源分配结合，以兼顾大运动建模、细节恢复和流式重建稳定性。

Abstract: Streaming 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) allows for online reconstruction of dynamic scenes from multi-view videos for Free Viewpoint Video (FVV). Current time and storage efficient methods rely on selecting a subset of anchor Gaussians and neighborhood interpolation for fast streamable 3DGS. However, these methods can overlook object-specific motion and introduce inconsistencies near object boundaries. We propose OCLDA-GS, an object-centric streaming framework that dynamically reallocates object and motion-aware Gaussian anchors to allocate anchor Gaussian support for objects undergoing large motion while at the same time, restricting the gaussian neighborhood within object groups for more precise object boundaries. Furthermore, it estimates object-wise rigid transformations (e.g. a hand moving) and leverages these estimates to learn per-anchor local deformation correction for small deformations (e.g. wrinkles on a shirt). Then, we conduct experiments on three dynamic multi-view video datasets and demonstrate improvements over the previous baselines alongside improved quality between refinement timesteps. Under matched refinement budgets, our method improves PSNR over Instant Gaussian Stream (IGS) by 0.169 dB on N3DV, 0.202 dB on Meeting Room and +0.199 on Google Immersive, while reducing the mean PSNR drop between refinement timesteps by 9.2% and 13.8% in N3DV and Meeting Room, respectively.

[14] Autoregressive View Model cs.CVPDF

Jensen, Zhou, Samuel Rota Bulo, Denis Rozumny, Duncan Zauss

TL;DR: 论文提出 Autoregressive View Model（AVM），不构建显式3D表示，而是将场景生成建模为沿指定相机路径输出视图流。AVM 通过统一 in-context 接口接收图像或视频条件，并检索输入与生成历史中的相关视图形成有界主动上下文。方法先用 flow matching 将预训练双向视频扩散 Transformer 微调为 N-in-1-out 形式，再在自身 rollout 上调优因果模型，以提升真实感、减少去噪步数并抑制长时漂移。

Details

Motivation: 论文旨在避免显式3D重建，统一处理图像/视频输入与沿相机路径的新视图生成任务，并解决外推到未见区域及长时场景一致性问题。

Result: 论文在相机控制、文本控制、视觉质量、动态、稀疏与4D重建、长时一致性等多个 benchmark 上评估生成能力和场景持久性；摘要未给出具体数值或SOTA结论，仅声称 AVM 能通过一个 in-context 接口适应多样输入输出设置。

Insight: 创新点在于将场景生成转化为自回归视图流，并用有界主动上下文检索融合重建、插值与外推；同时通过 N-in-1-out flow matching 微调和因果模型自 rollout 调优，兼顾多任务统一、少步去噪与长时漂移控制。客观看，其可借鉴之处是无显式3D表示下用上下文检索和自回归生成维持场景持久性。

Abstract: We present the Autoregressive View Model (AVM), which generates a scene as a stream of views without constructing an explicit 3D representation. Through a unified in-context interface, AVM conditions on images or videos to generate short clips along a requested camera path. Visual context grounds reconstruction and interpolation, while a learned prior enables extrapolation into unseen regions. AVM retrieves relevant views from inputs and generated history to form a bounded active context. We fine-tune a pretrained bidirectional video diffusion transformer with flow matching in an N-in-1-out formulation. We then tune a causal model on its own rollouts to improve photorealism, reduce denoising steps, and limit long-horizon drift. We scale training to approximately 8M scenes and video clips. We evaluate generation capacity and scene persistence across benchmarks for camera control, text control, visual quality, dynamics, sparse and 4D reconstruction, and long-horizon consistency. The results demonstrate that AVM adapts to diverse input and output settings through one in-context interface.

[15] Bits, Not Boxes: Label-Free Layer Selection for Visual Localization in VLMs cs.CV | stat.MEPDF

Haisheng Fu, Gexin Huang, Haikai Zhao, Jie Liang, Zhenman Fang

TL;DR: 论文提出 WhereBits，一种面向视觉语言模型视觉定位的无标注层选择方法。它利用问题与通用提示对比后的注意力差异，将“看哪里”的信息量以比特度量，在无标注问题上识别适合定位的层，并结合逐头对比分数形成层先验。方法无需框标注，只需在新模型或分辨率上使用无标注问题即可迁移。

Details

Motivation: 注意力图虽能低成本反映 VLM 的关注位置，但 attention sink 使其定位不可靠；对比通用提示可抑制 sink，不过只有部分层能定位目标，现有找层方法依赖框标注或固定规则。论文希望仅凭注意力本身实现无标签、可泛化的层选择。

Result: 在 8 个 VLM 上，WhereBits 在 COCO 和 RefCOCO 上匹配框调优层的指向准确率，并更好地排序目标 token；COCO 指向准确率为 0.72，平均注意力仅 0.22。无训练 zoom-in 中，其裁剪在强制选择评测下将 V* 准确率提升 6.0 点，优势在留出模型家族和高分辨率下仍成立。

Insight: 创新点在于将问题对注意力头的影响拆分为“关注图像多少”和“关注图像何处”，并用无标注问题上的 where-to-look 比特量作为层先验，再与逐头对比分数结合。客观看，其可借鉴之处是无需框标注即可选出定位层，且设计常数只需在 COCO 上固定一次，迁移到新模型或分辨率仅需无标注问题。

Abstract: Attention maps cheaply show where a vision-language model (VLM) looks, but attention sinks make them unreliable for localization. Contrasting the question with a generic prompt suppresses sinks, yet only some layers localize the queried object. Existing methods find these layers with box annotations or fixed rules. We show that attention itself can identify them. A question changes how much a head attends to the image and where this attention goes. The two effects separate exactly, and the second, measured in bits on unlabeled questions, indicates which layers localize. WhereBits turns these where-to-look bits into a layer prior and combines it with per-head contrastive scores. Its three design constants were fixed once on labeled COCO data, and a new model or resolution needs only unlabeled questions. Across eight VLMs, WhereBits matches the pointing accuracy of box-tuned layers on COCO and RefCOCO and ranks target tokens better. This advantage holds on a held-out model family and at higher resolutions. Its COCO pointing accuracy is 0.72, against 0.22 for mean attention. In training-free zoom-in, its crops raise V* accuracy by 6.0 points under forced-choice evaluation.

[16] TransFIRA+: Transfer Learning for Biometric Recognizability Assessment cs.CVPDF

Allen Tu, Kartik Narayan, Joshua Gleason, Jennifer Xu, Matthew Meyn

TL;DR: 论文提出TransFIRA及TransFIRA+，用于评估生物特征图像对已部署编码器的可识别性。TransFIRA从预训练编码器的嵌入空间导出类中心相似度CCS和类中心角分离度CCAS，并学习从图像预测这些指标而无需质量标注；TransFIRA+将其扩展到跨相机多模态行人识别，并通过后验sigmoid校准处理身体可识别性分数饱和问题。

Details

Motivation: 监控、视频和网络图像中的人脸/行人识别受姿态、模糊、光照、遮挡和距离影响，现有视觉质量指标或质量评估方法难以预测输入对具体部署编码器是否可识别，且往往依赖启发式、人工标注或昂贵生成流程。

Result: 在BRIAR人脸验证上，使用预测CCS进行模板加权并以CCAS>0过滤，将10^-6 FMR下的TAR从均匀平均的0.085提升到0.598，达到SOTA；TransFIRA+经sigmoid校准后，在两个未见多相机数据集上提升人脸与身体融合识别，优于检测器置信度加权和未校准CCS-rank加权。

Insight: 创新点在于将可识别性直接建立在编码器嵌入空间的决策几何上，以CCS和CCAS作为无需质量标注的学习目标，并提供自然过滤阈值；TransFIRA+进一步用后验sigmoid校准实现跨模态、跨相机场景的免重训练迁移，展示了编码器 grounding 的可识别性评估具有泛化性。

Abstract: Biometric recognition in surveillance, video, and web imagery must contend with extreme variation in pose, blur, illumination, occlusion, and distance, where visual quality metrics fail to predict whether inputs are recognizable to the deployed encoder. Existing face image quality assessment methods rely on visual heuristics, curated annotations, or costly generative pipelines, leaving their predictions detached from the encoder’s decision geometry. We introduce TransFIRA, a lightweight framework that grounds recognizability directly in embedding space. TransFIRA derives class-center similarity (CCS) and class-center angular separation (CCAS) from a pretrained encoder’s embeddings and learns to predict them from images without quality annotations. Using predicted CCS for template weighting and CCAS>0 as a natural filtering threshold yields state-of-the-art face verification performance on BRIAR, raising TAR at $10^{-6}$ FMR from 0.085 with uniform averaging to 0.598. The predicted scores also reveal how image degradations affect encoder-specific recognizability. TransFIRA+ extends this framework to cross-camera multimodal person identification in untrimmed surveillance video. In this setting, body recognizability scores saturate within a narrow range, making direct weighting effectively uniform. We introduce a post-hoc sigmoid calibration that transforms these saturated scores into informative weights without retraining. After calibration, the BRIAR-trained TransFIRA predictor improves fused face and body identification on two unseen multi-camera datasets, outperforming detector-confidence weighting and uncalibrated CCS-rank weighting. Together, TransFIRA and TransFIRA+ demonstrate that encoder-grounded recognizability generalizes across biometric modalities and recognition settings.

[17] Where Global Context and Weight Sharing Work in Compact Vision Backbones cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.LGPDF

Michael Schroeder

TL;DR: 论文以EfficientNet-B0为受控实验对象，在ImageNet-1k上研究紧凑视觉骨干中全局上下文与权重共享应放在哪里。结果表明，在MBConv主干P3/P4/P5阶段插入轻量零门控残差自注意力适配器可稳定提升识别精度，而重复MBConv块采用交替双模板权重共享可在显著减少参数的同时较小精度损失。

Details

Motivation: 紧凑卷积骨干虽参数高效，但混合设计中全局自注意力在哪些阶段最有效、重复块能否共享权重而不损失表征能力仍不明确。

Result: 插入P3窗口化、P4/P5全局自注意力后达到74.02% standalone，并在匹配控制实验中比plain-B0高0.954个百分点；Stage 6交替共享两个块模板减少10.9%参数（582K），单epoch恢复至70.92%，仅比未共享控制低1.84个百分点。

Insight: 创新点在于系统定位全局自注意力的有效阶段，并用零门控残差适配器实现深度递增的注意力幅度；权重共享方面，交替双模板配合每pass独立BatchNorm比朴素核平均或单模板绑定更有效。

Abstract: Compact convolutional backbones such as EfficientNet achieve strong parameter efficiency through local depthwise separable convolutions, yet two structural questions remain central to hybrid design: where does global self-attention improve recognition over pure convolution, and can repeated blocks share weights without losing representational capacity? We investigate both questions on ImageNet-1k (50,000-image validation) using controlled EfficientNet-B0 experiments. First, we show that wrapping a classification backbone in multi-scale feature pyramids (BiFPN) fails to outperform a plain MBConv trunk (69.99% - 71.81% vs. 72.86% plain B0) until paired with stage attention (73.15%). In contrast, keeping the MBConv trunk intact and inserting lightweight zero-gated residual self-attention adapters at stages P3 (28x28 windowed), P4 (14x14 global), and P5 (7x7 global) reaches 74.02% standalone and outperforms a bit-for-bit matched plain-B0 control by +0.954 percentage points (73.88% vs. 72.92%) across an 88-epoch stateless replay schedule, with learned attention amplitude rising monotonically with semantic depth (27.2% at P3 -> 45.4% at P4 -> 66.3% at P5). Second, we evaluate recurrent weight sharing across repeated MBConv blocks with pass-specific BatchNorm statistics (+1,536 parameters per pass). While post-hoc kernel averaging degrades accuracy to 17.03% and single-template stage tying (-27.6% parameters) plateaus at 66.10%, alternating two shared block templates ([B1, A, B, A]) in Stage 6 cuts total model parameters by 10.9% (582K weights) and recovers by +10.82 points in a single epoch to 70.92% (-1.84 points vs. unshared control).

Abstract: Training 3D vision-language models (VLMs) on large medical imaging datasets collected across institutions is constrained by data quality. Vast, uncurated pools of image-report pairs are characterized by redundancy, class imbalance, and cross-institutional heterogeneity. We address this challenge of data curation for improving 3D VLM pretraining by proposing an offline, training-free, reference-guided data curation recipe. Our approach leverages a small, high-quality reference set to independently extract quality signals for each modality of a large, uncurated pool and merges them through a rank-aggregation step into a single ranking that guides sample selection. We validate this method on datasets comprising 35k 3D abdominal CT volume-report pairs from two institutions and 58k 2D chest X-ray image-report pairs. On 3D CT, using our reference-guided data curation strategy, our curated model matches the zero-shot disease classification performance of a state-of-the-art baseline (Merlin) while using $3.3\times$ less training data and a reference set of 5k samples. On 2D chest X-rays, our method yields a curated model that matches or exceeds the zero-shot disease classification performance of a model trained on the full pool while using up to $10\times$ less training data and a reference set of only 74 samples. Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) encode images with 2D encoders that capture little 3D structure. A common approach is to feed the policy estimated depth, but a UAV chooses its own altitude, must judge distances tens of meters away, where monocular depth is less reliable, and must relate its forward and downward views. On long aerial routes, adding depth features directly brings no significant gain (42.5% vs. 42.6% success), so we study how 3D information should be given to the policy. Analyzing flight failures, we find that two forms of 3D grounding fail in opposite ways: explicit 3D lifting of depth collides less on the way but more often fails to finish at the target, while implicit alignment with a 3D geometry model finishes more often but collides more. We propose GRID, which combines both: (1) it lifts monocular depth into ray-corrected 3D positions in the UAV’s frame for both cameras, and (2) during training only, it aligns its visual features with a frozen multi-view geometry model, at no inference cost. GRID adding explicit lifting to implicit alignment significantly increases how often the policy reaches the target ($61.5% \rightarrow 69.5%$, $p<0.001$), and adding implicit alignment to explicit lifting significantly increases how often it completes the task once there ($57.7% \rightarrow 61.4%$, $p<0.05$). On TravelUAV (3,005 test episodes), GRID achieves the best success rate on every split, with relative gains over the state of the art of 9.5% (seen), 17.1% (unseen objects) and 25.4% (unseen map).

[20] Beyond the Sink Token: Sink-Guided Key Editing in Vision-Language Models cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Motivation: 现有工作多解释 sink token 为何吸引注意力，本文关注能否利用 sink-key 方向作为参照来调控对图像和问题 token 的注意力，从而改善 VLM 的推理与生成表现。

Abstract: In vision-language models (VLMs), a few sink tokens attract a disproportionate share of attention. While previous studies have examined why these tokens attract attention, we investigate how the sink-key direction affects attention to image and question tokens. Across four probed VLMs, boosting the magnitude of positive sink-aligned components increases attention on average, whereas boosting negative components decreases it; these responses persist through later layers. This identifies the native sink key as a reference for controlling attention beyond its own position. Motivated by this finding, we propose SHRINK, a training-free method that partially reduces sink-aligned components across visible keys during generation. On nine benchmarks under sampled reasoning, it improves mean accuracy by 5.1 and 2.7 percentage points on LLaVA-OneVision-7B and Qwen2.5-VL-3B, with smaller gains on the other two main models, Qwen2.5-VL-7B and Gemma-3-4B. Our benchmark assesses 12 vision encoder checkpoints and 7 generative VLMs using matched patient comparisons, organ erasure, lesion replacement, and laterality perturbations, revealing substantial alignment failures even among top performing models. To learn organ level image text correspondence, ORGANIC3D aligns each finding sentence with the anatomy aware image features and trains on edited scans to anchor this alignment in the organ’s image evidence. [22] Layer-Matched Sparse Attention for Low-Redundancy 3D Reconstruction cs.CVPDF

Sammam Mahdi, Fariha Binta Salim, K. M. Jubair Sami, Rakin Bin Rabbani

TL;DR: 论文针对VGGT这类单次前馈式三维重建模型，其全局注意力随视角数二次增长的问题，指出现有的免训练稀疏注意力加速器在低冗余（视角间几何重叠少）场景下会损失位姿精度。作者发现注意力层可分为两类：扩散层（聚合分布式上下文）和匹配层（依赖查询特定的交互、注意力质量很小），并提出用逐层统计量（logit方差作为均值token遗漏log质量的免行代理）为每层选择估计器：扩散层使用共享的重要性采样hub键并配合对数权重使softmax分子分母无偏，匹配层则保持精确稠密计算。由此得到LMSA方法。

Details

Motivation: VGGT的全局注意力随视角数量二次增长，现有免训练稀疏化加速器在冗余视频上有效，但在视角间几何共享很少的低冗余场景中位姿精度显著下降；作者将损失归因于统一的稀疏近似无法同时保留扩散层与匹配层这两类跨视角信息。

Result: 在13个未见过的32视角低冗余场景上，LMSA与稠密注意力的AUC@5差距为1.4，而AVGGT为10.3、SAF3R为4.5，与同期GPA-VGGT差异不显著（仅对AVGGT的增益通过两项场景级检验）；100视角下差距1.9 AUC@5且聚合器运行快2.0倍；作为后处理方法为GPA-VGGT选择保护层，以多5% FLOPs换取1.2 AUC@5提升；未经调参迁移到π³上优于AVGGT但距稠密精度仍有差距。

Insight: 创新点在于揭示了VGGT注意力层的功能异质性（扩散层 vs 匹配层），并提出用逐层logit方差这一无需按行计算的代理统计量来自动选择稀疏估计器；同时为扩散层设计了对数加权的无偏softmax估计（分子分母均无偏），并实证发现匹配层任何激进稀疏化都会损失精度。可借鉴之处是“按层自适应选择稀疏策略”以及该统计量可作为通用后处理层选择器。

Abstract: VGGT reconstructs cameras and geometry from unposed images in one pass, but its global attention grows quadratically with the number of views. Training-free accelerators thin this attention and work well on redundant video, but lose pose accuracy when views share little geometry. This paper traces these losses to two kinds of cross-view information that a uniform sparse approximation can fail to preserve together: diffuse layers aggregate distributed context, whereas matching layers depend on query-specific interactions that carry little attention mass. A per-layer statistic measures the log-mass missed by a mean token. Its row-free proxy is the logit variance, and it selects the estimator for each layer. Diffuse layers use shared, importance-sampled hub keys, with log-weights that make the softmax numerator and denominator unbiased. Matching layers stay exact: every aggressive thinning tested cost accuracy. At equal cost, the selected layers outperform the four shifted windows tested. On 13 unseen low-redundancy scenes with 32 views, LMSA stays within 1.4 AUC@5 of dense attention, compared with 10.3 for AVGGT and 4.5 for SAF3R; its difference from contemporaneous GPA-VGGT is not significant, and only its gains over AVGGT pass both scene-level tests. With 100 views, it stays within 1.9 AUC@5 while its aggregator runs $2.0\times$ faster. Used post hoc to choose the layers GPA-VGGT protects, the statistic improves it by 1.2 AUC@5 for 5% more FLOPs. Details

Motivation: 现有转导和测试时适配方法在批次中仅有少量类别或数据流相关时，可能低于零样本基线。作者将其归因于各类独立估计参数，导致缺席或稀有类停留在有偏文本锚点或被外来样本错误拟合。

Result: 在11个基准和4个ImageNet shift上，采用现实批次与在线协议，GRAFT在所有设置中均优于零样本模型，并在少类场景下优于基于锚点的转导方法。摘要未明确宣称总体SOTA。

Abstract: Unlabeled test data can improve the zero-shot classifier of a vision-language model, yet transductive and test-time adaptation methods often fall below the zero-shot baseline when only a few vocabulary classes are present in a batch or when streams are correlated. We trace this failure to a shared design: every class owns independent parameters estimated from its own samples, so absent or rare classes are left at biased text anchors or fitted to foreign samples. We propose Graph-Regularized Anchor-displacement Field Transduction (GRAFT), which models the adapted prototypes as text anchors plus a displacement field that is partially pooled over a semantic class graph through a Gaussian Markov random field prior, together with an explicit class-presence model. Inference is closed-form and gradient-free on cached features. Anchor-only shrinkage and a single global shift are the two extreme settings of the prior; absent classes are provably harmonic extensions of the present ones, and the pooling strength can be chosen by empirical Bayes. Under the realistic batch and online protocols on eleven benchmarks and four ImageNet shifts, GRAFT improves over the zero-shot model in every setting and over anchor-based transduction where few classes are present.

[24] Weight Space Programming for Vision Language Model Repair cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Avinash Goutham Aluguvelly

TL;DR: 论文研究无梯度权重空间编程能否修复视觉语言模型，通过在 SmolVLM-256M 和 LFM2-VL-450M 权重中植入故障，让编码智能体直接编辑权重并与原始权重对照。结果显示智能体能发现投影器缩放行和视觉编码器噪声并恢复精度，但修复依赖故障计算留下的低位痕迹；舍入这些低位后修复能力显著下降，而统计线索仍可引导定位。论文据此建议权重修复研究报告数值格式并加入舍入控制。

Details

Motivation: 论文关注权重空间编程中直接无梯度修改网络权重进行修复的可靠性：成功的修复究竟依赖什么信号，是否受数值精度和故障计算痕迹影响，并希望建立更严谨的评测规范。

Result: 在 SmolVLM-256M 和 LFM2-VL-450M 上，智能体可推断缩放因子、恢复大部分或全部被改权重并使准确率接近原始水平，且生成的程序可修复同模型未见检查点。但将低位舍入后，智能体无法精确恢复权重：SmolVLM 上找不到损伤，LFM2-VL 上仅通过重缩放答案 token 嵌入带来有限收益；交换行列在预算内未被发现。

Insight: 创新点在于用植入故障与原始权重对照系统分析权重修复依赖的线索，揭示 32 位故障在 16 位权重中残留低位痕迹，以及分布和行范数等统计证据可指导修复。客观看，论文强调了数值格式与舍入控制对权重修复研究的重要性，并指出不改变存储值集合的交换行列故障更难被发现。

Abstract: In weight-space programming, a language model edits a network’s weights directly, without gradients. We ask what a successful repair of this kind relies on. We plant faults in the weights of two vision-language models, SmolVLM-256M and LFM2-VL-450M, and check each edit made by a coding agent against the original weights. Without being told where to look, the agent finds rescaled rows in the projector and noise in the vision encoder of both models, infers the unknown scale factors, restores most or all of the rescaled weights to their exact original values, and returns accuracy to near its original level; programs that it writes from such weights repair unseen checkpoints of the same model without feedback. These repairs depend on a trace of how the faults were computed. The original weights have 16-bit precision, and faults computed in 32-bit arithmetic leave low bits that only the damaged weights carry. Rounding those bits away leaves each model’s answers to our test questions unchanged, and the agent, unaided, then restores no weight exactly: on SmolVLM it no longer finds the damage, and in a single session on LFM2-VL its only gain comes from rescaling answer-token embeddings, which changes how often the model answers yes. Marking the damaged weights again in their low bits brings the edits back to them. Rounded weights still hold statistical evidence of the rescaling, in the distribution of stored values and in row norms, and one sentence in the prompt that names such a statistic directs the agent’s edits to the damaged rows on both models. Swapped rows or columns, which leave the set of stored values unchanged, are not found unaided on either model within our session budgets. We recommend that studies of weight repair report the number format of the weights and include a rounded control.

[25] Isthmus: Bridging Supervision Islands Restores Uncertainty and Stability in One-Step Flows cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Luming Yang, Siqing Li, Haoxian Liu, Zhenyu Bu, Jiazheng Zhu

TL;DR: 论文研究一步流映射作为后验采样器时丢失不确定性且不稳定的问题，将其归因于训练时间对在“时间三角”上的监督孤岛现象。作者提出“地峡”方法，将5%的训练对放置在连接一步采样对与对角线的边上，在不改网络、损失和推理代码且无额外成本的情况下恢复一步采样的不确定性与稳定性。

Details

Motivation: 一步流映射本应作为后验采样器保留样本不确定性，但相比同网络多步采样，其样本方差降低、相关性丢失且训练延长后不稳定；直接监督一步跳变甚至会加剧向条件均值塌缩。论文旨在定位并修复这一失败。

Result: 在MRI重建、人脸修补、CIFAR-10和ImageNet 32x32无条件生成上，方法在多种流映射目标下改善一步采样。脑MRI上将一步校准恢复到0.96，与多步样本相关性达到0.93，跨随机种子波动显著更小，训练延长至三倍仍保持一致性。

Insight: 创新点在于诊断出一步采样失败源于时间对监督孤岛：一步采样对未被监督或位于自洽恒等式不连通对角线的孤岛上，目标无法确定并锚定一步映射。提出沿连接一步对与对角线的边放置少量训练对形成“地峡”，用自洽残差界定一步映射误差，且不增加推理成本，具有较强可迁移性。

Abstract: One-step flow maps are increasingly used as posterior samplers, where the point of sampling is the uncertainty that the samples carry. We find that the one-step sampler loses this uncertainty and becomes less stable. Compared with multi-step samples of the same network, its samples lose variance and decorrelate. Supervising the one-step jump directly makes this worse; the samples collapse further toward the conditional mean and break down when training is extended. We trace both failures to the locations where training places its pairs of start and end times. Only pairs on the diagonal of the time triangle receive targets from data, and the pair used for one-step sampling is either left unsupervised or supervised on an island, a set of pairs that the self-consistency identity does not connect to the diagonal, where the objective neither determines the one-step map nor anchors its training. We bridge the island with an isthmus, which places 5% of the training pairs along the whole edge of the triangle that joins the one-step pair to the diagonal. The error of the one-step map is then bounded by the self-consistency residual along this edge. With the network, the loss and the inference code unchanged and at no added cost, the isthmus improves one-step sampling across several flow-map objectives, in MRI reconstruction, face inpainting, and unconditional generation on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet 32x32. On brain MRI, it restores one-step calibration to 0.96 and the correlation with multi-step samples to 0.93; both vary far less across seeds, and the agreement holds when training is extended to three times its length.

Abstract: An anatomical pose provides interpretable joint structure, but no fixed set of physical landmarks can fully specify a person’s movement. We introduce PRISM, a complementary representation that characterizes repetitive movement using tracked masks and dense optical flow. Torso-relative flow measures intensity, frame-wise mask variation measures spatial extent, signed-flow recurrence estimates frequency, and persistent sliding-window geometry describes the rhythmicity of the movement. Across 1,890 synthetic conditions, the measurements recover controlled speed, amplitude, and frequency while exposing observation limits. An idealized hand-motion experiment demonstrates variation omitted by identical exact COCO-17 trajectories; controlled corruption experiments separately examine measurement sensitivity. Audits of two pose methods on the same data cohort and 36,930 external skeleton clips document incomplete observation and dependence on processing choices. In 1,404 annotated complex stereotypical motor movement bouts from six autistic participants, total flow converges with pose motion, while matched-coordinate examples and repeated-session profiles show differences in intensity, extent, frequency, and rhythmicity within behavior labels. A separate VLM comparison evaluates quantitative frequency estimation, and a profile-conditioned decoder links the measurements to illustrative skeletal movements. PRISM connects numerical profiles to visible examples, providing an interpretable way to compare how the same behavior is expressed beyond its label or event count.

Abstract: Degradations in smart-glasses images obscure small text and fine-grained structures needed by vision-language models (VLMs) for visual assistance. We introduce an high-resolution (HR)-referenced semantic evaluation protocol that compares VLM outputs on restored images with those on corresponding HR references. Our evaluation reveals two failure modes: generative SR can synthesize misleading details, whereas restoration-oriented SR can leave critical evidence blurred or ambiguous. These findings motivate improving faithful detail recovery in restoration-oriented SR, where a key training challenge is to provide sufficiently difficult inputs while retaining recoverable visual information. We propose ReDiC (Recoverability-Difficulty Curriculum), which combines a model-independent recoverability proxy with model-relative restoration difficulty to adapt training degradation severity. Abstract: Powder X-ray diffraction (PXRD) is the primary structural evidence for covalent organic frameworks (COFs) and many other materials. Yet decades of measured patterns exist only as images in published figures. Automatic recovery is difficult, because PXRD figures vary widely in format, from a single curve to crowded stacks with labels, insets, and neighboring plots that challenge traditional computer vision. This work presents a pipeline in which a vision large language model (LLM) interprets the layout of each figure, locating the diffraction plot and its curves and sketching a coarse trace of each, while classical computer vision (CV) refines every trace to pixel-level accuracy. Applied to 3,954 COF articles, the pipeline produces 11,445 calibrated diffraction curves, released in the open database COF-PEAK. Compared with a hand digitization of 60 panels, extracted peak positions differ by a median of only 0.07 degrees. Abstract: The rise of the agentic web has been accompanied by growing demand for on-device Vision-Language Models (VLMs) that perform diverse cross-modal web tasks with enhanced privacy and reduced latency. Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are widely adopted in modern VLMs for compute-efficient scalability, yet their multi-expert architecture poses deployment challenges due to substantial memory demands. Mixed-precision quantization is an effective compression technique that reduces bit-widths while adapting to the heterogeneous hardware budgets of edge devices, particularly well-suited for MoE due to its inherent structural sparsity, which causes imbalanced importance among experts. Experimental results show that MoMoVL improves the accuracy-compression trade-off relative to state-of-the-art mixed-precision baselines and reduces memory footprint by up to 15.44% with a modest drop in accuracy over its uniform-precision baseline. Abstract: Selective state-space models (SSMs) have recently shown strong potential for efficient image restoration. Existing deterministic scans follow space-filling principles but remain fixed and content-agnostic, and can yield fragmented or semantically inconsistent contexts under degradations. We propose \textbf{CoScan}, a \textbf{co}ntent-adaptive \textbf{scan}-order design for causal vision SSMs. CoScan learns image-dependent space-filling traversals via differentiable proxy supervision driven by \textbf{scan-quality objectives} that \textit{encourage short-range coherence and causal predictability, while preserving spatial adjacency}. To support hierarchical restoration backbones, we introduce an \textbf{efficient multi-scale scan construction by coarsening a fine-level minimum spanning tree}, producing coherent scans across feature resolutions. Abstract: Accurately matching human intentions in 3D space is an important goal of artificial intelligence. Recently, 3D Intention Grounding (3D-IG) has emerged, aiming to localize target 3D objects given a natural-language intent. Unlike conventional visual grounding with descriptive referring expressions, 3D-IG intents are abstract and non-descriptive, making object localization substantially more challenging. However, existing methods largely rely on implicit intent-object matching, leading to logical gaps and limited interpretability, robustness, and generalization. To address these challenges, we propose Chain-of-Causal Reasoning (CoCR), a causality-inspired functional dependency reasoning framework that explicitly bridges abstract intentions and candidate objects through intermediate functional requirements. Specifically, CoCR progressively decomposes complex intents into ordered functional requirements, thereby forming an explicit intent-function-object reasoning chain that links abstract intentions to object suitability. Building on this chain, we construct a causality-inspired functional dependency graph to model requirement-attribute relationships and introduce a causal-visual alignment module that aligns function-aware representations with the geometric-semantic features of 3D point clouds, enabling bidirectional verification between structured functional reasoning and visual evidence. Extensive experiments on 3D Intention Grounding and 3D Visual Grounding demonstrate that our method enhances intent-aware object localization. Our code is available at https://2490o.github.io/CoCR.

Abstract: Deep networks achieve high realism in texture synthesis but often rely on opaque, over-parameterized representations that have limited direct connection to biological vision. Neuroscience-informed pyramid models are interpretable, compact, and biologically inspired, but rely on small, hand-curated statistics sets that are difficult to generalize or extend. We introduce GramStatTexNet, a hybrid analysis-by-synthesis framework that combines the multi-scale Gabor filter structure of classical models with the flexibility of Gramian-based correlations, organized into structured filter families. This structured representation enables analyses that are challenging for deep-feature methods: each statistic carries a named filter-family identity, allowing us to decompose synthesis fidelity into per-family contributions and to learn an interpretable, low-dimensional embedding that preserves perceptual texture content. We compare our framework to classical and deep texture-synthesis baselines across diverse texture categories, and on multiple perceptual metrics, highlighting the compactness and interpretability advantages of this hybrid representation, alongside competitive synthesis quality. GramStatTexNet provides a unified, interpretable framework for analyzing and modeling visual information with natural extensions across space, time, and eccentricity. [36] Keep the Reasoning, Read the Image: On-Demand Visual KV for Multi-Turn Reasoning VLMs cs.CVPDF

Ziyi Wang, Xiyue Zhang, Bing Hu, Guangmin Song, Chenyi Zhang

TL;DR: 论文研究多轮推理视觉语言模型（VLM）中的KV cache剪枝问题，发现现有保留策略让推理token与图像共享预算，导致图像信息被挤出、多轮对话性能大幅下降。为此提出免训练的ShelfKV方法，将全部视觉KV条目保留在CPU内存中，在每个解码步按当前查询打分，把高分视觉条目换入低分文本条目的注意力槽位，实现“图像不被驱逐、只在被读取时占用显存”。

Details

Motivation: 推理VLM在回答前会生成上千token的推理，KV cache膨胀，现有剪枝保留策略在多轮对话中让图像与推理共享同一预算，不断增长的推理把视觉条目挤出，造成图像信息丢失；实验表明同一图表问题先问时仅掉8分，在一轮推理后再问则掉24分。

Result: 在四个benchmark（其中三个为多轮对话）、两种保留策略、两种推理VLM上评估：同等GPU内存下比最强现有策略DBTrimKV最高提升20.3分，即使DBTrimKV使用两倍内存仍领先8.7分；也稳定优于同等内存的固定视觉预留方案，且不损害单轮问题准确率。

Insight: 核心洞察是“图像缺的是保护而非容量”——为视觉条目设小预留即可挽回大部分损失，而把同样槽位加入共享预算收益有限。创新点在于将视觉KV常驻CPU、按需换入注意力槽位的免训练解耦设计，可叠加在任意现有保留策略上，兼顾推理保留与图像读取。

Abstract: Reasoning vision-language models (VLMs) think for thousands of tokens before they answer, and retention policies keep the KV cache affordable by pruning it as the reasoning grows. We find that this pruning sacrifices the image in multi-turn conversations: the image and the reasoning share one budget, so the growing reasoning crowds the visual entries out. A chart question that loses 8 points to the full cache when asked first loses 24 points when asked after a round of reasoning. Protecting a small visual reserve from eviction recovers most of this loss, while adding the same slots to the shared budget recovers considerably less. The image therefore lacks protection, not capacity. Based on this finding, we propose ShelfKV, a training-free method that works on top of existing retention policies. It keeps every visual entry in CPU memory and, at each decoding step, swaps the visual entries that the current query scores highest into the attention slots of the text entries it scores lowest. The image is never evicted, yet it occupies attention slots only while it is read. We evaluate ShelfKV on four benchmarks, three of them conversational, with two retention policies and two reasoning VLMs. In conversations, it improves the strongest existing policy, DBTrimKV, by up to 20.3 points at matched GPU memory, and still leads by 8.7 points when DBTrimKV uses twice the memory. It also consistently outperforms a fixed visual reserve of matched GPU memory in these settings, without hurting accuracy on standalone questions. These results show that the image should be protected from the reasoning but read only on demand.

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) now underpin the visual encoders of most multimodal models, but this success comes at a cost: relying on third-party pretrained checkpoints leaves these systems open to backdoor attacks. Existing test-time detection methods rely on either the predicted label or intermediate embeddings, limiting their applicability across diverse tasks and incurring substantial computational overhead. In this work, we study how backdoor triggers affect the multi-head attention mechanism of ViTs and identify two distinct anomalous patterns: $\textit{complete attention hijacking}$, causing the attention to exhibit abnormally high inter-head spatial overlap, and $\textit{partial attention hijacking}$, causing an abnormally large variance in the concentration of the attention distribution. Motivated by these findings, we propose $\textbf{DetectViT}$, a test-time backdoor detection method that requires neither training data, model outputs, nor any prior knowledge of the trigger. DetectViT quantifies the two hijacking phenomena via an inter-head consistency score and an inter-head entropy variance score, with thresholds estimated from a small out-of-distribution (OOD) calibration set drawn from an arbitrary source. Across four representative attacks, diverse architectures (DeiT, CLIP, and LLaVA), and downstream tasks (image classification and captioning), DetectViT substantially outperforms all baselines with as few as 64–128 OOD calibration samples, achieving a true positive rate of $100%$ and a false positive rate of $0.17%$ on BadCLIP. Notably, it introduces negligible additional overhead by relying solely on attention weights already computed during inference. Code is available at: https://github.com/siquanhuang/DetectViT.

Abstract: Automated assessment of the critical view of safety (CVS) in laparoscopic cholecystectomy has advanced rapidly, but a deployed check must decide, before the first clip, whether to confirm CVS. The standard rule calibrates a threshold so that false confirmations are rare on average. We show that this rule concentrates its errors on disputed videos, which one of three expert raters judged sufficient, and that the failure is structural. When reference labels are votes of a panel sampled from a larger expert pool, the rule that maximizes sensitivity at a fixed average false-confirmation rate also maximizes false confirmation of disputed videos, and for recognizers that recover expert judgement with less noise this rate rises with accuracy. We propose certified confirmation, a risk-controlled monitoring rule whose threshold is selected by multiple testing so that, with probability at least 1 - δ, false confirmation is at most α overall, among disputed videos, within vendor and country groups, and before CVS is visible, using disputed labels only to calibrate. In a pre-specified, hash-locked evaluation on the 300 test videos (18 countries) of the SAGES CVS Challenge, standard calibration falsely confirmed 8 of 39 disputed videos (0.21; one-sided p = 0.037 against α = 0.10), whereas certified confirmation confirmed 2 (one-sided 95% upper bound 0.15). On development data, protection held for vendor and country groups left out of calibration. The cost was confirming 10% rather than 35% of sufficient videos on time. Average accuracy hides this risk; disputed cases must be protected explicitly.

Abstract: Digital watermarking provides a promising solution for verifying the provenance and integrity of generated content. In this paper, we propose ROBIN++, a paradigm that shifts from single-domain entanglement to a Dual-Domain Synergistic design. Our key insight is to explicitly decouple robustness and fragility during injection by assigning them to distinct domains, while re-coupling them for mutual reinforcement during verification. Specifically, we introduce an Adversarial Optimization Algorithm to inject a frequency-domain copyright watermark during generation, maximizing extraction robustness without visual artifacts. Complementarily, a Content-Aware Perturbation generator is integrated into the latent space to inject the spatial-domain localization watermark, ensuring sensitivity to spatial tampering with minimal interference to the copyright watermark. Extensive experiments show that ROBIN++ significantly outperforms existing approaches in both copyright recovery and tamper localization accuracy, effectively mitigating the long-standing trade-off between the two tasks. Motivation: 驾驶场景在模型计算期间持续变化，因此观测新鲜度和动作交付时间对世界-动作规划至关重要；论文希望在保留丰富生成式世界先验的同时不阻塞实时控制。

Result: 在NAVSIM开环达到94.2 PDMS、每决策198.4 ms；Bench2Drive闭环达到80.30 Driving Score、前台延迟109.9 ms；Waymo E2E隐藏测试为8.0050 Rater Feedback Score；nuScenes平均终点误差0.54 m、平均碰撞率0.03%。在四个基准上均优于对比的世界-动作方法，取得较好的精度-延迟权衡。

Insight: 核心创新是将世界更新移出观测到动作的关键路径，实现慢速世界模型与快速动作模型的异步协同；通过覆盖更新间隔和后台计算跨度的预测训练，以及前台门控KV bank编码当前环视图像，使Action DiT无需等待即可融合实时视觉反馈与最新完整世界上下文。

Abstract: Driving scenes keep changing during model computation, making both observation freshness and action-delivery time central to world-action planning. To retain rich generative world priors in autonomous driving without stalling real-time control, we present SWIFT (Slow World, Instant Freshness, and Timely action), a deployment-oriented asynchronous world-action system that moves world updates off the observation-to-action critical path. On a background GPU, a slow Video DiT streams surround-view history into read-only predictive world context, trained with a predictive span that covers both the update interval and background world computation. On a foreground GPU, a lightweight freshness carrier encodes current surround-view images into a gated key/value bank at every step, letting a fast Action DiT combine live visual feedback with the latest completed world context without waiting. On open-loop NAVSIM, SWIFT reaches 94.2 PDMS at 198.4 ms per decision with a trajectory scorer; on closed-loop Bench2Drive, it achieves an 80.30 Driving Score at 109.9 ms foreground latency. SWIFT further transfers to the Waymo E2E hidden test (8.0050 Rater Feedback Score) and nuScenes (0.54 m mean endpoint error, 0.03% mean collision rate). Across all four benchmarks, SWIFT achieves the best results among compared world-action methods, establishing a favorable accuracy-latency trade-off.

Abstract: Text-conditioned 3D indoor scene generation requires layouts satisfying geometric, functional, and semantic constraints. However, existing diffusion methods rely primarily on static textual conditions, limiting their ability to assess evolving scenes and translate semantic discrepancies into executable object-level corrections. Motivation: 传统鼠标动态认证依赖手工特征或时间序列表示，近期图像化方法虽可利用预训练视觉模型，但多将光标坐标画成轨迹图，仅保留空间信息而丢弃有助于区分用户的时间特征。论文动机是设计能编码速度等时间信息的图像表示，以提升认证性能。

Abstract: Mouse dynamics authentication has traditionally relied on handcrafted features or time-series representations that capture both spatial and temporal characteristics of mouse movements. More recent approaches have modeled mouse data as images, enabling the use of powerful pretrained computer vision models. However, prior work primarily focuses on spatial-only representations where the cursor coordinates are plotted as a trajectory image. These representations discard the temporal characteristics which can help discriminate users. In this work, we propose image representations that incorporate speed and velocity information for mouse dynamics authentication. We investigate two mouse data representations, a XYPlot representation that intuitively shows the path of movement, and a Pairwise Distance (PWD) representation that utilizes the entire image canvas to encode relationships between mouse events. Both representations are evaluated under both location-aware and location-free settings using pretrained 2D-CNN and ViT architectures. We found that the incorporation of temporal information improved performance over spatial representations alone, and that the location-aware variants generally outperformed the location-free. We achieve state-of-the-art performance on two datasets, Balabit and TWOS, and extend our evaluation to two other datasets. We found that in general, location-aware PWD with temporal encoding combined with the 2D-CNN architecture provided the best results, with mean EERs of 5.21% and 12.70% on the Balabit Test-1 and Test-2 sets, respectively, 4.94% on TWOS, 6.16% on ChaoShen, and 0.59% on DFL.

[44] MobileWeave: Program-First Synthesis of Dependency-Linked Tasks for Training Mobile GUI Agents cs.CVPDF

Ziyi He, Xian Wu, Qiyuan Chen, Haitao Li, Cehao Yang

TL;DR: 论文提出 MobileWeave，一种面向移动 GUI 智能体训练的“程序优先”任务合成方法。它将每个任务合成为由可控模拟器应用实现派生的可执行程序，并通过显式依赖组合单应用与跨应用任务，再经原生执行验证，从同一程序生成指令和完成检查。基于教师轨迹和策略停滞处的恢复监督训练的 MobileWeave-8B 在多个移动 GUI benchmark 上显著提升，并表明子目标依赖结构本身可作为学习组合技能的训练信号。

Details

Motivation: 现有自动任务合成管线将任务生成为自由文本，子目标之间的依赖关系是隐式的，导致策略可能单独完成每个子任务却无法串联成完整工作流。论文关注如何大规模合成既覆盖单应用功能又覆盖多目标/跨应用工作流的训练任务，并显式建模任务组合结构。

Result: MobileWeave-8B 在 MobileGym 上较基座模型提升 26.7 点，在 AndroidWorld、AndroidLab、MobileWorld 上分别提升 8.6、21.3、17.6 点，4B 模型也有相近增益。结果表明该方法在多个移动 GUI 智能体 benchmark 上取得显著提升。

Insight: 创新点在于将任务表示为可执行程序，用带共享语义类型的类型化能力图抽象应用，并通过显式值和对象依赖组合原子程序为单应用/跨应用复合任务；同时用原生执行验证程序，并从同一程序派生指令与完成检查。客观来看，其关键创新是把任务结构中的依赖关系作为可验证、可监督的训练信号，从而训练策略组合已掌握的技能，而不仅是学习单个子任务。

Abstract: Native mobile GUI agents require large-scale training trajectories covering both individual app functions and multi-goal workflows within and across apps, which makes automatic task synthesis a promising way to scale training data. Existing synthesis pipelines, however, produce each task as free-form text, leaving implicit how its subgoals depend on one another. This matters because composition is a skill in its own right: a policy may complete each subtask of a workflow on its own, yet fail to chain these subtasks into the complete workflow. We present MobileWeave, which instead synthesizes every task as an executable program derived from the app implementations of a controllable simulator. MobileWeave abstracts each app into a typed capability graph with semantic types shared across apps, composes atomic programs into single- and cross-app composite programs through explicit value and object dependencies, and verifies each program by native execution, deriving both its instruction and its completion check from the same program. Trained on teacher trajectories collected for these tasks and on recovery supervision at the policy’s own stalls, MobileWeave-8B improves its base model by 26.7 points on MobileGym and by 8.6, 21.3, and 17.6 points on AndroidWorld, AndroidLab, and MobileWorld, with comparable gains at 4B. Beyond these gains, we find that composition is learnable from task structure: explicit dependencies between subgoals act as a training signal that teaches a policy to compose skills it already masters. We release data, model, and code at https://maxxnotmarx.github.io/MobileWeave/ .

[45] On Demand, Not Uniformity: Adaptive Expert Orchestration for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning cs.CVPDF

Mengxin Qin, Kun Wei, Weitao An, Jiahua Li, Xu Yang

TL;DR: 论文面向多模态持续指令微调（MCIT），指出现有基于混合专家（MoE）的方法虽能通过冻结历史专家、增加新专家缓解遗忘，却忽略了不同任务在层级别和token级别的差异化适应需求。为此提出CIRA-MoE框架，利用可学习阈值路由得到专家接受边距作为覆盖信号，选择性地向覆盖不足的层注入新专家，并通过历史与当前专家的相对证据调节token级残差贡献，结合仲裁一致正则化促进专家专业化。

Details

Motivation: 现有MoE式MCIT方法对新增专家和专家贡献采用较为统一或固定的处理方式，未充分考虑不同任务在层梯度压力和token预测难度上的异质性，因此需要解决“何处需要新增容量”和“新专家应对各token贡献多强”两个问题。

Result: 在UCIT和CoIN两个MCIT基准上进行实验，摘要称CIRA-MoE优于现有最先进方法，但未给出具体定量数值。

Insight: 创新点在于按需而非均匀的专家编排：用可学习阈值路由形成统一覆盖信号，以历史接受证据指导覆盖不足层的新专家注入，并以历史与当前专家相对证据进行token级残差仲裁和一致性正则化。可借鉴之处是将持续学习中的容量扩展与token级专家贡献解耦，并用历史路由证据自适应决定层级别容量分配。

Abstract: Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT) enables Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to adapt to sequential instruction data while retaining previously acquired knowledge. While recent Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)-based methods mitigate forgetting by freezing historical experts and introducing new ones, they largely overlook the varying adaptation demands across tasks. Probing incoming tasks reveals pronounced heterogeneity in layer-wise gradient pressure and token-wise prediction difficulty. This observation highlights two unresolved decisions: where additional capacity is needed and how strongly new experts should contribute to individual tokens. To address both questions, we propose the Coverage-Calibrated Expert Injection and Residual Arbitration framework (CIRA-MoE), which derives expert acceptance margins via learnable threshold routing to serve as a unified coverage signal. Specifically, historical acceptance evidence guides the selective injection of new experts into insufficiently covered layers. Meanwhile, relative evidence between historical and current experts modulates token-wise residual contributions, coupled with arbitration-consistent regularization to promote expert specialization. Comprehensive experiments on UCIT and CoIN demonstrate that CIRA-MoE outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods in MCIT.

Abstract: Reconstructing hand-object interactions is an important task, and achieving it with a single camera makes large-scale capture practical but challenging due to heavy occlusions. For two-hand manipulation, we jointly model fingertip forces of both hands and the contact forces between objects when two objects are presented. Result: 在Euclid30K、SolidGeo和ChartQA柱状图上，对4B、8B、Qwen3-VL-Flash、32B配置均优于独立模型，最高分别提升11.68、18.42、24.82个百分点；相同工具/critic/交互限制下，结构化Board比共享自由笔记高6.67–13.00点、比无Board重试高7.35–18.80点，并少用7.9%–38.2% token；SFT后直接回答与无critic工具使用进一步提升。

Abstract: Multi-step visual reasoning suffers when early visual misinterpretations persist as incorrect premises in subsequent reasoning. We argue that how visual evidence is represented during reasoning matters as much as how much of it is gathered. Rather than appending observations and inferences to an ever-growing dialogue, we introduce a Visual Board-centered harness that separates visual observations, uncertain interpretations, and derived reasoning within a structured, capacity-bounded, and revisable workspace. This separation makes each premise explicit and auditable, so that conflicts between what is seen and what is derived become detectable even by a text-only critic and can trigger targeted reinspection of the original image.
