[48] NullTok: empirical-null token selection for training-free long video understanding cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Zhang Ziyue, Juwei Jian, Baiyang Song, Yunshan Zhong, Jun Peng

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需训练的 NullTok 方法，用于长视频多模态大模型理解中的视觉 token 压缩。它不先做易受噪声影响的帧选择，而是对每帧每个 patch 用 64 组匹配的无关控制查询构建经验零分布，将目标相关性校准为右尾蒙特卡洛 p 值，再结合高召回语义守卫、视觉去重和联合时空预算分配筛选候选 token。

Details

Motivation: 长视频会累积大量视觉 token，给现有多模态大语言模型带来理解挑战。现有帧选择与 token 选择及其简单结合方法依赖帧级相关性估计，噪声可能导致不可逆的证据丢失，因此需要更可靠地评估查询相关证据的时空分布并实现免训练压缩。

Result: 作者将 NullTok 应用于 LLaVA-Video-7B 和 Qwen3-VL-8B 两个代表性 MLLM，并在多个视频基准上实验。在 64 个 full-frame equivalents 的视觉 token 预算下且不使用字幕和音频时，Video-MME 分别达到 65.81% 和 68.85%，在两个骨干网络的同预算设置下均优于所有评估基线。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新点是从经验零分布校准查询特定证据，将 patch 亲和度转化为右尾蒙特卡洛 p 值，并结合高召回语义守卫、确定性视觉去重和联合时空预算分配，实现免训练长视频 token 选择。客观看，其可借鉴之处在于用匹配的无关控制查询建立 null 参考来降低相关性估计噪声，并以校准筛选替代易丢失证据的粗粒度帧预选。

Abstract: Long video understanding remains a daunting challenge for existing multimodal large language models (MLLMs), mainly due to the large number of visual tokens accumulated in long videos. Currently, frame selection and token selection are two main paradigms for addressing this issue, both aiming to select relevant visual information to form a compact input for MLLMs. A natural approach combines these two paradigms by selecting frames before pruning tokens, but noisy frame-level relevance estimates can cause irreversible evidence loss. In this paper, we study this problem from the perspective of assessing the spatiotemporal distribution of query-specific evidence and propose a novel and training-free approach termed NULLTOK. For every patch across all frames, we construct an empirical-null distribution from 64 group-matched irrelevant control queries, capturing the scores expected in the absence of target-specific evidence. Target affinities are calibrated against these same-position references into right-tail Monte Carlo p-values, which are discrete super-uniform under a conditional exchangeability assumption. Calibrated screening and a high-recall semantic guard produce candidate tokens for deterministic visual deduplication and joint spatiotemporal budget allocation. To validate NullTok, we apply it to two representative MLLMs and conduct extensive experiments on a set of video benchmarks. The experimental results demonstrate its effectiveness under a bounded visual token budget. For example, it achieves 65.81% and 68.85% on Video-MME using frozen LLaVA-Video-7B and Qwen3-VL-8B backbones, respectively, at a cap of 64 full-frame equivalents (FFE), without subtitles or audio. These results outperform all evaluated baselines on Video-MME under the same budget for both backbones. Code is available at https://github.com/potato-kitty/NullTok.

[49] Can AI Understand Indonesian Children’s Book Illustrations? Compliance, Safety, and the Cultural Alignment Gap cs.CVPDF

Rian Adam Rajagede, Rochana Prih Hastuti, Fitri Hasanah Amhar

TL;DR: 论文研究视觉语言模型（VLM）能否理解并应用印尼儿童图书插图的评估标准，从指南合规、安全和文化对齐三个维度展开。作者先在真实出版页面上比较VLM评估与人类基线，再将VLM作为评审器评估两个生成模型产出的插图，并与人类插画师评分交叉验证。结果显示VLM在显式合规和安全标准上较可靠，但在文化对齐上存在系统盲区，AI生成插图也呈现相同问题。

Details

Motivation: 随着AI系统作为评估者和生成者进入儿童出版流程，需要确认其能否可靠满足儿童图书插图在结构、安全和文化层面的规范要求，尤其是政府指南中明确规定的视觉标准。

Result: 在印尼《图书分级指南》的四级阅读标准下，VLM对合规和安全等显式标准表现可靠，但在文化对齐上显著不足，会系统性漏掉人类评审标记为文化不当的内容；AI生成插图同样满足结构与安全标准，却难以捕捉人类评审一致识别的隐含文化细节。

Insight: 论文提出将指南合规、安全和文化对齐结合为统一评估框架，并用真实出版页面和人类评审进行交叉验证，揭示了VLM在文化对齐上的系统性差距；其可借鉴之处在于同时审视VLM作为评审器和生成器时的文化盲区，而不仅关注显式规范符合性。

Abstract: Children’s book illustrations carry developmental, pedagogical, and cultural responsibilities that are often formally codified in guidelines. As AI systems begin entering children’s publishing pipelines, both as evaluators and as generators, a natural question arises: can they reliably meet the structural, safety, and cultural standards required? We investigate this across three criteria: guideline compliance, safety, and cultural alignment, using Indonesia’s Book Leveling Guideline as a government-issued evaluation framework with explicit visual criteria for four reading levels. To understand how well VLMs can identify and apply these guideline criteria, we first evaluate them on real published children’s book pages and compare their assessments against a human baseline. To then examine whether AI-generated illustrations meet the same standards, we apply the VLM judge to images produced by two generation models and cross-check them against a subset of human illustrator raters. We find that VLMs are reliable on explicit criteria such as compliance and safety, but fall short on cultural alignment, systematically missing content that human raters flag as culturally inappropriate. AI-generated illustrations show the same blind spot: they satisfy structural and safety criteria but fail to capture implicit cultural details that human raters consistently identify.

[50] SixthView: A Benchmark for Hidden-View Reconstruction and 3D Assembly Reasoning cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Pooya Khosravi, Jen-Feng Chang, Fnu Pramono

TL;DR: 论文提出 SixthView 基准，用于从五个正交视图和部件形状信息推断第六个隐藏正交视图，并预测缺失视图的密集部件标签网格。基准包含 602 个由 2–10 个部件组成的合成多立方体装配体，并穷举验证与观测一致的所有合法装配体均产生相同目标视图，以保证任务无歧义。作者评测三个视觉语言模型在四种输入条件下的表现，发现精确符号信息对性能影响远大于图像信息。

Details

Motivation: 推断 3D 装配体不可见一侧需要理解部件放置与遮挡，但不同装配体可能匹配相同观测视图却对应不同隐藏视图，导致目标本身存在歧义。论文希望构建一个可精确评估、且能检验符号装配与坐标推理及遮挡下形状恢复能力的基准。

Abstract: Inferring the unseen side of a 3D assembly requires reasoning about part placement and occlusion. Different assemblies can match the observed views while producing different hidden views, leaving the target ambiguous. We introduce SixthView, a benchmark in which models predict a sixth orthographic view from five observed views and information about the component shapes. The benchmark contains 602 synthetic polycube assemblies with 2–10 parts and requires a dense grid of part labels for the missing view, allowing both exact-view and cell-level evaluation. For each item, we exhaustively verify that all legal assemblies consistent with the exact component geometry and observed label grids yield the same target view. With these inputs, the task tests symbolic assembly and coordinate reasoning. The image-only variants additionally require shape recovery from rendered views, where occlusion can make different 3D shapes look identical. We evaluate three vision-language models under four input conditions. Across 552 items with complete evaluations, exact-grid accuracy with images plus exact text ranges from 33.2% to 85.4%. Adding exact text to separate images improves accuracy by 19.7–63.0 percentage points, whereas adding images to exact text changes it by only $-0.5$ to $+2.1$ points. Separate image panels outperform a merged sheet by 6.7–11.1 points. The results show that performance depends strongly on access to exact symbolic information, with little further improvement from images once the geometry is specified. Chengzhuo Gui, Zhenglin Zhou, Fan Ma, Zongxin Yang, Yi Yang

TL;DR: 论文面向视频到4D网格生成中的粘连伪影问题，提出TRoPE拓扑感知相对位置编码：从图拉普拉斯提取谱特征，并结合多尺度扩散特征距离构建成对拓扑关系，通过旋转相位调制顶点-锚点注意力；同时引入顶点级尾部风险目标，并构建MeshSticking-4D基准来量化该失败模式。

Details

Motivation: 现有前馈式视频到4D网格生成方法容易把空间接近但测地距离远的顶点错误地一起运动，根源是运动解码未能区分空间邻近性与网格连通性，造成跨部件运动泄漏和粘连伪影。

Result: 在MeshSticking-4D上，TRoPE在抑制粘连伪影的同时提升整体运动精度，最差顶点误差相比基线降低39.4%，分离失败率从49.35%降至34.90%；在Motion-80和Motion324-Subset上取得更低尾部误差，并在Mesh4D上验证了跨骨干适用性。

Insight: 创新点在于将网格拓扑显式编码为相对位置信息，以谱特征和多尺度扩散特征距离构造拓扑关系，并用旋转相位调制注意力；尾部风险目标聚焦持续高误差顶点，配合专用基准使问题定义与评估更明确。

Abstract: Video-to-4D mesh generation has made substantial progress in recovering dynamic 3D geometry and motion from monocular videos. However, commonly used feed-forward approaches remain prone to sticking artifacts, where geodesically distant vertices move together incorrectly when they become spatially close. We attribute this failure to cross-part motion leakage: spatially nearby yet structurally distant vertices may retrieve similar motion information when motion decoding fails to distinguish spatial proximity from mesh connectivity. To address this issue, we introduce TRoPE, a topology-aware relative positional encoding that makes mesh connectivity explicit in the interaction between vertex queries and mesh-supported motion sources. TRoPE derives spectral features from the graph Laplacian and constructs pairwise topological relations from distances between multi-scale diffusion features, using these relations to modulate vertex–anchor attention through rotary phases. We further introduce a vertex-wise tail-risk objective that emphasizes persistently high-error vertices to explicitly suppress sticking artifacts. To better characterize and quantify these failures, we introduce MeshSticking-4D, a dedicated benchmark for this failure mode. Experiments on MeshSticking-4D show that TRoPE suppresses sticking artifacts while improving overall motion accuracy. Compared with the baseline, it reduces the worst-vertex error by 39.4% and lowers the separation failure rate from 49.35% to 34.90%. Additional evaluations on Motion-80 and Motion324-Subset show lower tail errors on diverse objects, while transfer to Mesh4D demonstrates applicability across backbones.

Jingxing Wang, Teng Wang, Xingyu Lou, Zihan Guo, Jun Wang

TL;DR: 论文系统研究了训练GUI Agent时环境完整性的三个维度（奖励覆盖、数据覆盖、特征覆盖），在MobileGym中分别独立调节各维度训练Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct，并统一在原始完整环境中评估。结果显示奖励与数据覆盖需要保持完整，而冗余特征反而可以剪枝，据此构建的MobileGym-Lite以更低构建成本取得更高成功率。

Details

Motivation: 现有工作出于降低成本、批量构建环境和减少人工的考虑，各自按工程经验牺牲环境的部分完整性，但从未测量这些取舍对训练效果的影响。论文旨在回答：要训练出有能力的GUI Agent，环境需要多完整、应在哪些维度上完整。

Result: 仅奖励单一路径使成功率下降10.53个点，去除冗余数据同样降低成功率；剪除冗余特征反而使成功率从51.61%升至56.13%。据此构建的MobileGym-Lite在完整环境中达到55.48%成功率，超过Full环境的51.61%，且构建成本更低。

Insight: 创新在于将环境完整性拆解为奖励、数据、特征三个可独立调节的维度并量化其训练影响，揭示‘奖励/数据需完整、特征可剪枝’的非对称规律；同时发现特征剪枝的收益依赖按长度加权（等权时反转），并指出长绕路成功样本会被有害强化。可借鉴其可控变量分解实验范式与基于特征剪枝的高效环境构建思路。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents are widely trained in executable GUI environments. To lower cost, build environments in batches, and reduce manual effort, each work trades away part of the environment through its construction choices. We describe these choices along three dimensions: reward coverage, the goal-reaching trajectories the verifier rewards; data coverage, the application data the agent is exposed to beyond what the task reads or writes; and feature coverage, the application features beyond what the task uses. Existing works set each dimension on engineering grounds, but they do not measure its effect on training. How complete must an environment be to train a capable agent, and along which dimensions? In MobileGym, we adjust each dimension separately, train Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct, and evaluate every policy in the original full environment. A verifier that rewards only one reference path lowers success by 10.53 points, and removing unneeded records also lowers success. Pruning unneeded features instead raises success from 51.61% to 56.13%, entirely on tasks whose environments it left unchanged. The gain reverses when each episode is weighted equally and vanishes when only episodes on the pruned tasks are balanced. Without pruning, length weighting there reinforces long detour successes that erode skills on other tasks. Following these findings, we build MobileGym-Lite, which keeps reward and data coverage complete and prunes unneeded features. It outperforms Full in the full environment (55.48% versus 51.61% success) with lower construction cost.

Details

Motivation: 基因敲低后单细胞未来形态演化与分裂时间均不确定；现有方法多预测终点或群体层面变化，缺少对个体轨迹和分裂时序的联合建模。

Result: 在 MitoCheck 的 325 条留出轨迹、17 种敲低和对照上，相较最强基线 FID 降低 34.6%，视频指标 JEDi 降低 45.6%；隐空间预测区间达到接近名义的 90% 覆盖率且比自由拟合噪声模型窄 35%，分裂风险给出的细胞周期长度分布与基于模型的有限样本参考 KS 距离接近。

Insight: 创新在于将随机隐动力学、分裂风险与条件扩散渲染统一为可检验的单细胞世界模型，并利用配对观测识别离散转移；其高斯参考保持和生存似然使噪声动力学与分裂时序可验证，适合时间序列扰动筛选。

Abstract: A cell observed after a gene knockdown has an uncertain future, both in how its appearance will evolve and when it will divide. Existing approaches predict endpoints or population-level changes without jointly modeling individual trajectories and division timing. In this work, we present MitoWorld, a stochastic world model that generates these future appearances as continuous evolution in a Gaussian reference latent, terminated by a division hazard and converted into images by a conditional diffusion renderer. The ordinary transition provably preserves its Gaussian reference, tying its noise to its dynamics; paired observations identify its discrete transition where stationary marginals cannot. The hazard turns division timing into a survival likelihood that can be tested against real cycle lengths. On 325 held-out MitoCheck tracks spanning 17 knockdowns and a control, MitoWorld lowers FID by 34.6% and the video metric JEDi by 45.6% relative to the strongest baselines. Its latent-space prediction intervals achieve near-nominal 90% coverage, are 35% narrower than those of a model with freely fitted noise. The hazard also yields cell-cycle length distributions with KS distance close to the model-based finite-sample reference. Testable single-cell stochastic dynamics thus offer a generative tool for time-lapse perturbation screens.

Details

Abstract: Proactive video understanding requires vision-language models (VLMs) to decide not only what to answer as a video unfolds, but also when newly arrived evidence warrants a response and when to remain silent. Although modern VLMs excel at offline long-video understanding, they struggle in proactive streaming settings, leading prior work to introduce task-specific reinforcement learning or auxiliary triggering modules. We find that explicit reporting rules and structured streaming context substantially improve a frozen VLM’s proactive response performance. We exploit this observation to build SIMPLEPRO, a training-free baseline for proactive video understanding. At each decision step, the model combines recent high-resolution frames, bounded historical observations, and prior answer records to determine whether new information warrants a response and, when needed, generate a concise answer. The system then filters consecutive duplicate answers while retaining the raw outputs as context for subsequent decisions. Without any parameter updates or auxiliary triggers, SIMPLEPRO with a 4B backbone consistently outperforms specialized proactive systems across OVO-Bench, StreamingBench-PO, and ProactiveVideoQA. SIMPLEPRO shows overall performance gains as backbone size increases from 2B to 32B. We hope this simple yet effective method will serve as a strong baseline for proactive video understanding, and we will release our code and inference pipeline to support future research.

TL;DR: 论文提出DORMS，一种仅依赖单目深度图的纯算法图像分割方法。它使用现成单目深度估计器生成深度图，在分割阶段不训练神经网络或学习参数；通过确定性的并查集扫描构建按深度排序的合并树，并结合区域面积、深度持续性和空间接触约束进行区域合并，再用阈值搜索控制目标区域数。

Details

Motivation: 现有分割多依赖学习模型，论文希望探索无需学习分割模型、仅靠几何与深度推理完成图像分割的可行性，并利用单目深度估计器提供的深度信息替代端到端学习分割。

Abstract: We introduce DORMS (Depth-Ordered Region Merging for Segmentation), a purely algorithmic approach to image segmentation from monocular depth maps, requiring no neural networks or learned parameters in the segmentation process. An off-the-shelf monocular depth estimator provides the input depth maps. Our method constructs depth-ordered merge trees through a deterministic union-find sweep, combining component area, depth persistence, and spatial contact constraints to guide region merging. A threshold search determines the segmentation granularity according to a specified target region count, while global depth detrending improves the separation of local geometric structures. Extensive experiments on COCO Panoptic, Cityscapes, and ADE20K demonstrate consistent improvements over classical segmentation baselines and competitive performance against zero-shot neural segmentation models. Notably, our method outperforms all evaluated zero-shot models on Cityscapes, while remaining competitive with several approaches on COCO and ADE20K. These results highlight the potential of purely algorithmic geometric reasoning for image segmentation without learned segmentation models.

[57] SEE: Unlocking the Hidden Perception of Frozen VLMs cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Alin-Ionut Popa, Tuo Zhang, Dimitrios Dimitriadis

TL;DR: 论文提出一种免训练方法 Self-Elicited Evidence（SEE），让冻结的视觉语言模型在初始答案置信度较低时，根据模型自己指出的区域重新观察：先提出可能包含细节的候选区域，再在放大裁剪图上重新作答，最后通过置信度加权投票与原始预测聚合；模型还会用自己的转写文本辅助回答富文本图像。SEE 不需要检测器或 OCR 引擎。

Details

Motivation: 现有 VLM 能理解图像整体内容，但常遗漏问题所依赖的细粒度细节，例如大图中的小物体或密集信息图中的数字；即使答案错误，模型指向的区域往往仍包含证据，因此需要无需训练地挖掘这种隐藏感知能力。

Result: 在五个骨干模型上，SEE 使所有小物体 benchmark 提升 2.3-13.1 个点，并在小物体任务上优于五个免训练基线；使用 SEE 后 Qwen3.5-4B 超过 Qwen3.5-9B 的直接回答，并匹配大 6 倍的 Qwen3.5-27B。

Insight: 创新点在于将模型自身指向的区域作为证据来源，通过低置信度触发二次观察、区域裁剪放大、置信度加权投票和自转写辅助，实现免训练、无需外部检测器或 OCR 的细粒度感知增强；客观看，其可借鉴之处是复用 VLM 已有的定位与局部理解能力来弥补全局回答的细节缺失。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) grasp the overall content of an image but often miss the fine details a question hinges on, such as a small object in a large photo or a number in a dense infographic. Yet even when their answer is wrong, the region they point to often contains the evidence. We introduce Self-Elicited Evidence (SEE), a training-free method that lets a frozen VLM look again where it points. When the initial answer has low confidence, SEE prompts the model to propose regions where the details may appear, answers the question on these enlarged crops, and aggregates the answers with the original prediction through confidence-weighted voting. The model also answers with its own transcript, which helps on text-rich images. SEE needs no detector or OCR engine. Across five backbones, SEE improves every small-object benchmark by 2.3-13.1 points and outperforms five training-free baselines on small objects. With SEE, Qwen3.5-4B surpasses the direct answer of Qwen3.5-9B and matches the 6 times larger Qwen3.5-27B.

[58] TOMAS: Topology-Aware Operation Modeling and Adaptive Staging for Egocentric Hand Forecasting cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Guanzhong Sun, Junyi Ma, Yuxuan Wu, Erhang Zhang, Chenyang Xu

TL;DR: 论文提出TOMAS框架，用于从第一视角视频预测未来手部运动。该方法通过拓扑感知的手物推理机制联合推断交互目标与潜在操作，并利用自适应分阶段机制将未来运动划分为可学习的潜在阶段，结合阶段几何子目标指导轨迹细化。

Details

Motivation: 同一物体在不同操作意图下会产生差异显著的手部轨迹，现有方法未充分建模关节手运动与物体语义之间的内在关系，难以捕捉操作相关的运动模式。

Result: 在多个公开基准上的实验表明，TOMAS在多维、多目标手部运动预测任务上达到state-of-the-art性能。

Insight: 创新点在于拓扑感知的手物推理与自适应分阶段建模：前者关联手部骨骼结构、关节动态、物体语义和空间关系以推断交互目标与操作类型，后者用可学习时间边界和阶段几何子目标提升未来轨迹预测的细粒度与可解释性。

Abstract: Forecasting future hand movements from egocentric videos plays an important role in understanding human intentions and hand-object interactions. However, different manipulation intentions can lead to substantially different hand trajectories even when interacting with the same object. Existing methods have not sufficiently explored the intrinsic relationships between articulated hand movements and object semantics to capture such operation-dependent motion patterns. Therefore, we propose TOMAS, a topology-aware operation modeling and adaptive staging framework for egocentric hand forecasting. Specifically, we develop a topology-aware hand-object reasoning mechanism that associates hand skeletal structures and articulation dynamics with object semantics and spatial relationships to jointly infer potential interaction targets and latent manipulation operations. The resulting object-operation representations provide interaction conditions for future hand trajectory prediction, with supervision adapted to the annotations available in each dataset. Furthermore, we introduce an adaptive staging mechanism that organizes future hand movements into ordered latent stages with learnable temporal boundaries. Stage-specific geometric subgoals are then incorporated to guide trajectory refinement according to the underlying manipulation patterns. Experimental results on multiple publicly available benchmarks demonstrate that TOMAS achieves state-of-the-art performance in multi-dimensional and multi-target hand motion forecasting.

[59] Agreement Is Not Accuracy: LiDAR Elevation Errors Hidden in LiDAR-Camera Extrinsics cs.CVPDF

Yeon Kun Lee, Dongseok Yang, Deok-Kyeong Jang, Choongho Chung, Junyoung Lee

TL;DR: 论文指出LiDAR与相机观测一致并不代表LiDAR内部几何准确：系统性的LiDAR高程偏差会被外参估计吸收。作者利用同步LiDAR强度和相机估计的棋盘格位姿，联合估计跨记录共享的传感器高程校正与每条记录的外参，并通过射线-平面相交定位棋盘格以避开测距误差。实验表明校正可显著降低外参平移参考误差并改善几何一致性。

Details

Motivation: 动机是揭示LiDAR-相机外参标定中“一致性”与“准确性”的混淆：标定后投影对齐可能掩盖LiDAR高程系统偏差，导致内部几何错误被误判为正确。

Result: 在六个同设计、同传感器型号的平台上，剔除记录后的高程校正将平均平移参考距离从7.57 mm降至3.04 mm；在独立结构上降低与稠密相机扫描参考的平面间角度误差并改善图像边缘对齐。标定靶投影和闭环一致性残差变化不大，说明传统一致性指标可能掩盖几何误差。

Insight: 创新点在于诊断并建模外参保定对LiDAR高程偏差的吸收效应，提出用普通棋盘格的同步强度观测和射线-平面模型联合估计共享高程校正与外参，无需大型标定场；客观上提供了“一致性不等于准确性”的标定评估视角。

Abstract: LiDAR and cameras provide complementary measurements for 3D vision, yet agreement between their observations does not establish the accuracy of either sensor’s internal geometry. We show that systematic LiDAR elevation biases can be absorbed into the estimated extrinsics. Using synchronized LiDAR intensities and camera-estimated checkerboard poses, we jointly estimate a sensor-specific elevation correction shared across recordings and extrinsics for each recording. Ray-plane intersections locate the checker pattern without using reported ranges, preventing range errors from directly moving the lookup. Under matched intensity and correction models, this lookup gives smaller camera-consistency residuals than measured-point lookup. Across six identically designed rigs using the same sensor models, recording-excluded corrections reduce the mean translation-reference distance from 7.57 to 3.04 mm. On separate structures, they reduce inter-plane angle errors against references from independent dense camera scans and improve image-edge alignment. Rolling the rigs shifts the plane pairs exhibiting the largest angular improvements, as predicted for an elevation-only correction. Within the ray-plane model, calibration-target projections and loop-consistency residuals change little despite these geometric improvements. These findings show why agreement after extrinsic calibration need not establish correct internal LiDAR geometry. Synchronized observations of a familiar checkerboard provide a practical route to estimating and testing elevation corrections without a dedicated large calibration field. We plan to release code and data for reproducing the main results.

Details

Motivation: 现有神经视觉解码方法学到的神经表示与生物视觉系统的层级处理机制存在根本差异，论文希望缩小该差距，并提升跨模态对齐与零样本图像检索性能。

Result: 在THINGS-EEG受试者内设置下达到top-1 92.5%、top-5 99.27%；在THINGS-MEG上达到top-1 50.25%、top-5 75%，表明方法有效改善零样本图像检索。

Abstract: Brain-computer interfaces aim to establish direct communication between neural activity and external systems, making interpretation of brain signals an important problem. In this context, neural visual decoding seeks to infer visual information from neural activity by learning a mapping between brain responses and the corresponding visual stimuli. It investigates how visual information emerges from neural signals and relates to perceptual processing while enabling the reconstruction or retrieval of visual stimuli from neural signals. Our brain primarily follows a hierarchical pathway to process visual information captured by the eye. However, neural representations learned by existing methods differ fundamentally from those produced by the biological visual system. To bridge this gap and mimic the human vision hierarchy, we introduce Sparse Inter-layer Residual Aggregation for Zero-shot (SIRAZ). SIRAZ is a framework that aligns neural visual representations by jointly modeling early visual processes via fovea- and magnocellular-pathway-inspired Difference-of-Gaussians transformations. To align the semantic information with neural information, it extracts multiple intermediate representations of the image at multiple depths and passes them through the Residual Gated Adapter Mechanism. To study the temporal dynamics of neural signals, the Direction-aware Multi-Scale Temporal Attention Encoder is introduced to align the learned neural representation with the final visual representation. The proposed framework achieves 92.5% top-1 and 99.27% top-5 accuracy in the intra-subject setting on the THINGS-EEG dataset, and 50.25% and 75% top-1 and top-5 accuracy in the THINGS-MEG dataset. The results indicate that combining biologically motivated visual representations with the structured modalities of neural dynamics improves cross-modal alignment and zero-shot image retrieval.

[61] WaveView: The Novel View Is in the Noise cs.CVPDF

Kaiwen Wang, Jan-Hendrik Pauls, Yinzhe Shen, Frank Bieder, Christoph Stiller

Details

Motivation: 现有显式 warp+视频扩散或隐式相机/3D 线索方法会增加推理开销，且生成布局常偏离真值；论文希望在几乎不增加开销、不改变生成器架构的前提下，用源视角对应关系约束生成过程，缓解布局漂移。

Result: 在 DL3DV 基准上，WaveView 在每个 overlap bin 均优于所有基线；最困难 bin 的共视区域 PSNR 比共享生成器的 MetaView 高 0.84 dB，同时快 2.1 倍。动态 NVS 中，用同一噪声机制微调 AnyView，使 AnyViewBench 零样本 PSNR 从 11.58 dB 提升到 12.35 dB。

Abstract: Generative novel view synthesis (NVS) employs a diffusion model to render a static or dynamic scene from a target camera view. Explicit warp methods complete a reprojection of the source with video diffusion models, and implicit methods encode the camera parameters or 3D cues. Both add inference overhead, and the generated layout often drifts from the ground truth. We present WaveView, which injects the warped source into the phase of the initial noise of a pretrained generator. The Dual-Tree Complex Wavelet Packet Transform (DT-CWPT) allows local and anisotropic phase injection. The generator is thus constrained less in disoccluded regions and along the epipolar line. WaveView keeps the architecture and adds negligible overhead. On the DL3DV benchmark, it outperforms all baselines in every overlap bin. In the hardest bin, it gains 0.84 dB PSNR on the covisible region over MetaView, which shares its generator, while running 2.1 times faster. For dynamic NVS, fine-tuning AnyView with the same noise raises its zero-shot PSNR on AnyViewBench from 11.58 to 12.35 dB.

Abstract: People often understand the world by comparing different views of it. Vision-language models (VLMs), however, receive these views as separate images and still struggle to relate them. One problem is data. Training on these tasks requires groups of images with a known relationship: the same object from different viewpoints, or the same scene before and after a change. Motivation: 现有前馈4D重建方法严重依赖数据集提供的相机、LiDAR深度或3D框标注，限制了在未标注驾驶视频上的应用；预训练视觉模型的多视角和单目预测几何不一致，无法直接用于重建。

Abstract: Feed-forward 4D reconstruction enables efficient creation of renderable driving scenes for autonomous driving simulation. However, most existing methods rely heavily on dataset-provided cameras, LiDAR depth, or 3D box annotations, limiting their applicability to unannotated driving videos. Pretrained visual models can predict cameras, depth, and object tracks from RGB alone, but their geometry is mutually inconsistent: multi-view predictions come in short windows with independent scales and frames, while monocular depth is metric only per image. Our key insight is that, once reconciled into a single metric frame, these priors can replace dataset annotations for feed-forward 4D reconstruction. We present \textbf{FreeForward4D}, which calibrates overlapping prediction windows against monocular metric depth and registers them into one frame across time. On this geometry, 2D tracks are associated across cameras in 3D, and objects move through hierarchical motion that combines a shared object velocity with local Gaussian corrections, so that both training and inference use only RGB videos. On Waymo, FreeForward4D achieves 27.66 dB PSNR and 0.836 SSIM while recovering metric-scale cameras and depth without scale alignment. It surpasses STORM, SLARM-F, UFO, DGGT, and StreetForward, which rely on dataset annotations, in full-image PSNR and attains the best RGB fidelity on 8-second windows. RGB-only fine-tuning further adapts the framework to nuScenes and Argoverse 2. Code will be released.

Abstract: The ultimate ambition of visual quality assessment is to reproduce the neural computations of human vision and thereby achieve full alignment with human perception. In this paper, we use a shared, frozen fly visual circuit to perform full-reference quality assessment of frame-interpolated videos, whose artifacts can disrupt temporal consistency. Abstract: Pulmonary embolism (PE) is a pulmonary vascular disease associated with high morbidity and mortality, where delayed diagnosis can be fatal. In this work, we propose a semi-supervised segmentation method, employing generative adversarial networks (GANs) to leverage large pools of unlabeled CTPA scans. The proposed method addresses more effectively the challenges of CTPA image segmentation than previous methods by employing the dice loss and the cross-entropy loss to tackle the highly imbalanced nature of CTPA scans, the feature matching loss to encourage the generator to produce anatomically plausible segmentation maps that are indistinguishable from ground truth, as well as the self-training loss to reinforce learning from highly-confident pseudo-labeled samples. Abstract: The rapid proliferation of AI-generated videos (AIGVs) has raised critical concerns about content authenticity. To this end, we introduce SAVGE, a large-scale semantically aligned AIGV detection dataset comprising 2,369,822 videos, including 424,115 real videos and 1,945,707 generated videos. SAVGE organizes samples into real-fake semantic groups by using captions paired with real videos as prompts for text-to-video generation, thereby providing both group-level correspondence and textual semantic anchors. Based on SAVGE, we further propose Explicit Disentangling and Semantic-aware Contrasting (EDSC), a model-agnostic training framework for learning artifact-focused representations with reduced semantic interference. EDSC first extracts and aligns visual semantic features with textual representations, and then explicitly suppresses semantic information in the artifact branch while contrasting real and fake samples within each semantic group. By integrating caption-guided semantic supervision with intra-group contrastive learning in a two-phase optimization strategy, EDSC encourages detectors to capture generation-related cues under matched semantic conditions. Extensive experiments on SAVGE and GenVideo demonstrate that EDSC improves most evaluated backbone-metric combinations, enhances robustness under challenging evaluation settings, and strengthens cross-dataset generalization. These results highlight the importance of semantic alignment in AIGV detection and validate explicit semantic-artifact disentanglement as an effective strategy for robust detector learning.

Details

Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型使无人机能跟随自然语言指令，但在闭环飞行中仍频繁失败；而修复这些失败所需的状态-动作监督难以由人类大规模标注，规则规划器也不可靠。

Result: 在 TravelUAV 上，经过三轮自我改进，一个监督训练的 2B 参数策略成功率从 46.4% 提升到 67.5%，碰撞减少一半，并比此前 SOTA 高 10.6 个百分点；且无需额外人工标签或推理开销。

Insight: 核心创新是用策略自身从候选修复动作继续飞行的结果来评估修复，从而把采样动作甚至直接模仿会有害的规则提议转化为可靠监督；并通过每轮重新锚定当前策略失败、验证修复并蒸馏，实现递归自我改进。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to follow natural-language instructions, yet still fail frequently in closed-loop flight. Correcting these failures requires repairs, i.e., actions that avoid them at the states the policy itself reaches, which human pilots cannot label at scale and rule-based planners cannot supply reliably. In this paper, we present AeroRSI, a recursive self-improvement framework in which an aerial navigation policy iteratively learns from its own failures. Our key insight is that a failing policy should judge its own repairs by flying on from them. Judged in this way, its sampled actions and even rule-based proposals that hurt it when imitated directly become reliable supervision. Moreover, an episode terminates at its first failure, concealing subsequent failures until the earlier ones are repaired. AeroRSI thus renews its supervision in every round, anchoring the failures of the current policy, verifying repairs by its continuation, and distilling them while keeping its behavior elsewhere. On TravelUAV, three rounds raise the success rate of a supervised 2B-parameter policy from 46.4% to 67.5% and reduce collisions by half, surpassing the previous state of the art by 10.6 points from the same initialization without extra human labels or inference cost. Code and models are available at https://github.com/starsholic/AeroRSI.

Abstract: Recent advances in large vision-language models (LVLMs) have significantly improved video understanding capabilities, yet processing long videos remains challenging due to the substantial computational cost of extensive visual inputs. TL;DR: 论文提出CausalDrive，一种面向数据高效端到端自动驾驶的因果分解框架。该方法将驾驶策略拆分为确定性的名义意图预测和概率性的驾驶风格建模：先从导航命令、地图拓扑和自车状态构建去除风格影响的反事实名义轨迹，再用扩散模型在名义轨迹与专家轨迹的残差上建模多模态驾驶风格。通过这种因果结构化监督，减少常规场景中共享名义行为的冗余学习，提高专家数据利用效率，并在NAVSIM基准上取得有竞争力的结果。

Insight: 论文的主要创新是将驾驶行为因果分解为名义意图预测与风格执行建模，并利用反事实名义轨迹为意图规划器提供不依赖专家轨迹的监督；同时用扩散策略建模名义轨迹到专家轨迹的残差以捕捉多模态风格。客观来看，这种解耦式监督有助于减少共享行为冗余学习，把专家监督集中到有信息量的执行变化上，从而提升数据效率和交互场景表现。

Abstract: Efficient use of expert demonstrations is essential for end-to-end autonomous driving. However, directly imitating expert trajectories couples nominal intention, which specifies where the vehicle should go, with multimodal driving styles, which determine how that intention is executed. Different combinations of these factors can yield similar expert trajectories, obscuring their respective causal contributions and forcing the policy to learn both from a single entangled target. This ambiguity limits how effectively the policy can exploit expert demonstrations. We propose CausalDrive, a causal decomposition framework for data-efficient driving policy learning that separates deterministic nominal intention prediction from probabilistic driving style modeling. Specifically, we construct counterfactual nominal trajectories from navigation command, map topology, and ego state by removing style dependent effects, providing a Nominal Intention Planner with supervision independent of expert trajectories. Conditioned on the predicted nominal intention and surrounding agents, a diffusion-based Style Execution Policy models diverse driving styles as a multimodal distribution over residuals between nominal and expert trajectories. This causally structured supervision reduces redundant learning of shared nominal behavior in routine scenarios, directing expert supervision toward informative execution variations and improving data utilization. CausalDrive achieves 91.2 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 and 89.5 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2, matching or surpassing state-of-the-art performance. Further experiments demonstrate strong performance with limited expert data and clear advantages in challenging interactive scenarios.

Abstract: Magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is an essential medical imaging technique, but its high costs and long acquisition times can limit accessibility. Accelerated MRI reduces acquisition time through k-space undersampling, yet conventional methods often rely on predefined sampling patterns and uniform slice budgets, overlooking anatomical variations and inter-slice relationships. In this paper, we propose a volume-adaptive k-space acquisition framework that jointly learns where and how much to sample across an entire MRI volume. Using only acquired auto-calibration signal (ACS) measurements, our method exploits volume-wide anatomical information to adaptively allocate sampling budgets across slices while identifying informative k-space lines. A learned joint slice–frequency refinement module coordinates sampling decisions while preserving an exact total volume-level acquisition budget. To reconstruct the acquired measurements, we design a sensitivity-aware synchronous cross-slice reconstruction network that estimates coil sensitivities from ACS measurements and progressively refines neighboring-slice reconstructions while enforcing hard k-space data consistency. Both networks are jointly optimized using reconstruction-based supervision without requiring fully sampled k-space at inference. Experiments on knee and brain MRI across multiple contrasts and acceleration factors demonstrate the adaptive behavior of our acquisition framework and evaluate its reconstruction performance.

[79] PatchAdaNet: Unsupervised class incremental learning using local patches and global adapters cs.CVPDF

Kamel Aouaidjia, Yanchao Duan, Zihui Zhen, Xin Li, Yongfei Lv

TL;DR: 论文提出PatchAdaNet，一种用于图像分类的无监督类增量学习方法。它将图像划分为局部patch，在外部数据上训练独立自编码器，并把patch编码器层聚合为跨任务冻结的全局特征层；再用可学习adapter从这些特征重构整图并形成全局表示。为避免存储旧数据导致内存增长和任务噪声，方法引入固定大小的全局特征质心（GFC）惩罚表示漂移，并结合EWC约束可学习层的重要权重，最后在冻结潜表示和GFC上训练线性分类器。

Details

Motivation: 现有基于回放的方法需要存储旧任务样本或特征，内存随任务数增长，且可能保留任务特定噪声、降低泛化能力。论文希望在无监督类增量图像分类中不存储数据，同时缓解灾难遗忘和特征漂移。

Result: 摘要未给出具体定量数值。作者在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、miniImageNet和5-Datasets上，分别在CNN与Vision Transformer设置下进行类增量评估，并对两种架构在相同设置下做比较研究。

Insight: 创新点在于利用图像局部patch共享的边缘、角点和形状等结构特征，将独立patch自编码器聚合为全局冻结层，再通过可学习adapter重构全图；同时用固定大小GFC作为运行平均来惩罚表示漂移而无需存储旧数据，并用EWC保护重要权重。客观来看，该设计以轻量记忆和冻结通用表征降低任务特定噪声与内存开销，且对CNN和ViT做统一比较具有一定参考价值。

Abstract: Biological learning systems acquire knowledge incrementally through broad, reusable representations established early and kept largely stable, while integrating new experience with minimal interference with previously learned knowledge. Replay-based methods approximate this by storing samples or features from previous tasks and mixing them into the current task. However, memory buffers grow with the number of tasks and may retain task-specific noise that reduces generalization. To address these limitations, we propose PatchAdaNet (Patch Adapter Network), an unsupervised class-incremental learning method for image classification that continually updates feature representations using autoencoders. Since images share structural features such as edges, corners, and shapes, we first divide each image into patches and train independent autoencoders on external data to reconstruct each patch. The layers of all patch encoders are aggregated into global frozen layers that generalize across tasks and remain fixed throughout learning. Learnable adapters then build global image representations from these features to reconstruct the entire image. To prevent feature drift without storing data, we introduce the Global Feature Centroid (GFC), a fixed-size running average of adapter outputs updated after each task to penalizes representation drift. Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) is applied to the learnable layers to preserve previously learned features by constraining important weights. Finally, a linear classifier is trained on the frozen latent representation and GFC for class-incremental evaluation. We evaluate PatchAdaNet in both CNN and Vision Transformer settings on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, miniImageNet and 5-Datasets, providing a comparative study of the two architectures under identical class-incremental settings. Code is available.

Details

Abstract: Training-free open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) has made strong progress by adapting CLIP-like representations to dense prediction, but its remaining errors are often local, structured, and tied to the active label space. A successful correction must be visually plausible, legal under the target vocabulary, and executable in the segmentation score field. We propose SEER, a training-free test-time repair layer for Semantic Evidence and Executable Region propagation. SEER casts post-prediction OVSS repair as evidence-conditioned label propagation. The OVSS score space proposes compact candidate labels, a frozen VLM verifies closed-set regional evidence, and an execution gate certifies whether the evidence is legally and semantically actionable. The accepted label is then propagated by an OVSS-side policy: coherent high-confidence regions allow broad propagation, while object- or boundary-risk cases use conservative score-grounded propagation. Across eight standard OVSS benchmarks, SEER reaches 58.0 average mIoU without updating OVSS weights. Abstract: Games are a rich testbed for visual agents, demanding perception, long-horizon planning, and precise control. To address these gaps, we introduce Game2Player, a framework that co-develops game environments and players to collect diverse and verifiable gameplay experience at scale. In the Game2Player loop, a Builder agent adapts existing web games, exposes their internal state, and defines tasks with state-based progress measures and success checks. Abstract: Multi-modal image fusion combines complementary observations to improve scene visibility and perception. Existing methods typically rely on explicit registration, yet large parallax complicates correspondence estimation, while mismatched fields of view leave some regions without cross-modal counterparts. Result: 在16个基准上，AVTR相较Qwen3.5基线平均提升4.5分，同时降低33%推理延迟；并可有效迁移到更小骨干模型，优于评估的2B规模基线。

Insight: 主要创新是将视觉想象/生成式视觉反思作为与文本思维互补的推理模态，并通过学习路由自适应分配推理模态与深度；客观看，其价值在于把“是否视觉反思、何时文本推理”纳入统一策略，兼顾感知证据提取与推理效率。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models increasingly rely on textual reasoning to solve advanced multimodal tasks. Despite its effectiveness, this paradigm faces two fundamental limitations. First, relying on textual reasoning alone can limit perceptual ability in vision-centric questions, where accurate answers depend on interpreting task-relevant visual evidence. Second, applying textual reasoning uniformly across inputs overlooks question-dependent variation in both reasoning modality and depth, often producing verbose and unnecessary traces when little logical inference is required. To this end, we propose Adaptive Visual-Textual Reasoning (AVTR), which introduces visual imagination as a complementary reasoning modality to textual thinking, enabling the model to revisit its visual observations and distill the salient evidence through generative visual reflection. Furthermore, we formulate reasoning-modality selection as a learned routing problem, allowing a single policy to adaptively harness the complementary strengths of visual reflection and textual reasoning in response to the perceptual and inferential demands of the input. Across 16 benchmarks, AVTR improves the average score by 4.5 points over Qwen3.5 baseline, while reducing inference latency by 33%. It also scales effectively into a smaller size backbone, outperforming the evaluated 2B-scale baselines.

Abstract: Visual neural decoding aims to infer perceived visual stimuli from brain activity, offering important insights into human visual cognition while enabling transformative applications in brain computer interfaces and artificial intelligence. Recent advances in pretrained vision-language models have provided a semantically rich representation space for this task. However, reliable alignment between EEG/MEG signals and visual embeddings remains challenging because neural responses are noisy, highly variable across trials, and subject dependent. To address this issue, we propose a unified neural-to-image retrieval framework that improves retrieval performance by aligning neural and image representations in a shared semantic space. The framework integrates dual-stream adaptation, frequency-domain calibration, and phase-guided temperature modulation to promote more robust cross-modal semantic alignment. Specifically, dual-stream adaptation models a low-dimensional shared subspace with correlation regularization to suppress redundant noise and enhance cross-modal consistency. Frequency-domain calibration conditionally modulates neural representations using amplitude and phase statistics. Phase-guided temperature modulation further adjusts the supervision strength in contrastive learning according to the stability of neural responses. Experiments on THINGS-EEG and THINGS-MEG under both intra-subject and inter-subject settings demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms strong baselines. These results suggest that robust neural-to-image retrieval under heterogeneous neural conditions benefits from a stable shared semantic space, frequency-informed representation calibration, and adaptive supervision. Code will be released at github.com/wanfkuabc/Geometry-Regularized-Brain-Embeddings.

TL;DR: 论文提出高斯基元融合（GPF），将异构分辨率多模态图像融合重构到共享的连续高斯空间中。GPF不先重采样到统一分辨率，而是直接用共享高斯几何和模态特定内容表示拟合各模态原生分辨率观测，并在连续表示中学习模态贡献；优化后在最细原生分辨率渲染融合结果。

Details

Motivation: 现有融合方法通常在统一离散网格上估计模态贡献，当源模态原生分辨率不同时需重采样，容易引入伪影并影响后续融合决策。

Result: 在多个红外-可见光基准上取得定量最佳结果，优于配备插值或超分辨率前端的SOTA融合方法；定性上目标轮廓更清晰、纹理更细，模糊和块状伪影更少。

Abstract: Multimodal image fusion integrates complementary observations from different modalities into a unified representation. Existing methods typically estimate modality contributions on a common discrete grid. When source modalities have different native resolutions, constructing such a grid generally involves resampling, which may introduce artifacts and affect subsequent fusion decisions. To address this problem, we propose Gaussian Primitive Fusion (GPF), which reformulates heterogeneous-resolution fusion in a shared continuous Gaussian space. Instead of first converting source images to a common resolution, GPF directly fits their native-resolution observations with shared Gaussian geometry and modality-specific content representations, and learns modality contributions within the resulting continuous representation. Once optimized, the fused representation is rendered at the finest native resolution without resampling the sources. With heterogeneous-resolution inputs, GPF produces clearer target contours and finer textures, with less blurring and blocking artifacts than resample-then-fuse pipelines. Quantitatively, it achieves the best results on multiple infrared-visible benchmarks against state-of-the-art fusion methods equipped with interpolation or super-resolution frontends.

Abstract: Long-horizon visual agents must do more than inspect images: they must turn observations into evidence that remains usable as a task unfolds. Existing active-perception methods teach agents where and how to look, while a final-answer reward gives little structure for deciding which observations satisfy dependent subgoals or should be retained for later use. We present ViStep-RL (Visual Stepwise Reinforcement Learning), which models execution as a dependency-linked graph of evidence-bearing subgoals. A verifier tracks which subgoals are supported by valid evidence, and changes in graph potential provide a net-progress learning signal; an active memory protocol preserves evidence across turns and links retrieval to downstream use. We present TrajWAM, a trajectory-conditioned WAM that improves vision-language driving through auxiliary bird’s-eye-view (BEV) future learning. Training combines factual reconstruction under the executed trajectory with one-sided feature separation under an unlabeled perturbed trajectory. [89] WM^MLLM: Turning MLLMs into World Models cs.CVPDF

TL;DR: 论文提出 WM^MLLM，首个基于多模态大模型（MLLM）构建的交互式世界模型，可从单张图像出发进行持久、可编辑的世界生成。其核心做法是把世界状态放到生成器之外，用带修订历史的全景图表征，由 MLLM 将用户请求转化为对该状态的操作、决定局部编辑所写入的位置，并在候选结果成为下一状态前进行检查；图像生成器只负责提出视角，视频只负责呈现。用户可漫游并返回、通过文本或框编辑物体、放置主体、改变光照/天气/季节。

Details

Motivation: 现有视频世界模型把世界保存在生成器的上下文中，导致重访同一地点时需要重新合成，此前的编辑容易漂移或消失。论文旨在解决世界状态不可持久、不可精确编辑与不一致的问题。

Result: 在 MBench-A 上与 6 个世界模型基线相比，四项环境恢复指标均值全部最高（Epipolar 91.5，对比 0.1–74.0），完成相机往返比例最高（85.3%，对比最高 50.9%）；在 CompBench 上与 15 个基线比较，15 列全部最优。112 个盲评局部编辑中，先生成再选择写入区域使完成率从 34.8% 提升到 83.0%；258 个 WBench 与 MBench-A 样本中，46 次重访全部精确复现先前视角，97 个通过检查的局部编辑均未改变区域外全景像素。

Insight: 创新点在于将世界状态显式外置于生成器（全景图 + 修订历史），让 MLLM 充当状态的操作者、定位者与校验者，而生成器仅作视角提议与呈现，从而在机制上保证重访的精确复现与局部编辑的像素级局部性；此外，先生成候选再由 MLLM 选择写入区域，显著提升编辑完成率，这种“状态外置 + 大模型调度生成器”的解耦范式值得借鉴。

Abstract: Video world models keep the world in the context of a generator, so a revisit is synthesized again and earlier edits can drift or disappear. We present WM^MLLM, the first interactive world model built on multimodal large language models (MLLMs), for persistent, editable world generation from a single image. WM^MLLM keeps the world state outside every generator, as a panorama with a revision history, and lets the MLLMs write it: they turn each request into operations on this state, select where a local edit is written, and check every candidate before it becomes the next state. Image generators only propose views, and video only presents them. Users can walk to new places and return, edit objects through text or a box, place subjects, and change lighting, weather or season. By construction, a revisit at the same revision reproduces the earlier view exactly, a local edit leaves every panorama pixel outside its region unchanged, and each request starts from the result of the previous one. Against six world model baselines on MBench-A, WM^MLLM has the highest mean on all four environment-recovery metrics (Epipolar 91.5 versus 0.1 to 74.0) and completes the most camera round trips (85.3% versus at most 50.9%). It has the best value in all 15 CompBench columns against 15 baselines. On 112 local edits judged blind, selecting the region to write after generation raises completion from 34.8% to 83.0%. On 258 WBench and MBench-A items, all 46 revisits recover their earlier views, and all 97 local edits that pass the check leave panorama pixels outside their region unchanged.

We propose a dual-view semantic token bridging framework that exploits raw images and sketch images as complementary visual supervision to learn raw-oriented and sketch-oriented EEG semantic token streams. Abstract: Vision-language generation has predominantly relied on autoregressive and discrete diffusion models. Autoregressive models generate tokens sequentially, while discrete diffusion models typically predict tokens independently within each denoising step. We propose ViLFlow (Vision-Language Flow), a continuous-flow vision-language model that combines joint modeling of output embeddings with parallel token decoding. ViLFlow adapts pretrained continuous language generation to visual question answering and image captioning through learned image guidance and prediction-aware token decoding. A visually adapted teacher provides image-guided predictions as additional denoising supervision. The denoiser learns to reproduce these predictions directly, retaining one image-conditioned prediction per generation step. We train the token decoder on denoiser predictions and paired reference text to improve the conversion of continuous outputs into words. The decoder can be adapted independently, improving answering and captioning without further denoiser training. For captioning, it also supplies text feedback during late denoising, allowing decoded words to inform subsequent refinement. ViLFlow outperforms autoregressive and masked-diffusion baselines by up to 8.46 percentage points on VQAv2 and 4.50 points on GQA without GQA training. On COCO Karpathy test, it improves CIDEr by 16.25 over masked diffusion and 17.81 over sampled autoregression.

TL;DR: 论文提出 CityNav-VLA，一种用于城市空中语言目标导航的双帧视觉-语言-动作模型。它将动态的自我中心观测与持久的地图中心空间记忆结合：无人机在固定北向地图上增量积累视觉证据，并通过指令、当前观测、状态和运动历史检索记忆以预测地图坐标系目标，再由确定性控制器转换为机体坐标系动作。方法还引入无需额外可训练参数的早期目标承诺机制以稳定长时程目标。

Details

Motivation: 语言目标空中导航的关键难点在于：飞行中的自我中心感知不断变化，而长距离导航需要与稳定地理位置绑定的空间证据。论文旨在弥合全局地图推理与局部机体执行之间的鸿沟。

Result: 在 Test Unseen 上，NE 降低 21.2%，SR 和 SPL 分别提升 15.8% 和 30.9%；数据集精炼后，相较此前 SOTA，NE 降低 6.2%，SR 和 SPL 分别提升 5.6% 和 23.2%。4B 规模骨干下，单张 A100 平均每决策 0.68 秒、峰值显存 8.6 GB。

Abstract: Language-goal aerial navigation requires a UAV to reach a destination specified in natural language using observations collected during flight. A key challenge is linking changing egocentric perception with spatial evidence anchored to stable locations for long-range navigation. We propose CityNav-VLA, a dual-frame vision-language-action model for urban navigation integrating dynamic egocentric context with persistent allocentric spatial memory. The UAV incrementally accumulates visual observations on a fixed north-up map initialized with geographic and landmark priors, preserving evidence across viewpoint changes. A dynamic query conditioned on the instruction, current observations, UAV state, and motion history retrieves relevant memory evidence to predict a map-frame target. A deterministic controller converts this target into body-frame commands using the UAV’s current pose, connecting global reasoning with egocentric execution. To reduce target fluctuations over long horizons, early goal commitment (EGC) fixes the execution target to the coordinate-wise median of the first $K$ predictions without extra trainable parameters. On Test Unseen, CityNav-VLA reduces NE by 21.2% and improves SR and SPL by 15.8% and 30.9%, respectively. It also reduces NE by 6.2% and improves SR and SPL by 5.6% and 23.2%, respectively, relative to the former SOTA after dataset refinement. Cumulative ablations show that navigation-map features yield the largest gains, while metric coordinates further improve endpoint accuracy. With a 4B-scale backbone, inference averages 0.68 s per decision with 8.6 GB peak memory on a single A100, presenting remarkable model efficiency.

Details

Motivation: 传统影视制作中，若要在拍摄后增加新机位，需要昂贵的补拍。现有几何引导的重拍方法依赖前馈重建得到的点视频，但在长焦、浅景深、低调光的人物中心电影视频中重建质量下降，生成结果会继承退化外观。

Result: 论文在客观指标和人类评估中均优于现有重拍方法，但摘要未给出具体benchmark名称和数值。

Insight: 创新点在于利用训练-推理一致的真实多相机视频，使点视频主要提供人物与场景构图而非退化外观；并用几何锚定RoPE偏移保持与源视频的时间同步且避免复制点视频的退化外观；结合预训练R2V模型和可选参考图补全源视频未展示区域。

Abstract: Filming a scene from an additional angle after production usually requires a costly reshoot. We present One Shot Any View, a system that takes a recorded clip and generates a reshot video of the same performance along a user-specified camera trajectory. As in recent geometry-grounded methods, the system conditions generation on the source video and an explicit 3D prior, namely a point video rendered along the requested trajectory from a feed-forward reconstruction of the source video. We target human-centric cinematic videos, which are often shot with long lenses, shallow depth of field, and low-key lighting, all of which degrade feed-forward reconstruction. Existing geometry-grounded methods tend to inherit the degraded point video, and their output quality drops visibly. Two designs make such point videos usable. First, in addition to rendered videos of 3D assets, we train on real-world multi-camera videos, whose point videos are produced by the same reconstruction pipeline as at inference. This training-inference consistency enables the generator to take only the composition of people and scene elements from the point video, and their appearance from the source video. Second, a geometry-anchored RoPE offset keeps the generated video temporally synchronized with the source while largely preventing the output from copying the degraded appearance of the point video. Meanwhile, since the generator is a pretrained reference-to-video (R2V) model, the system also accepts optional character and scene reference images, which supply the appearance of regions that the source never shows. In both objective and human evaluations, One Shot Any View outperforms existing reshooting methods.

Zecheng Tang, Jiaye Fu, Youtian Lin, Xinyao Rao, Liuxuan Sun

TL;DR: 论文针对视频扩散模型中长序列注意力开销大的问题，提出免训练块稀疏注意力方法 Spark-Attn，包含按注意力偏好递归重分组的 Spark-Reblock 和对压缩块进行注意力质量校准的 Spark-Reweight，并在 MiniMax-H3 上实例化为 Spark-H3。

Details

Motivation: 现有块稀疏注意力使用固定 token 分组，容易把注意力偏好不同的 token 分到同一组；同时对压缩块采用均匀 key 平均会低估其注意力质量，导致效率与生成质量难以兼得。

Result: 在 MiniMax-H3 上，10% exact-block density 时实现 2.49 倍 DiT 加速，相对稠密输出达到 24.81 dB PSNR；20%密度下 PSNR 提升至 26.76 dB，仍有 2.26 倍加速，论文宣称达到免训练 BSA 在视频扩散模型上的 SOTA 质量-效率权衡。

Insight: 创新点在于免训练地结合自适应 token 重分组与基于全局均值查询锚点的压缩块重加权，在保持规则块形状以利于 GPU 高效执行的同时，缓解固定分组和均匀平均带来的注意力偏差。

Abstract: Long video sequences make attention a substantial cost in video diffusion models. Block sparse attention (BSA) reduces this cost by computing selected blocks exactly while compressing the remaining blocks. However, fixed token grouping can place tokens with dissimilar attention preferences in the same group, while uniform key averaging can underestimate the attention mass of compressed blocks. We introduce Spark-Attn, a training-free BSA method comprising Spark-Reblock and Spark-Reweight. Spark-Reblock recursively groups tokens by attention preference while preserving regular block shapes for efficient GPU execution. Spark-Reweight uses the global mean query as an anchor to construct attention-weighted key-value representations and logit biases for the compressed blocks, calibrating their attention mass. We instantiate Spark-Attn in MiniMax-H3 as Spark-H3. At 10% exact-block density, Spark-H3 delivers a 2.49$\times$ DiT speedup and reaches 24.81 dB PSNR against the corresponding dense outputs on MiniMax-H3 (20 denoising steps, 345 frames, 1344$\times$768). Increasing the density to 20% further improves PSNR to 26.76 dB while still achieving a 2.26$\times$ DiT speedup. These results demonstrate that Spark-H3 achieves a state-of-the-art quality-efficiency trade-off for training-free BSA on video diffusion models.

[95] Do Thinking VLMs Need to Keep Looking at Long Videos? Write It Down, Then Look Away cs.CVPDF

Pengyu Li, Zhitao Gao, Lingling Zhang, Heng Wang, Chengqian Ma

TL;DR: 论文提出 LookAway：让长视频视觉语言模型先写下带时间戳的笔记，记录问题所需信息，然后在后续推理中隐藏视频的 KV 缓存，从而避免每个解码步重复读取整段视频。方法无需训练即可在接近全视频思考准确率的同时大幅降低解码成本，并通过自训练和强化学习进一步缩短初始观看长度。

Details

Motivation: 长视频 VLM 在数百到数千 token 上推理时，每个解码步都要重读整个视频的 KV 缓存，计算成本很高；论文质疑模型是否必须在整个推理过程中持续“看”视频。

Result: 在 Qwen3.6-27B 上，无额外训练的 LookAway 准确率接近完整视频思考，建模解码成本仅为其 0.12-0.13 倍；训练后模型中位约 66-68 个 token 后隐藏视频，在三个留出 benchmark 上以匹配准确率仅读取 27-35% 的 KV 位置。

Insight: 核心创新是将长视频信息先压缩成带时间戳笔记，再在推理中隐藏视频 KV 缓存；利用视频依赖集中在响应早期的现象，并用强化学习奖励正确答案、惩罚 KV 读取，鼓励模型尽早结束观看。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) reason about long videos over hundreds to thousands of tokens. Every decoding step re-reads the key-value (KV) cache of the entire video. Must the model keep looking while it reasons? In LookAway, the model first writes timestamped notes on what the question needs and then puts the video away. Once the notes close, the inference engine hides the video’s KV cache for the rest of the response. Across both hybrid and pure-attention VLMs, dependence on the video is concentrated early in the response. On Qwen3.6-27B, writing notes preserves accuracy better than free reasoning at the same withdrawal step. Replacing them with another video’s notes harms accuracy, whereas keeping the video visible alongside the model’s own notes adds no significant benefit. Without additional training, LookAway nearly matches full-video thinking accuracy at 0.12-0.13 times its modeled decode cost. To shorten this initial look, we fine-tune the model on its own correct LookAway responses. Reinforcement learning then rewards correct answers while penalizing KV reads. The trained model puts the video away after a median of 66-68 tokens. Across three held-out benchmarks, it writes more tokens than an identically trained full-video model yet reads only 27-35% as many KV positions at matched accuracy.

[96] Look Closer, Look Back, or Wait for Agentic Streaming Video Understanding cs.CVPDF

Pengyu Li, Zhitao Gao, Lingling Zhang, Wenxuan Huang, YiCheng Xiao

TL;DR: 论文提出 ActiveForager，一个流式视频理解智能体：它只维持一个干净的短时工作窗口，针对每个查询按需选择直接回答、放大近期高分辨率帧、从记忆回忆历史事件或等待未来帧。回忆历史时由共享权重子智能体独立验证候选片段并返回紧凑证据摘要，再用流解耦注意力掩码隔离历史证据与当前视觉 token。策略通过反事实信息增益训练，仅在所采集证据使正确答案更可能时才奖励工具调用。

Details

Motivation: 流式视频模型必须在严格的内存与延迟约束下回答实时视频问题：只输入最近几帧能让当前感知保持敏锐，但加入过去帧会稀释对当前场景的注意力并引发幻觉；现有级联方法在不确定时才检索过去帧，当当前帧模糊或决定性未来线索尚未出现时仍会失败。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 与 Qwen3-VL-8B 上，于 OVO-Bench 同时提升实时感知、历史回忆与主动响应，Qwen3-VL-8B 总体准确率从 47.6% 提升至 65.9%；在多跳 Video-Holmes 基准上零样本准确率翻倍，且每次查询仅增加极少的视觉 token。

Insight: 摘要宣称的创新点包括：按需取证的工具化流式智能体、共享权重子智能体的候选片段独立验证、流解耦注意力掩码，以及基于反事实信息增益的策略训练以避免工具坍缩。从客观角度看，其核心价值在于把“何时看近、何时回看、何时等待”显式建模为可学习决策，并用信息增益作为奖励信号来校准工具调用，这一设计对流式视频理解中的记忆与延迟权衡具有较好借鉴意义。

Abstract: Streaming video models must answer questions about a live video under strict memory and latency limits. Feeding only the latest few frames keeps present perception sharp, whereas adding past frames dilutes attention on the current scene and triggers hallucinations. Recent cascade methods retrieve past frames when uncertain, but they still fail when current frames are blurry or when decisive future clues have not appeared yet. We introduce ActiveForager, a streaming video agent that maintains a clean short working window and gathers extra evidence only on demand. For each query, the agent either answers directly, zooms into high-resolution recent frames, recalls past events from memory, or waits for upcoming frames. When recalling history, a shared-weight sub-agent verifies candidate clips in isolation and returns a compact evidence summary. A stream-decoupled attention mask then keeps historical evidence from interfering with present visual tokens. We train this policy using counterfactual information gain, which rewards a tool call only when the gathered evidence makes the true answer more likely and prevents tool collapse across all actions. Across Qwen2.5-VL-7B and Qwen3-VL-8B on OVO-Bench, ActiveForager improves real-time perception, historical recall, and proactive response at once, lifting overall accuracy on Qwen3-VL-8B from 47.6% to 65.9%. It also doubles zero-shot accuracy on the multi-hop Video-Holmes benchmark while adding minimal visual tokens per query.

Yushen Xu, Xiaosong Li, Huaming Wu, Dongwei Ren

Details

Motivation: 高帧率红外-可见光视频融合需要恢复缺失帧，但级联插值与融合会累积误差并导致亮度波动；运动、遮挡以及缺失可见帧造成的语义引导缺失，进一步增加内容选择难度。

Abstract: Infrared and visible video fusion at high frame rates requires recovering missing observations when camera rates differ or fall below the desired output rate. Cascading interpolation and fusion can accumulate errors and cause brightness fluctuations. Motion and occlusion further complicate content selection, particularly when missing visible frames also remove direct semantic guidance. We propose SAFI, which uses semantics at the target time to guide joint interpolation and fusion. When the visible frame rate exceeds the infrared rate, available visible frames provide semantic context directly. At times without visible observations, motion guides local matching, and propagation recovers semantics from neighboring frames. Semantic context complements aligned image features at the target time, while matching confidence helps select reliable infrared and visible information. Reconstruction and fusion share source selection within each scale, allowing supervision on missing frames to guide fusion. To preserve visible appearance and reduce flicker, we bound thermal enhancement and penalize brightness inconsistencies across frames while accounting for changes in thermal contrast. Experiments on three datasets show improved visual quality with less computation than cascaded baselines and consistent temporal gains when input rates differ. SAFI maintains strong downstream tracking performance despite missing frames. The code is available at https://github.com/XylonXu01/SAFI.

Abstract: Domain adaptation for semantic segmentation is challenging when target-domain images are unavailable during training. Zero-shot domain adaptation addresses this by adapting models using only textual descriptions of the target domain. However, existing prompt-learning methods are not well suited for ResNet-based CLIP vision backbones or pixel-level cross-modal feature alignment. We propose CROMPT, a prompt-learning framework for zero-shot domain adaptation in semantic segmentation. [99] AnchorUp: Anchoring Feature Upsampling on a Classical Interpolation cs.CVPDF

Kotaro Horikawa, Keisuke Toida, Junya Komori, Kazuhiro Hotta

TL;DR: 论文提出 AnchorUp，一种以经典插值为锚的特征上采样方法。它不再让网络直接回归高分辨率特征图，而是用无参经典插值提供平滑内容，再由小型引导网络预测插值结果的修正量，并通过单个可学习标量门融合两者。方法参数高效，仅用 NAF 约四分之一参数和 64% 延迟，在多个骨干和密集预测/视频分割基准上与更强基线相当或更优。

Details

Motivation: 视觉基础模型的特征位于粗网格，密集预测需要像素级特征；现有学习上采样器通过交叉注意力回归高分辨率特征，但大量平滑区域可由经典插值免费获得，网络容量被浪费在重算平滑内容上。因此需要只学习经典插值无法提供的边缘和细节修正。

Result: AnchorUp 仅 167,425 参数，为 NAF 的四分之一，延迟为 NAF 的 64%；仅在 DINOv3 上用 NAF 两阶段中的第一阶段训练，在 Pascal VOC 和 Cityscapes 上与 NAF 两阶段训练相当，在 DAVIS-2017 视频目标分割上超过 NAF，且增益在七个骨干上成立，轮廓精度 F 提升更明显。

Insight: 核心创新是将学习上采样重构为“经典插值锚 + 残差修正”，用标量门融合，避免网络重复学习平滑内容；分析表明学习到的修正集中于边缘和细节，使小网络即可达到强性能。客观看，这种锚定式残差设计把归纳偏置显式注入上采样器，提升了参数和延迟效率，也提供了一种可解释且易迁移到多骨干的轻量上采样范式。

Abstract: Vision foundation models produce semantically strong features on a coarse patch grid, and dense prediction needs them at pixel resolution. Existing learned upsamplers close that gap by regressing the high-resolution feature map from the low-resolution one, typically through cross-attention driven by the input image. We observe that most of what they regress needs no learning: plain bilinear interpolation already reproduces the smooth regions of the target, and recent benchmarks even report bilinear and bicubic on par with several learned upsamplers. The rest is mostly edges and fine details, which suggests that these learned upsamplers spend much of their capacity re-computing the smooth content that classical interpolation provides. We therefore introduce AnchorUp, which stops predicting the high-resolution feature map and predicts only its correction from the interpolation. It keeps a parameter-free classical upsampler as an anchor, obtains a second prediction from a small guidance network, and blends the two through a single learnable scalar gate. Because the anchor supplies the smooth content, the guidance network only has to correct what remains. An analysis of the learned correction confirms that it concentrates on edges and fine details, and so the network can be small: AnchorUp uses 167,425 parameters, a quarter of the budget of the strongest baseline, NAF, and runs at 64% of its latency. Trained once on DINOv3 with only the first of NAF’s two training stages, and evaluated on that backbone plus six held-out ones, it is already on par with NAF trained through both stages on Pascal VOC and Cityscapes. Our method surpasses it on DAVIS-2017 video object segmentation; the gain holds on every backbone and is larger in contour accuracy F. Code is available at https://github.com/KotaroHorikawa/AnchorUp.

Abstract: Embodied AI systems, such as drones and autonomous vehicles, improve navigation by enhancing perception in environments containing unseen objects. Despite their potential, these models encounter significant challenges in cross-domain generalization, hindering their practical use in embodied AI applications. Our work sets a robustness-driven benchmark, providing key insights and laying the foundation for future research in open-world embodied AI perception. The benchmark and project page are available at https://aniruddhsikdar1.github.io/publications/pixel2perspective/

[101] CanopyKV: Remember beyond the Crop for Fast Active Visual Reasoning cs.CVPDF

Lei Yang, Dayan Wu, Yiliang Zhu, Xinze Liu, Hengjie Zhu

TL;DR: 论文提出 CanopyKV，一种面向主动视觉推理多模态模型的免训练 KV 缓存压缩框架。它利用父-子图像裁剪关系保留对后续探索有用的互补历史证据，并保持已保留历史稳定、保护持续推理所需状态。方法借助工具元数据作为保留信号，避免额外注意力打分，同时通过紧凑 KV 存储降低后续注意力开销。

Details

Motivation: 主动视觉推理通过迭代裁剪和工具调用积累大量图像块与推理 token，导致 KV 缓存膨胀、内存和推理成本上升。现有 KV 压缩方法基于注意力、新近性或冗余性选择历史 token，未显式区分被局部替换的证据和未来探索仍需的互补信息，可能损害准确率或增加搜索成本。

Result: 在 VisualProbe、V*、HR-Bench 和 MME-Lite 上，CanopyKV 在所评估的 KV 压缩方法中取得最优答案准确率和端到端效率。在 MME-Lite 上，它仅保留约 Dense 总 KV 的四分之一，同时准确率提升 10.5 个百分点，并将完整推理加速 1.64 倍。

Insight: 创新点在于将图像裁剪的父-子层级关系引入 KV 压缩，以区分局部替换证据与互补历史证据，并利用工具元数据提供免注意力的保留信号。客观上看，其可借鉴之处是把多模态工具调用过程中的结构信息转化为缓存管理信号，在不训练、不额外打分的情况下兼顾历史信息保留与推理效率。

Abstract: Active visual reasoning enables multimodal models to answer challenging visual questions by gathering fine-grained evidence through iterative image inspection and tool use. However, accumulating crops and reasoning tokens expands the key-value (KV) cache, making compression important for reducing memory and inference costs. Existing KV compression methods typically select historical tokens based on attention, recency, or redundancy. These criteria do not explicitly distinguish locally replaced evidence from complementary information needed for future exploration: each crop refreshes a local region, while earlier views may still support subsequent decisions. Discarding such evidence can impair accuracy or require additional search, while scoring and cache reorganization can offset the computational savings from compression. We introduce CanopyKV, a training-free KV compression framework that uses parent-child crop relationships to retain complementary evidence, keeps retained history stable, and protects the state needed for ongoing reasoning. Tool metadata provides retention signals without additional attention scoring, while compact KV storage reduces subsequent attention costs. Across VisualProbe, V*, HR-Bench, and MME-Lite, CanopyKV achieves state-of-the-art answer accuracy and end-to-end efficiency among the evaluated KV compression methods. On MME-Lite, it retains approximately one-quarter of Dense’s total KV while improving accuracy by 10.5 percentage points and accelerating complete reasoning by $1.64\times$.

[102] Where Should a Diffusion Transformer Meet Its Teacher? cs.CVPDF

Zhuoqi Hu, Shiwei Li, Haozhao Wang, Xiandi Luo, Yihao Ouyang

TL;DR: 论文研究Diffusion Transformer中表示对齐（REPA）应作用于哪一层教师监督。作者发现最佳对齐层会随训练阶段变化，提出AdaREPA：通过衡量flow-matching与对齐目标的梯度兼容性，并用softmax概率路由在线采样监督层。该方法以很小训练开销自适应调整教师监督，且不增加推理成本。

Details

Motivation: 现有REPA通常将预训练视觉教师的监督固定接到学生模型的单一中间层，但学生表示随训练演化，固定层可能不再是最适合对齐的层；若逐层搜索又代价高昂，因此需要在线自适应选择监督层。

Result: 在ImageNet-256上，AdaREPA能加速收敛并提升生成质量，且适用于不同模型规模；摘要未给出具体数值或SOTA比较，但强调训练开销极小且无额外推理成本。

Insight: 创新点在于揭示监督层适配性随训练阶段变化，并提出基于梯度兼容性评分与softmax概率路由的动态层选择机制。客观看，将“对齐层选择”转化为在线轻量路由问题，兼顾训练效率与监督效果，对扩散模型训练中的自适应教师监督有启发意义。

Abstract: Representation Alignment (REPA) significantly accelerates Diffusion Transformer training by guiding intermediate student features with a pretrained visual teacher, yet existing methods typically attach supervision to a single fixed layer. As student representations evolve, however, we find that the most effective layer for alignment varies across training stages. To track this evolving layer suitability online without costly search, we measure gradient compatibility between the flow-matching and alignment objectives, and propose AdaREPA, an online routing framework that adaptively selects the supervision layer as training progresses. AdaREPA uses a softmax distribution over smoothed layer compatibility scores to sample a layer for alignment at each training step. This probabilistic routing adapts teacher supervision to the student’s changing learning needs, with minimal training overhead and no additional inference cost. On ImageNet-256, AdaREPA accelerates convergence and improves generation quality across model sizes, while revealing how the most suitable layers for supervision shift over training.

[103] StrideWorld: Benchmarking Scene Rigidity and Action Consistency in Interactive Video World Model cs.CVPDF

Chuang Wang, Rui Nie, Jie Song, Junyuan Gao, Jiahe Song

TL;DR: 论文提出 StrideWorld，一个用于交互式视频世界模型的基准，评估相机运动下场景刚性和动作一致性。它使用运动前固定的参考：起始视角真值几何和模型自身初始步测得的动作步长，避免参考随测试视频拟合而掩盖形变或节奏错误。论文对多个开源模型在140个起始视角生成的视频进行评测，并公开代码、数据和榜单。

Details

Motivation: 现有基准的参考常由被测视频自身重建或自由缩放对齐命令轨迹，会吸收渐进场景形变和节奏、路线长度变化，导致错误漏检。因此需要一个参考先于运动固定、能更严格检测场景刚性和动作一致性的评测基准。

Result: 在140个起始视角、多个开源模型生成的视频上，除一个模型外均出现场景形变，偏移最高达同路径刚性渲染的7.3倍；单动作保持时多数模型节奏稳定，但组合路线中常停滞且终点偏离自身步长预测；仅两个按估计场景深度缩放命令的模型使多数路线接近预测，微调两个模型可消除多数中途停滞。

Insight: 摘要宣称的创新点是将参考锚定在起始真值几何和模型自身首步步长，实现“自洽但不自适应”的动作一致性评测，并用已知相机运动渲染验证测量、与人工判断一致。客观看，其价值在于揭示参考拟合评测的盲区，并提供可复现基准、数据和榜单。

Abstract: Interactive video world models turn user commands into the exploration of a generated scene, which requires the scene to keep its 3D structure as the camera moves and each action to keep a stable effect. Existing benchmarks mostly evaluate these properties against references fitted to the video under test, either a scene reconstructed from its own frames or a camera trajectory aligned to the command path with a free scale. Such references can absorb gradual scene deformation and changes in pace and route length, so these errors can pass undetected. We therefore introduce StrideWorld, a benchmark whose references are fixed before the motion they judge. It evaluates scene rigidity against the ground-truth geometry of the start view, and action consistency against the stride of each action, the camera motion of one command. The stride is measured on the model’s first steps and then used to test the steps that follow, so each model is judged only against its own steps. Its measurements are validated on rendered videos with known camera motion, and its scores agree with human judgments. Across \nvideos{} videos generated by \nmodels{} open models from $140$ start views, all models but one deform their scenes as the camera moves, with offsets up to $7.3$ times those of a rigid rendering along the same camera path. Most models keep a steady pace when a single action is held. Along composed routes, several models often stall, and most completed routes end away from the positions predicted by each model’s own strides. Only the two models that scale commands by the estimated scene depth bring most routes close to these positions. Fine-tuning two models on a few rendered clips removes most of their mid-route stalls. Code, data, and a leaderboard are publicly available at https://github.com/chuangwang123/strideworld.

Yuhan Li, Shuran Zhang, Haixin Tian, Baonuo Rao, Weipeng Li

TL;DR: 论文提出 PhysResponse 基准，用于评估视频生成模型对物理参数（如重力、摩擦、初速度）的响应能力，而非仅关注视觉合理性。该基准包含场景条件下的模拟器反演和临界转变事件评估两个层面，并发现现有图像到视频模型物理参数误差显著偏大。为弥补训练信号缺失，作者构建 72,000 条受控多物体交互模拟视频进行微调，证明可降低参数误差并迁移到真实视频和外部物理基准。

Result: 评估十个图像到视频模型发现：最低参数误差仍比物理正确视频高一个数量级以上，模型很少同时复现临界转变的两种结果，且两个评估层面暴露出 PSNR 等视觉指标忽略的互补失败。使用 72,000 条受控模拟视频微调后，参数误差下降，并可泛化到未见交互类型、真实视频和外部物理基准；结合数值编码器继续训练可进一步降低未见类型上的三项误差。

Insight: 创新点在于从“物理合理性”转向“物理参数响应性”，设计双层基准：通过模拟器反演恢复有效重力、摩擦和初速度，并通过事件评估检验临界转变附近及跨转变的结果。客观看，其贡献还包括构建大规模受控多物体交互模拟数据作为训练信号，并验证其跨域迁移价值。

Abstract: Video generation models are increasingly viewed as world simulators, which requires not only plausible motion but dynamics that respond correctly to specified physical parameters. Existing models, datasets and benchmarks, however, largely target physical plausibility, leaving this physical parameter responsiveness insufficiently explored. We introduce PhysResponse, a benchmark that measures it at two levels: scene-conditioned simulator inversion recovers the effective gravity, friction and initial speed of generated motion, and event evaluation tests outcomes near and across critical transitions. Evaluating ten image-to-video models, we find that even the lowest parameter errors exceed those of physically correct videos by over an order of magnitude, that models rarely reproduce both outcomes of a transition pair, and that the two levels expose complementary failures that visual metrics such as PSNR miss. TL;DR: 论文提出 SkimPrefill，用于加速长交错图文输入下视觉语言模型的预填充。它先用小型混合模型以半分辨率读取整个输入至早期层，并利用一个注意力头对图像或段落等完整单元排序，再将排名靠前的单元作为短提示交给目标 VLM。在 MMLongBench 128K 等 19 个数据集上验证了显著加速且保持平均准确率。

Details

Motivation: 长交错图文输入使 VLM 预填充耗时很长，现有剪枝方法需用目标模型或同族小模型打分，而打分本身开销大，抵消剪枝收益。论文发现问题证据集中在少数完整单元中，且小型混合模型早期层的一个注意力头即可定位这些单元。

Result: 在 MMLongBench 128K tokens、19 个数据集和五类任务上，保留 10% 输入 token 时，SkimPrefill 将 Qwen3.5-9B、Gemma-4-12B 和 Qwen3-VL-32B 的预填充分别加速 3.7 倍、11.6 倍和 8.2 倍，同时维持平均准确率。

Insight: 创新点在于用小型混合模型只运行到早期层并以半分辨率快速“略读”输入，再基于单个注意力头做单元级排序；一个未经训练的 selector 可服务不同模型族的目标模型。客观来看，将长输入证据定位到早期注意力头并做整单元剪枝，避免了昂贵的逐 token 打分，兼顾效率与跨模型通用性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can answer questions about long interleaved inputs, such as documents of many pages and collections of hundreds of images, but they are slow to start: on inputs of about 100K tokens, the prefill takes most of the wait for the first output token. A common approach prunes the input by scoring its tokens with the target model itself or with a small model of the same family that first writes an answer. However, this scoring takes much of the time that pruning saves. We find that the evidence for a question lies in a few whole units (images or paragraphs), and that a small hybrid model finds them with one attention head of an early layer, even at half image resolution. Based on these findings, we propose SkimPrefill, which lets the small hybrid model read the whole input first, up to that early layer, with images at half resolution. Most layers of the small hybrid model (Qwen3.5-0.8B) run in linear time, so this pass is short. SkimPrefill then ranks every unit with that head and gives the target the top units as an ordinary shorter prompt. One untrained selector serves targets of different model families. Extensive experiments on MMLongBench at 128K tokens, with 19 datasets of five task types, show the effectiveness of SkimPrefill. With 10% of the input tokens kept, SkimPrefill speeds up the prefill of Qwen3.5-9B, Gemma-4-12B and Qwen3-VL-32B by 3.7 times, 11.6 time and 8.2 times while maintaining their average accuracy.

Motivation: 现有面向文本到图像生成的思维链方法主要依赖文本推理，难以完整保留和利用图像中的视觉语义信息；而将图像作为上下文逐轮传递又会导致早期不合适图像干扰模型，且反复整图重生成会引入并累积新的幻觉。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has proven effective in improving the reasoning abilities of large language models by breaking complex tasks into step-by-step solutions. In recent years, researchers have attempted to extend CoT reasoning to visual generative models to improve their ability to handle complex tasks. However, existing CoT methods for text-to-image generation still rely mainly on textual reasoning and therefore cannot fully use and preserve the correct semantic information already present in an image, as even the most detailed language cannot capture all visual information. With advances in image generation, models such as FLUX and BAGEL can generate images conditioned on visual inputs. Recent work has attempted to carry images forward as context in the chain of thought, but this introduces another limitation: unsuitable earlier images can strongly interfere with the model, while repeated whole-image regeneration introduces new hallucinations at each round and causes them to accumulate. To address these issues, we propose MLIE-CoT, a visual chain-of-thought framework based on multi-level image editing. MLIE-CoT learns to select the repair granularity from three levels, preserving correct semantics whenever possible during reasoning while reducing the effect of incorrect content. To improve the ability of multimodal models to route errors of different severity to appropriate repair levels, we construct an image-repair dataset covering different error cases and use it to train a repair-level router. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MLIE-CoT significantly outperforms both its base models and methods using fixed repair strategies, producing clear improvements across multiple benchmarks.
