[108] Retroactive Concept Bottleneck Models for Exemplar-Free Class-Incremental Learning cs.CVPDF

Yang Ji, Ying Sun, Yuanxin Zhuang, Yuting Zhang

TL;DR: 论文提出 RetroCBM，用于无样本类增量学习中的可解释概念瓶颈模型。它保留少量与词汇无关的统计量来关联冻结视觉特征和预训练视觉语言模型嵌入空间，并通过回溯回归规则和全历史分类损失重投影，使新概念能有效参与旧类预测，同时用图加权惩罚抑制系统性概念错误导致的类间区分问题。

Details

Motivation: 现有持续概念瓶颈模型在无样本类增量学习中多关注保留已学概念或用生成特征近似旧类，导致新概念在旧类预测中利用不足。论文希望在不保留旧样本的情况下，让新概念也能被有效监督并贡献于旧类预测。

Result: 在六个数据集上的所有类增量设置中，RetroCBM 均优于当前最先进的可解释方法；相比最强无样本基线，平均增量准确率取得 18.1% 的相对提升。

Insight: 创新点在于存储少量词汇无关统计量以支持新概念监督；提出回溯回归规则，使新概念预测器等价于全历史类平衡岭回归；重投影统计量恢复全历史平方分类损失以学习新概念对旧类预测的贡献；并引入图加权惩罚来约束概念错误造成的 margin shift。

Abstract: Exemplar-free class-incremental learning (EFCIL) requires models to learn new classes sequentially without retaining training samples from previous tasks. Continual concept bottleneck models (CBMs) make their predictions interpretable by identifying visual concepts in an image and using them to score each class. However, these methods typically focus on preserving learned concepts or approximating past classes with generated features, which can leave new concepts underused in old-class predictions. We propose RetroCBM, which retains a small set of vocabulary-independent statistics relating frozen visual features to the embedding space of a pretrained vision-language model. Even after earlier training images have been discarded, we can use these statistics to supervise new concepts. To do so, we develop a retroactive regression rule that yields the same predictor for each new concept as class-balanced ridge regression on all images seen so far. We then reproject the stored statistics to recover the full-history squared classification loss and thus learn how much each new concept should contribute to old-class predictions. Since the classifier may use systematic concept errors to distinguish nearby classes, we further introduce a graph-weighted penalty on the margin shifts caused by those errors. Across six datasets, RetroCBM outperforms state-of-the-art interpretable methods in all class-incremental settings, with an 18.1% relative gain in mean average incremental accuracy over the strongest exemplar-free baseline.

[109] Every Query Routes Itself: Accelerating Video Diffusion Transformers with Per-Query Routed Sparse Attention cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Zhen Huang, Shuwei Li, Ruizhe Yao, Xinrui Chen, Danyi Liu

TL;DR: 论文针对视频扩散Transformer中长token序列的自注意力推理开销大的问题，提出了一种免训练的逐查询路由稀疏注意力方法PQRS。该方法只对key进行聚类，让每个query独立选择自己的key聚类，并用未选中簇的质心进行补偿。在此基础上，论文还设计了高效的执行策略：按相同的key簇选择把query分桶，一次遍历合并结果。该方法在多个视频生成模型上实现了比现有稀疏注意力更快的加速，同时输出更接近稠密注意力。

Details

Motivation: 现有基于聚类的稀疏注意力让同一query簇内的所有query共享同一套key簇选择，论文发现这种共享会限制精度：在key簇和稀疏度固定的情况下，query簇越细化精度越高，逐query独立选择时最准确。因此论文希望通过取消query聚类共享来提升稀疏注意力的精度。

Result: 在Wan和HunyuanVideo系列的四个文生视频和图生视频模型上，PQRS是所评估的稀疏注意力方法中最快的，且输出最接近稠密注意力；在Wan2.1-T2V上达到28.9 dB PSNR，在HunyuanVideo上达到35.4 dB PSNR，端到端加速1.66–2.10倍。

Insight: 创新点在于：通过实证表明query聚类共享key选择会损害精度，提出仅聚类key、每个query独立路由并用质心补偿未选簇的免训练方案；在系统层面，由于逐query选择无法形成共享key列表的query tile，论文改为遍历key簇、按相同选择把query分桶成稠密任务并一次合并，同时沿用上一步去噪的固定softmax偏移以保持数值一致。客观上，工作兼顾了稀疏注意力的精度与不规则访存下的执行效率。

Abstract: Self-attention over long token sequences dominates the inference cost of video diffusion transformers. Clustering-based sparse attention clusters both queries and keys and lets all queries in a cluster share one selection of key clusters. We show that this sharing limits accuracy: with key clusters and density fixed, attention grows more accurate as the query clusters are refined, and is most accurate when each query selects on its own, which needs no query clustering at all. We therefore propose Per-Query Routed Sparse Attention (PQRS), a training-free method that clusters only keys, lets each query select its own key clusters, and compensates for the unselected ones with their centroids. Per-query selections leave no query tile with a shared key list, so PQRS traverses key clusters instead of queries, buckets queries with identical selections into dense tasks, and merges their partial results in one pass under a fixed softmax offset carried from the previous denoising step. On four text-to-video and image-to-video models from the Wan and HunyuanVideo families, PQRS is the fastest of all the sparse attention methods we evaluate and the closest to the dense output, reaching $28.9$ dB PSNR on Wan2.1-T2V and $35.4$ dB on HunyuanVideo at $1.66$–$2.10\times$ end-to-end speedup.

[110] Comparing Each Generation with Independent Siblings Gives Video World Model Post-Training a Proper Objective for Outcome Frequencies cs.CVPDF

Xin Li, Yiwen Chen

TL;DR: 论文研究视频世界模型作为物理模拟器时的后训练目标：当同一首帧存在多种可能结果时，重复生成应匹配数据中的结果频率。作者指出仅用单一记录未来做物理后训练会使模型偏向最可能结果，并提出SibPO，通过同一历史下带独立噪声的“兄弟”生成估计截断逆频率权重，同时加入与兄弟生成一致性的校正项，使有限组下的期望目标成为结果单元上的严格适当散度。

Details

Motivation: 现有物理后训练让每个生成视频对齐唯一记录未来，虽提升单视频保真度，但期望评分对模型分布线性，容易被“总是生成最可能结果”所最大化，导致结果频率偏差，不符合世界模型作为模拟器需复现数据结果分布的需求。

Result: 在PisaBench上，SibPO在四个视频模型中的三个上将模拟着陆时间与真实分布的KS距离相对其奖励族外最强方法降低9.1%至10.2%，且不损害单视频质量；同时将估计机器人策略成功率的误差降低13%。

Insight: 创新点在于用同历史独立噪声的兄弟样本估计生成结果频率，并以此构造逆频率加权的一致性目标；第二项减去与兄弟生成的一致性，使有限样本组下期望目标仍为严格适当散度。客观看，其价值在于把视频世界模型后训练从单样本保真转向结果频率匹配，并给出有限组偏差校正。

Abstract: Video world models are used as simulators, so when a first frame leaves several physical outcomes open, repeated generations should produce each outcome as often as it occurs in the data. Physics post-training scores every generation against the single recorded future of its history, which makes single videos more faithful, but the expected score is linear in the model distribution and is maximized by always producing the most likely outcome. The log-likelihood of the recorded outcome is maximized by the data distribution, and its gradient weights agreement with the record by the inverse of how often the model produces the generated outcome, a frequency that other generations of the same history with independent noise, its siblings, can estimate. To train with this weight from a finite group, we propose SibPO, which performs Sibling-paired Policy Optimization: siblings estimate a truncated inverse-frequency weight for agreement with the record, and a second term subtracts the same weight times the agreement with the siblings, which makes the expected objective a strictly proper divergence over outcome cells at every group size. On PisaBench, SibPO lowers the KS distance to simulated landing times by 9.1-10.2% relative to the strongest method outside its reward family on three of four video models without degrading single videos, and it lowers the error of estimated robot policy success rates by 13%.

[111] RAID: Representation Autoencoder for Intrinsic Decomposition cs.CVPDF

Claudia Cuttano, Tamy Boubekeur, Elena Garces, Giuseppe Vecchio

TL;DR: 论文提出 RAID，一种基于 DINOv3 强预训练表示的单图内在分解方法。它先在结构化潜在空间中用 X→X teacher 学习重建各内在图，再用轻量模态特定投影训练 student 从 RGB 预测 teacher 表示，从而在单次前向中输出所有内在属性。该方法以 165M 参数实现比扩散方法快 100 倍的分解，并保持有竞争力或更优的质量。

Details

Motivation: 单图内在分解是病态问题，需要从单张 RGB 中解耦强耦合的外观因素。现有方法依赖大型扩散先验，带来模型大、迭代慢和随机性强的问题，论文希望证明精确分解不必依赖大型生成模型。

Result: RAID 使用 165M 参数，比扩散类方法快 100 倍；摘要称其达到有竞争力或更优的分解质量，并对输入分辨率更鲁棒，但未给出具体 benchmark 名称和数值。

Insight: 核心创新是在强预训练表示上解耦重建与分解：用 X→X teacher 避免 RGB-to-intrinsic 配对监督，用轻量模态投影支持半标注训练和单次前向预测。客观看，其创新点在于以 DINOv3 表征空间替代扩散生成先验，在效率、紧凑性和可解释模态特征空间之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Single-image intrinsic decomposition is an inherently ill-posed problem, requiring the disentanglement of strongly coupled appearance factors from a single RGB observation. Recent methods address this ambiguity with diffusion priors, at the cost of large, iterative, and often stochastic models. We show that accurate decomposition does not require a large generative model. Our key idea is to decouple reconstruction from decomposition on top of a strong pretrained representation. First, an $X \rightarrow X$ teacher learns to reconstruct each intrinsic map from DINOv3 features using a shared decoder, without paired RGB-to-intrinsic supervision. Then, a DINOv3-based student predicts the corresponding teacher representations from RGB through lightweight, modality-specific projections, enabling training on partially annotated datasets and predicting all intrinsics in a single forward pass. \textbf{RAID}, predicts intrinsic properties in the structured latent space, enabling a compact 165M-parameter model, 100x faster than diffusion-based methods. Despite its compact size, it achieves competitive or superior decomposition quality, with improved robustness to input resolution. Additionally, our learned projections yield interpretable, modality-specific feature spaces.

[112] SCRiD: Spatial and Contextual References for Generalizable AI-Generated Video Detection cs.CVPDF

Hao Tan, Zekai Zuo, Boyang Chen, Jinghui Qin

TL;DR: 论文提出 SCRiD，一种面向可泛化 AI 生成视频检测的框架，通过引入空间与上下文参考来解读视频中的变化，而非依赖孤立的合成伪影。方法包括匹配坐标残差响应 MCR、真实条件分数自适应 RCSA 和可逆证据交换 REE，并在多基准多子集上验证了泛化检测性能。

Details

Motivation: AI 生成视频检测的泛化性仍具挑战，因为自然运动与内容变化可能类似合成伪影，使单一视频差异成为不可靠的真实性线索。论文旨在利用互补的空间和上下文参考，提高跨生成器和任务的检测鲁棒性。

Result: 论文仅用 4 个生成器的合成视频训练，在覆盖 6 个基准的 88 个生成器或任务子集上评估，平均 ACC 和 AP 分别达 72.72% 和 74.23%，比最强基线高 3.24 和 0.81 个百分点；消融与鲁棒性实验验证了各组件有效性及常见后处理下的稳定性。

Insight: 创新点在于用匹配子像素采样条件下的运动与坐标参考刻画时序残差关联，从真实视频学习内容条件去噪参考并在多噪声尺度和质量视图下探测响应以指导特征自适应，并通过可逆辛更新实现互补证据交换。客观看，其可借鉴之处是将检测依据从孤立伪影转向多参考条件下的响应一致性，以增强跨生成器泛化。

Abstract: Generalizable AI-generated video detection remains challenging because natural motion and content variations can resemble synthesis artifacts, making isolated video discrepancies unreliable indicators of authenticity. To address this challenge, we propose Spatial and Contextual Reference-informed Detection (SCRiD), a framework that interprets video variations through complementary spatial and contextual references. Specifically, we introduce Matched-Coordinate Residual Response (MCR) to characterize temporal residual associations under motion and coordinate references with matched subpixel sampling conditions. We further develop Real-Conditioned Score Adaptation (RCSA), which learns a content-conditioned denoising reference from authentic videos and probes its responses across multiple noise scales and quality views to guide feature adaptation. To integrate these complementary sources of evidence, we design Reversible Evidence Exchange (REE) to enable information exchange through reversible symplectic updates. We train SCRiD using synthetic videos from only 4 generators and evaluate it across 88 generator or task subsets spanning 6 benchmarks. Our method achieves average ACC and AP of 72.72% and 74.23%, respectively, outperforming the strongest baseline by 3.24 and 0.81 percentage points.Ablation and robustness experiments further validate the effectiveness of the proposed components and the stability of SCRiD under common post-processing operations.

[113] Fuse Before Reconstruct: A Novel Reconstruction Paradigm for Multimodal Industrial Anomaly Detection cs.CVPDF

Zhi Li, Hanyu Wang, Qiyu Chen, Fei Shen, Zhengtao Zhang

TL;DR: 论文面向多模态工业异常检测，指出现有模态内重建忽略跨模态互补信息、跨模态重建存在投影误差和一对多映射歧义的问题，提出“先融合再重建”的FBR范式：先通过双向跨模态注意力与可学习逐像素门控融合图像和点云特征，再以双路径协同重建恢复原始模态特征并施加跨模态一致性约束。

Details

Motivation: 现有多模态工业异常检测中的特征重建主要分为模态内和跨模态两类，前者忽视跨模态互补信息，后者易受投影偏差和模糊一对多映射影响，导致检测失败，因此需要一种更稳健的多模态特征重建范式。

Result: FBR在MVTec 3D-AD上达到98.3% I-AUROC，在Eyecandies上达到92.6% I-AUROC，分别较此前最先进方法提升2.9%和4.1%。

Insight: 主要创新在于将多模态分析从“分别重建/跨模态映射重建”转为“先深度融合再重建”，并通过双向跨模态注意力、通道对齐与动态空间先验、逐像素门控融合以及双路径重建的一致性约束，缓解投影偏差和映射歧义。客观上看，其可借鉴点是把融合质量与重建约束联合设计，以多模态一致性提升异常检测鲁棒性。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection plays a pivotal role in precision manufacturing, where joint analysis of multimodal data emerges as a critical approach to enhance detection accuracy. Feature reconstruction, a dominant paradigm for such joint analysis, is primarily implemented through intra-modal and cross-modal methods, both of which are plagued by significant limitations. Intra-modal reconstruction neglects complementary cross-modal information, while its cross-modal counterpart is prone to projection errors and ambiguous one-to-many mappings (e.g., same textures mapping to multiple geometries), leading to detection failures. To address these limitations, we propose a novel feature reconstruction paradigm, Fuse Before Reconstruct (FBR), which first fuses multimodal features and then performs reconstruction for anomaly detection. Firstly, we propose a bidirectional cross-modal attention mechanism to achieve deep feature complementarity between image and point cloud features, mitigating projection bias through channel alignment and dynamic spatial prior integration. Besides, a learnable pixel-wise gated fusion strategy is adopted to preserve multi-modal structural consistency while optimizing local feature combination. Finally, dual-path collaborative reconstruction is implemented to recover original modal features from the fused representations, where cross-modal consistency constraints effectively eliminate mapping ambiguities. Extensive experiments show that FBR achieves 98.3% I-AUROC on MVTec 3D-AD and 92.6% I-AUROC on Eyecandies, surpassing previous state-of-the-art methods by +2.9% and +4.1%, respectively.

Abstract: Image-to-code methods for 3D scene reconstruction produce executable code that generates editable digital content and supports visual simulation. However, existing methods primarily rely on appearance similarity, which provides limited guidance for distinguishing errors in geometry, materials, and illumination. Light shapes scene appearance through its interactions with geometry and materials, influencing shading, cast shadows, and reflections. We therefore present LightHarness, a VLM-based agentic framework that incorporates explicit lighting reasoning into iterative scene reconstruction. To be specific, we leverage complementary geometric, intrinsic, and illumination cues to jointly refine scene structure and appearance, and propose Lighting Reasoning Memory to maintain light-source hypotheses, which supports evidence, and revision history throughout iterations. Together, they support reasoning about partially visible and unseen sources, with evidence-guided verification directing subsequent revisions. Our framework reconstructs explicit scenes through coherent reasoning about geometry, materials, and illumination without additional training or differentiable rendering. Extensive experiments demonstrate superior image-to-code reconstruction accuracy under challenging shadows and complex illumination, further supporting downstream scene editing, object insertion, and versatile relighting.

[115] BaseRSI: Recursive Self-Improvement of a Base Vision-Language Model via Training-Free Harness Internalization cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Mingda Zhang, Yuanhao Dong, Qiang Huang, Rui Qian, Yanjin Li

TL;DR: 论文提出 BaseRSI，一种面向基础视觉语言模型的递归自我改进新范式：模型先改进其提示/工具等外挂框架（harness），再通过免训练权重编辑把外挂带来的增益内化到自身参数中，并多轮基于自身响应迭代。方法包含 ReGap 与 ReKey，分别用逐 Transformer 块的高维激活差监督并以低维子空间更新，以及将信号回归写入少量 KV 神经元并测量编辑位置与强度。

Details

Motivation: 现有递归自我改进的收益多停留在基础模型外部的提示和工具框架中，监督仅依赖黑盒输出，且权重编辑的位置和强度预先固定、不够精确；论文希望把外挂框架的增益真正内化到基础模型权重中。

Result: 在十个视觉推理基准上，BaseRSI 在分布内和留出基准上均能持续提升基础视觉语言模型；摘要未声称达到 SOTA，主要强调稳定一致的改进。

Insight: 创新点在于提出递归式框架内化范式，并设计 ReGap 以逐块激活差进行高维监督、低维子空间更新，再用 ReKey 将编辑回归到少量 KV 神经元并基于自身响应确定编辑位置与强度；所有递归角色仅靠前向传播完成，不同位置的编辑可在同一检查点中组合而无需重新拟合。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) is moving from the harness of prompts and tools around a base model toward the model itself. However, existing RSI still faces key challenges, including harness-bound gains that stay outside the base model, black-box supervision that reads only the model’s outputs, and imprecise edits whose site and strength are fixed in advance. To address these challenges, we propose BaseRSI, a new paradigm of Recursive Harness Internalization: the base model improves its harness, then writes what the harness adds into its own weights through training-free edits, round after round on its own responses. To support this paradigm, we introduce ReGap, a mechanism that supervises every transformer block with the high-dimensional activation difference that the harness makes at that block alone, and updates the block’s weights along this signal in a low-dimensional subspace. Building on this mechanism, we further propose ReKey, an algorithm that writes this signal by regression into a few key-value neurons of every block, and measures the site and strength of each edit on the model’s own responses. Moreover, the base model fills every role of this recursion with forward passes alone, and its edits, written at different sites, compose in one checkpoint without refitting. Experimental results on ten visual reasoning benchmarks show that BaseRSI consistently improves a base vision-language model both in distribution and on held-out benchmarks. Our code is available at https://github.com/beita6969/BaseRSI.

[116] ALIVE: Bring Foundation Features to Life with Events cs.CV | cs.ROPDF

Rong Zou, Haiqing Hao, Davide Scaramuzza

TL;DR: 论文提出ALIVE，一种基于事件相机的方法，可在两次图像观测之间增量更新视觉基础模型特征。它以锚点图像提取的冻结DINOv3多层特征为起点，利用后续事件流对特征进行传输和校正，从而避免反复运行昂贵的图像编码器。轻量更新器仅用合成数据端到端训练，无需下游任务监督，并能零适配迁移到真实事件流。

Details

Motivation: 视觉基础模型特征会随着图像观测之间的场景变化而变得陈旧，传统刷新方式需要新帧并重复运行计算昂贵的图像编码器。论文旨在利用事件流在图像帧之间低成本、低延迟地更新基础模型特征。

Result: 在三个真实数据集和五个任务-数据集设置上，ALIVE在目标检测、语义分割和深度估计的所有指标上均优于所评估的基于事件的方法。在DSEC上，当锚帧已过时200ms时，检测提升30.9 mAP、分割提升11.6 mIoU、深度AbsRel改善15.9%；相比图像重编码，FLOPs减少82.4%，延迟降低2.5倍。

Insight: 创新点在于将事件流用于基础模型特征的增量刷新，通过轻量更新器对冻结DINOv3的多层特征进行传输与校正，并以纯合成数据训练实现无需下游监督和真实事件流零适配迁移。客观上看，其可借鉴之处是解耦特征提取与特征更新，利用事件相机的高时序性降低重复图像编码成本。

Abstract: Visual foundation models provide powerful representations for diverse tasks, but their features become stale as visual content changes between image observations. Refreshing them requires new frames and repeated runs of a computationally expensive image encoder. We introduce ALIVE, an event-based method that incrementally updates native foundation model features between image observations. Given an anchor image, ALIVE uses subsequent events to transport and correct existing multi-layer features extracted by a frozen DINOv3 encoder. Its lightweight updater is trained end-to-end using only synthetic data, without downstream task supervision, and transfers directly to real-world event streams without any adaptation. Across three real-world datasets and five task-dataset settings spanning object detection, semantic segmentation, and depth estimation, ALIVE outperforms all evaluated event-based methods in every metric. On DSEC, when the anchor frame is 200,ms old, ALIVE improves over anchor-image predictions by 30.9,mAP in detection, 11.6,mIoU in segmentation, and 15.9% in depth AbsRel. Compared with image re-encoding, ALIVE reduces FLOPs by 82.4% and latency by a factor of 2.5.

[117] EgoJev: Finding Evidence in Egocentric Video by Reading VLM Decisions cs.CVPDF

Chunggi Lee, Hanspeter Pfister

TL;DR: 论文提出 EgoJev，一种面向可穿戴助手的免训练帧选择方法，用于回答长第一视角视频中依赖短暂关键时刻的问题。它让冻结的 VLM 在带标签的缩略图候选中选择证据位置，并从输出分布中读取选择结果，再聚焦对应窗口并按分布分位数选取少量答案帧，由同一个 VLM 完成选择、回答和证据时间返回。

Details

Motivation: 长第一视角视频中的问题往往只依赖某一短暂瞬间，均匀稀疏采样帧容易被 VLM 漏掉；现有免训练方法要么依赖额外的图文模型排序帧，要么让 VLM 直接写出时间，效果和效率均受限。

Result: 在 QAEgo4D 上，使用 Qwen3.5-4B 和 8 个答案帧时，EgoJev 将准确率从均匀采样的 51.2% 提升到 62.0%，并在 QAEgo4D 和 MyEgo 上优于近期免训练帧选择方法；单层级版本可在 Mac 和 iPhone 上运行。

Insight: 核心创新是发现 VLM 在带标签帧候选中做选择时更能定位关键时刻，且可直接从其输出分布读取选择，优于图文打分或生成时间跨度。EgoJev 据此用单个冻结 VLM 同时完成证据选择、回答和时间返回，无需第二模型，并可在录制时预编码粗粒度缩略图以适配可穿戴设备。

Abstract: Questions about long egocentric video often depend on one brief moment, such as the second a hand puts an object down. A vision-language model (VLM) that sees a few evenly spaced frames usually misses it. Training-free methods rank frames with a separate image-text model or ask the VLM to write a time. In a controlled test, the VLM itself locates the moment better when it chooses among labeled frames, and we read this choice from its output distribution. With the same candidates, this choice lands on the evidence more often than image-text scores or a written time span, and it compares all candidates in one decision. Based on this finding, we propose EgoJev, a training-free frame selector for wearable assistants. EgoJev reads where the evidence is from labeled thumbnails and looks again at the windows it points to. It places its few answer frames at the quantiles of the resulting distribution. One frozen VLM selects, answers and returns the evidence time, with no second model. A wearable device can encode its coarse thumbnails while it records. With Qwen3.5-4B and 8 answer frames, EgoJev raises QAEgo4D accuracy from 51.2% with uniform sampling to 62.0%, and it outperforms recent training-free frame selectors on QAEgo4D and MyEgo. A single-level version runs on a Mac and an iPhone.

Kangjie Chen, Xiangyu Li, Jinhao Deng, Dongbin Zhang, Chaoda Zheng

TL;DR: 论文提出H3Remover，基于预训练视频编辑器MiniMax-H3 Base Ref2VA，在无需配对“无目标”训练数据的情况下，用强化学习实现视频目标移除。方法通过DiffusionART在冻结主干的前提下优化LoRA适配器，并设计目标移除、背景几何、外观三类奖励，外加冻结HPSv3的感知质量反馈，引导模型真正移除目标而不损伤周边场景。

Details

Motivation: 预训练视频编辑器虽具备移除与补全的先验，但容易残留待移除目标或改动无关内容，且训练中难以获得配对的“移除后”目标视频。论文希望在没有配对数据的情况下约束编辑行为，实现干净、保真的视频目标移除。

Result: 实验显示，相比原始H3，论文方法在重建指标上有显著提升；定性对比表明目标移除更有效，且对周围场景内容的保持更好。

Insight: 创新点在于用与“揭示更远内容”一致的深度变化作为目标移除奖励，避免仅改色等表面编辑；结合相对深度序保持和受保护区域RGB约束来限制无关改动；整体形成无需配对数据的奖励强化框架，并以固定评估区域、冻结主干仅调LoRA的方式实现。

Abstract: Pretrained video editors possess rich removal and completion priors, yet may retain targets or alter unrelated content. We present H3Remover, which reinforces MiniMax-H3 Base Ref2VA for video object removal without paired object-free training targets. To discourage superficial edits such as recoloring, a target-removal reward favors calibrated depth changes consistent with revealing farther content. To limit unintended scene changes, a background-geometry reward encourages preservation of relative depth ordering. To preserve background appearance without retaining unwanted shadows or reflections, an appearance reward constrains RGB changes in source-derived protected regions while exempting targets and estimated effects. Frozen HPSv3 complements these constraints with perceptual-quality feedback for synthesized content. Evaluation regions remain fixed across candidates, and DiffusionART optimizes LoRA adapters with the backbone frozen. Experiments show substantial reconstruction gains over original H3, with qualitative comparisons demonstrating more effective object removal and better preservation of surrounding scene content.

[119] AggCLIP: Aggregating CLIP for Zero-Shot Multimodal Anomaly Detection cs.CVPDF

Zhi Li, Hanyu Wang, Fei Shen, Zhengtao Zhang

TL;DR: 论文提出 AggCLIP 框架，用于零样本多模态异常检测（ZSMAD），在不使用目标类别训练数据的情况下同时检测外观缺陷和几何缺陷。该方法基于冻结的 CLIP 表示，引入几何感知空间聚合（GSA）和上下文感知空间聚合（CSA）分别增强点云局部几何结构与 RGB 多尺度空间上下文，并通过双流提示对比损失（DSPC）对齐点云流与 RGB 流中的正常/异常语义。

Details

Motivation: 现有基于 CLIP 的零样本多模态异常检测方法存在三维到二维点云渲染中的几何信息丢失、原始 CLIP patch 特征空间噪声大且模态融合较浅、以及解耦处理流导致跨模态语义错位等问题，论文旨在提升细粒度缺陷定位和跨模态语义一致性。

Result: 在 MVTec 3D-AD 和 Eyecandies 基准上，于 one-vs-rest 和跨数据集零样本设置下取得 state-of-the-art 性能，像素级 AUPRO 最高超过最强对比方法 2.9%。

Insight: 创新点在于显式引入局部几何结构和视觉空间上下文来适配冻结 CLIP：GSA 通过密度自适应邻域加权细化反投影三维点特征，CSA 用轻量可训练适配器平滑多尺度二维 patch 嵌入，DSPC 则在双流之间对齐正常与异常概念，从而缓解几何信息损失、空间噪声和跨模态语义漂移。

Abstract: Zero-Shot Multimodal Anomaly Detection (ZSMAD) aims to identify both appearance and geometric defects in novel categories without relying on target-specific training data, making it especially valuable in real-world scenarios constrained by data scarcity, privacy concerns, or high annotation costs. While recent approaches successfully adapt vision-language models like CLIP to achieve this open-vocabulary generalization, they still face significant challenges: (i) severe geometric information loss during 3D-to-2D point cloud rendering; (ii) spatially noisy 2D feature maps produced by raw CLIP patch embeddings, compounded by shallow late-stage modality fusion; and (iii) severe cross-modal semantic misalignment due to decoupled processing streams. To address these limitations, we propose AggCLIP, a novel framework that adapts frozen CLIP representations for ZSMAD by explicitly incorporating local geometric structure and visual spatial context. Specifically, to accurately localize fine-grained defects, we design a Geometry-Aware Spatial Aggregation (GSA) module that refines back-projected 3D point features via density-adaptive neighborhood weighting, alongside a Context-Aware Spatial Aggregation (CSA) module that smooths multi-scale 2D patch embeddings using a lightweight trainable adapter. Furthermore, to prevent semantic drift between modalities, we introduce a Dual-Stream Prompt Contrastive (DSPC) loss, which aligns the abstract concepts of normality and abnormality across the point cloud and RGB streams. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed AggCLIP framework achieves state-of-the-art performance on the MVTec 3D-AD and Eyecandies benchmarks under both one-vs-rest and cross-dataset zero-shot settings, notably surpassing the strongest competitive methods by up to 2.9% in pixel-level AUPRO.

[120] DrawScore: Evaluating Generated CAD Models against Engineering Drawings cs.CV | cs.SEPDF

Haorui Chen, Shiteng Lu, Zhanyu Guo, Zaixi Li, Yunhe Ni

TL;DR: 论文指出现有CAD生成评估指标（体素IoU、Chamfer距离、表面F-score）对孔位错误、槽面错误等局部制造关键缺陷不敏感，并从统一积分形式分析其局部误差权重随尺寸高阶衰减。为此提出DrawScore：将CAD模型投影为带可见线和隐藏线的三个正交工程视图，并沿投影曲线施加饱和惩罚，使局部误差权重仅线性衰减。实验验证了衰减阶预测，并显示DrawScore能揭示IoU近完美结果中的错误，还可作为强化学习奖励和无需3D真值的测试时验证器。

Details

Motivation: 现有CAD生成评估指标主要衡量整体体积或表面相似性，几乎不惩罚仅占小部分体积/表面积但对制造至关重要的局部错误，如孔位置错误或槽位于错误面。论文旨在设计更符合工程制造语义、对局部错误敏感的CAD生成评价指标。

Result: 受控实验确认了论文预测的局部误差衰减阶；在现有CAD生成智能体输出上，DrawScore能暴露被IoU评为近乎完美的输出中的错误。DrawScore还可作为强化学习奖励改进生成，并在工程图到CAD生成中作为测试时验证器，无需3D真值或额外奖励模型。

Insight: 创新点在于从统一积分形式理论分析现有指标的局部误差衰减问题，并利用工程图式三视图投影与沿投影曲线的饱和惩罚，将局部误差权重从高阶衰减降为线性衰减。客观来看，其可借鉴之处是将评估表示与制造语义对齐，并让指标同时可作为强化学习奖励和无需3D真值的测试时验证器。

Abstract: Automatic CAD generation is commonly evaluated with volumetric IoU, Chamfer Distance, and surface F-score, which barely penalize local errors that occupy only a small fraction of a part’s volume or surface area, such as a misplaced hole or a slot on the wrong face, even though such errors are critical to manufacturing. We formalize these metrics in a unified integral form and show that the weight of a local error decays as the $(k+q)$-th power of its size, where $k$ is the dimension of the integration domain and $q$ is determined by the penalty function. Guided by this analysis, we propose DrawScore, which projects both models into three orthographic views with visible and hidden lines, as in engineering drawings, and applies a saturating penalty along the projected curves, so that the weight of a local error decays only linearly. Controlled experiments confirm the predicted decay orders, and on outputs of existing CAD generation agents, DrawScore exposes errors in outputs that IoU rates as nearly perfect. DrawScore further improves generation as a reinforcement learning reward and, for CAD generation from engineering drawings, serves as a test-time verifier without 3D ground truth or an additional reward model. Our code is available at https://github.com/UESTC-Simon/DrawScore.

Eerik Alamikkotervo, Risto Ojala

Abstract: Semantic 3D mapping is a powerful representation that enables platform agnostic mobile robot navigation in challenging offroad conditions. Current vision foundation models can accurately predict semantics zero-shot on structured scenes but performance is compromised in natural environments. On the other hand, 3D point clouds encode valuable information for discriminating semantics in scenarios where vision foundation models fail. In this work, we propose fitting a sphericity-elevation distribution for each semantic class to act as geometric anchor. Only the points with the most confident vision foundation model predictions are considered when fitting to produce high quality geometric prior distributions. The geometric prior is fused with the vision foundation model prediction to produce the final semantic mapping. The proposed prior improves SAM3 baseline by +6.3 in mIoU and +8.9 in accuracy on the RELLIS-3D dataset, and by +1.7 in mIoU and +2.1 in accuracy on the WildScenes dataset. Details

Motivation: 现有音视频基准多以独立文本给出问题，交互式基准又往往整体打分，难以判断模型是否能识别用户何时在求助，以及识别后回复是否真正满足需求。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models (omni-modal LLMs) can respond to spoken requests directly through voice and video calls. Such requests rely on the scene shared by the user and the assistant, so the same utterance may call for different help or for no response. Most audio-visual benchmarks provide questions as separate text input, and interactive benchmarks mostly score a response as a whole. It thus remains unclear whether a model recognizes when the user is asking for help, and whether its reply meets the user’s need once the request is recognized. We propose O-IN-Bench to evaluate how well omni-modal LLMs understand spoken intentions. A model watches a complete video with native speech and outputs one text response, or [SILENCE] when no response is needed. O-IN-Bench contains 1,000 Chinese and English samples in five categories and 16 subcategories. Contrastive pairs combine the same request with different scene conditions to test whether the response changes with the context. A sample-specific rubric judges language, intent, and content separately, and O-IN-Engine verifies each rubric against the video. Across 30 omni-modal model configurations, the best overall score is 59.6, achieved by Gemini3.7-Flash. Gemini3.7-Flash passes 74.4% of the intent judgments but only 42.6% of the content judgments. When the speech is a conversation between peers rather than a request to the assistant, even strong models often keep responding. When a reply must satisfy several people, the best score is only 25.9. These results indicate that recognizing a request and meeting its requirements are separate abilities, and O-IN-Bench measures each of them.

Details

Motivation: 现有基于视觉语言模型的端到端自动驾驶计算开销大，且无法在运行时开关语言推理而不损害闭环性能，阻碍实际部署；论文旨在实现轻量、可灵活启用或禁用推理的端到端驾驶方案。

Result: DRIFT仅255M参数，在Bench2Drive上最高达到DS 97.03、成功率89.39%（三次运行均值），与最强非语言模型相当且远小于现有VLM模型；在NAVSIM和AlpaSim真实世界基准上也有竞争力，计算成本显著更低。

Abstract: The integration of vision-language models (VLMs) into end-to-end (E2E) autonomous driving has demonstrated strong potential by providing scene description and reasoning capabilities. However, VLMs are computationally expensive, and switching language reasoning on and off at runtime without hurting closed-loop performance has not been demonstrated, which hinders practical adoption. To address these issues, we propose DRIFT, an E2E autonomous driving framework that integrates a lightweight language model (LM) for visual question answering and action commentary. By efficiently projecting intermediate driving representations into the language embedding space, we demonstrate that a compact LM (<150M parameters) can achieve strong scene description and action commentary. Furthermore, we introduce a runtime-flexible planning module that is trained on surrogate reasoning features distilled from the LM and can be conditioned on either the LM or the surrogate reasoning features at inference. This design enables bypassing the LM entirely when required by deployment constraints, without sacrificing driving performance. With only 255M parameters, DRIFT reaches up to a Driving Score (DS) of 97.03 and a Success Rate of 89.39% (mean over three runs) on Bench2Drive by interleaving its LM and surrogate reasoning at a chosen reasoning rate. This is on par with the strongest non-language models while being far more compact than existing VLM-based models. DRIFT further achieves competitive results on the real-world NAVSIM and AlpaSim benchmarks at significantly lower computational cost than existing VLM-based approaches. Project page: https://drift-e2e.github.io/

TL;DR: 论文提出 HandWorldBench，一个面向以手为中心的世界模型的统一评测基准，覆盖人手、仿真灵巧手和真实灵巧手三类形态。该基准从六个维度系统评估手物交互世界模型，并基于超过600小时视频构建了20,500个高质量第一人称手物交互片段，人工筛选500个覆盖七类任务的片段用于评测。论文在统一协议下评测11个模型，揭示了手部姿态控制与物体状态演化、跨具身泛化之间的不一致性。

Details

Motivation: 现有世界模型评测缺乏针对手物交互的系统性基准，而手部动作如何改变物体以及物体状态如何随时间保持，是具身任务中的关键能力。论文旨在填补这一空白，建立统一、多具身形态、多维度的手中心世界模型评测体系。

Result: 论文构建了包含20,500个高质量第一人称片段的数据集，并人工选取500个覆盖七类任务的片段进行评测。在统一协议下评测11个模型后发现：更强的手部姿态控制并不稳定带来一致的物体状态演化与保持能力，也不保证稳健的跨具身性能；部分专用手部控制模型在灵巧手任务上甚至不如通用文本/图像到视频生成模型。

Insight: 主要创新在于提出统一的手中心世界模型评测基准，覆盖人手、仿真灵巧手和真实灵巧手，并从视觉质量、手部保真度、外观一致性、指令遵循、物理一致性和时序连贯性六个维度评估。其客观价值在于通过数据整理流程和统一评测协议，揭示了手部控制能力与物体状态建模、跨具身泛化之间的差距，为后续世界模型研究提供了诊断性基准。

Abstract: Recent advances in world models are opening new possibilities for fine-grained embodied tasks, with hand-object interaction offering a particularly demanding setting. Modeling these interactions involves capturing how hand actions change objects and how the resulting states persist over time. Yet systematic evaluation tailored to these hand-centric capabilities remains underdeveloped. To address this gap, we introduce HandWorldBench, a unified benchmark that systematically evaluates hand-centric world models across human hands, simulated dexterous hands, and real dexterous hands along six dimensions: visual quality, hand fidelity, appearance consistency, instruction following, physical consistency, and temporal coherence. To construct the benchmark, we develop a data curation pipeline that extracts hand-object interaction clips and screens them for quality, yielding 20,500 high-quality egocentric clips from over 600 hours of footage. We then manually select 500 clips covering seven task families for evaluation. Benchmarking eleven models under a unified protocol reveals that stronger hand-pose control does not consistently translate into coherent object-state evolution and persistence or robust cross-embodiment performance, with several specialized hand-control models underperforming general-purpose text/image-to-video generators on dexterous-hand tasks.

[125] Jeveration: System One Models Now Generate cs.CVPDF

Yuhe Liu, Qi Li, Shihua Zhang, Xinchao Wang

TL;DR: 论文提出 Jeveration，将 System One 模型的快速并行决策范式扩展到图像生成：基于冻结视觉语言模型，在单次前向中生成高质量图像，并统一文生图、图生图和密集预测三类任务。方法通过共享随机状态协调并行预测以保持全局一致性，并先用离散图像码分布引导单步生成。实验显示其生成 512x512 图像仅需 78 ms，较现有统一模型最高快 122 倍。

Details

Motivation: 现有 System One 模型擅长快速并行决策，但如何用同一范式进行高质量、全局一致的图像生成仍是问题；单提示可对应多张有效图像，而并行预测需收敛为连贯整体。

Result: 在 512x512 图像生成上达到 78 ms，较现有统一模型最高快 122 倍，同时保持有竞争力的图像质量和密集预测性能；一个 checkpoint 支持文生图、图生图和密集预测，且每项任务均为单次前向。

Insight: 创新点在于复用冻结视觉语言模型的视觉语义知识进行整体图像生成，用共享随机状态协调并行预测，并通过学习离散图像码分布引导单步生成；客观看，其价值在于把 System One 并行决策、单步生成和多任务统一结合起来，在效率与一致性之间取得平衡。

Abstract: System One models such as Jev return decisions through fast, parallel prediction. In this paper, we introduce Jeveration, showing that the same decision paradigm can also generate high-quality images in a single forward pass. Jeveration builds on a frozen vision-language model, repurposing its visual-semantic knowledge for holistic image generation. The key challenge is to maintain global consistency. A single prompt admits many valid images, yet parallel predictions must converge on a coherent whole. We therefore condition all positions on a shared random state to coordinate parallel predictions, so that each draw yields a different but coherent image. Since the model tends to ignore this state under direct image supervision, we first learn a distribution over discrete image codes to guide single-pass generation. Beyond text-to-image generation, we further unify image-to-image generation and dense prediction within the same architecture, so that one checkpoint supports all three tasks, each in one forward pass. Experiments show that Jeveration generates a 512x512 image in 78 ms, up to 122x faster than existing unified models, while maintaining competitive image quality and dense prediction performance.

Austin C Baker, Raiyaan Abdullah, Daniel Zeev Yoffe, Shruti Vyas, Yogesh S Rawat

TL;DR: 论文提出 A^3Bench，一个用于评估多模态大语言模型在视频中理解攻击性交互的基准，要求模型不仅识别攻击行为，还要区分施暴者、受害者和旁观者，并将动作正确绑定到参与者。作者评测了17个多模态模型，发现它们在细粒度攻击场景理解上普遍表现不佳，且随推理复杂度增加而下降。论文还探索了角色图提示策略，以促进结构化角色交互推理。

Details

Motivation: 现有暴力和犯罪视频数据集主要评估是否发生攻击，缺少对施暴者、受害者、旁观者身份及其场景关系的细粒度测试。论文旨在填补这一空白，研究模型能否在干扰条件下联合完成攻击动作识别与角色-动作关联。

Result: 在 A^3Bench 上评测17个多模态模型，发现它们在细粒度攻击场景理解上存在明显困难，常混淆施暴者、受害者和旁观者，表现出系统性的角色-动作关联失败；增大模型规模或使用通用推理模型提升有限。角色图提示策略可改善组合推理表现，但未提及具体SOTA数值。

Insight: 创新点在于构建面向攻击性交互的角色感知视频推理基准，强调施暴者/受害者/旁观者区分与动作绑定，而非粗粒度暴力检测；并提出角色图提示策略，引导模型对参与者交互进行结构化推理。

Abstract: Recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved strong performance on general video understanding benchmarks, yet their ability to reason about participant roles in aggressive interactions remains largely unexplored. Existing violence and crime datasets primarily evaluate whether aggression occurs, without testing whether models can identify who performed the action, who received it, and how participants are related within the scene. To address this gap, we introduce A$^3$Bench, a benchmark to study aggressive action and association in videos. The benchmark evaluates whether models can jointly recognize aggressive actions, distinguish aggressors from victims and bystanders, and correctly bind actions to participants under challenging distractor settings. We benchmark 17 multimodal models and find that they struggle substantially on fine-grained aggressive-scene understanding. Performance degrades consistently as reasoning complexity increases, with models frequently confusing aggressors with victims or bystanders, revealing a systematic failure in role-action association rather than coarse aggression recognition alone. We further observe that increasing model scale or generic reasoning models experience limited improvement. Finally, we explore a simple role-graph prompting strategy that encourages structured reasoning over participant interactions, leading to improvements in compositional reasoning. Our findings highlight that reliable aggressive-scene understanding remains a major challenge for current MLLMs and motivate future research on role-aware video reasoning beyond coarse violence detection. Code and dataset: https://github.com/austincbaker/A3Bench.

[127] Read Together, Answer Apart: How Earlier Questions Help Vision-Language Models Look cs.CVPDF

Xinyi Guo, Kaile Huang, Xitong Ling, Mingyi He, Chende Zheng

TL;DR: 论文研究视觉语言模型（VLM）在单张图像上一次性回答多个问题（co-asking）时的行为，通过逐题配对比较单题回答与联合提问回答，覆盖22个开源权重模型（1B-35B），揭示了联合提问的两种相反效应：较早的问题在prefill阶段把视觉信息读入自身的问题token，从而帮助后续问题把图像注意力集中到各自的目标区域；但解码阶段解码器对其他问题词干的注意力又会带来损害。据此提出免训练的“Read Together, Answer Apart”解码掩码，在保留共享prefill的同时隔离每个答案。

Details

Motivation: 现有评估只在prompt层面报告准确率增益和幻觉，结论相互矛盾，且未追踪单个答案的变化，因此需要弄清联合提问对每个答案的影响及其机制。

Result: 在DCI上，所有模型在联合提问下修复了15.4%-29.8%的单题错误，正确转错比例从弱模型的30.8%降至强模型的4.7%；该方法在DCI上使14个模型中的12个提升，最高+4.71个百分点，弥补了与单独提问差距的70%，在WebUI、FinTabNet、GQA上分别有10、8、5个模型提升，最高+11.4、+5.3、+1.1个点。

Insight: 创新在于逐题配对分析揭示联合提问的两种相反效应，并借助注意力读出与层窗口干预，把助益定位到prefill早中期层的跨问题视觉信息传递，同时发现解码阶段的问题词干干扰可通过免训练掩码消除，为多问题VLM推理提供了机制性理解与轻量改进。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are often asked several questions about the same image in a single prompt (co-asking), yet existing evaluations report conflicting accuracy gains and hallucinations at the prompt level without tracking how individual answers change. By pairing each question’s single-question and co-asked answers across 22 open-weight VLMs (1B-35B), we find that erratic net shifts reflect two opposing effects: on Densely Captioned Images (DCI), every model repairs 15.4%-29.8% of its single-question errors under co-asking, while the share of correct answers turned wrong falls from 30.8% on weaker models to 4.7% on stronger ones. Across all 14 models supporting custom attention masks, we show through attention readouts and layer-window interventions that earlier same-image questions read visual information into their question tokens in the early-to-middle layers during prefill, helping later questions concentrate image attention onto their queried targets. This visual help accumulates across the scene even across non-overlapping regions, whereas blocking earlier questions from attending to the image lowers both target localization and accuracy in all 14 models. While cross-question help is established during shared question reading, part of the co-asking damage enters later during answer decoding when the decoder attends to other question stems. We therefore introduce Read Together, Answer Apart, a training-free decoding mask that keeps shared prefill intact and isolates each answer from other stems, improving co-asking accuracy on 12 of 14 models on DCI (up to +4.71 percentage points, closing 70% of the pooled gap to asking alone) and on 10, 8, and 5 of the 14 models on WebUI, FinTabNet, and GQA (up to +11.4, +5.3, and +1.1 points).

Details

Motivation: 自监督动物行为编码器通常基于手工运动特征训练或输入这些特征，但此前缺乏公平对比来验证编码器是否真正优于这些手工特征；论文旨在回答：在控制表示维度和分类器的条件下，编码器能否超越其输入的手工特征。

Result: 在CalMS21上达到85.8 macro F1（最佳编码器82.4），MABe22小鼠三元组83.7 vs hBehaveMAE 80.3（+3.4），MABe22果蝇组50任务平均F1 51.6 vs BAMS 48.2，Shot7M2上68.8 vs hBehaveMAE 64.7；但hBehaveMAE在最短动作上仍更优，且原始关键点在部分数据集上已可匹配或超越编码器。

Abstract: Self-supervised encoders for animal behavior are often trained on, or fed, hand-built movement features. We ask whether they beat those ingredients when compared fairly. A training-free representation, made of generic per-frame measurements summarized over fixed time windows and reduced with PCA, is compared with retrained and released encoders (BAMS, hBehaveMAE) under a preregistered protocol: frozen representations of equal size and one shared classifier. On CalMS21 it reaches 85.8 macro F1 against 82.4 for the best encoder, ties with four labelled videos, and leads when the annotator changes or new behaviors must be learned from a few videos; the reading holds for encoders retrained with three seeds. On the MABe22 mouse triplets it scores 83.7 against 80.3 for the released hBehaveMAE (gap +3.4, 95% interval +2.1 to +4.7). On a second species, the MABe22 fly groups, the same recipe scores 51.6 mean F1 over 50 tasks against 48.2 for BAMS, the best published method. On Shot7M2, the synthetic basketball benchmark built by hBehaveMAE’s authors, it scores 68.8 against 64.7 for hBehaveMAE, the best method in that paper, though hBehaveMAE remains better on the shortest movements. Short time windows carry most of the result. Raw keypoints in each body’s own frame, summarized over the same windows, already match or beat the encoders on both mouse datasets and on Shot7M2; on the fly groups they fall short, and the measurements add 5.3 points. On these benchmarks, current behavior encoders have yet to beat the hand-built features they learn from, and such baselines should be reported alongside them.

Tobias Braun, Marcus Rohrbach, Lorenzo Cavallaro

TL;DR: 论文提出 V-lens，一种无需训练解码器的方法，利用统一多模态模型中图像生成器与文本理解共享表征的特性，将指定 token 的隐藏状态直接可视化为图像，从而检查模型从问题到答案之间的中间概念。作者在 4 个基于扩散和 2 个纯自回归的统一模型上验证了该方法的有效性，并提出 lens tuning：用 V-lens 生成的图像监督选定的隐藏状态。该监督能显著提升代词指代的可视化正确率，并意外地改善文本答案质量。

Details

Motivation: 模型推理的中间步骤是黑箱，难以检查；统一多模态模型在同一架构中结合理解与生成，其图像生成器天然可访问文本处理时形成的表征，论文希望利用这一连接，在无需训练额外解码器的情况下可视化隐藏状态，并进一步利用这种可视化反向改进模型。

Result: 在 4 个扩散式和 2 个纯自回归统一模型上，V-lens 无需训练就能揭示连接问题与答案的关键中间概念（即使概念未被显式提及）；在 BAGEL、SenseNova-U1、HunyuanImage-3.0 上，lens tuning 将代词指代可视化正确率从 2–11% 提升到 86–94%，并且在不直接监督答案 token 的情况下也提升了文本答案的表现。

Insight: 创新点在于零训练地复用统一模型内部的生成能力作为“探针”，以及提出用图像作为监督信号来调控隐藏状态；从客观角度看，值得注意的是视觉监督能够间接改善语言输出，这揭示了统一模型中理解与生成表征之间的紧密耦合关系，为可解释性与训练信号设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Visualizing intermediate representations could help us inspect the reasoning steps underlying a model output. Unified multimodal models offer this opportunity by combining understanding and generation in one architecture. Their image generators can access representations formed while processing text. We introduce the V-lens, which uses this connection to visualize a chosen token’s hidden state without training a decoder. Across four diffusion-based and two purely autoregressive unified models, the resulting images reveal key intermediate concepts that connect a question to its answer even when the concepts are never named explicitly. Controlled tests show how this content changes with context, token position, and depth. We then introduce lens tuning, which supervises selected hidden states with pictures through the V-lens. Across BAGEL, SenseNova-U1, and HunyuanImage-3.0, the rate of correct visualizations of pronoun referents rises from 2-11% to 86-94%. Surprisingly, such visual supervision also improves text answers without supervising the answer tokens directly. Code is available at https://github.com/multimodal-ai-lab/V-lens.

[131] ReCoCo: From Patches to Compositions for Visual Representation Learning cs.CVPDF

Juan Gutiérrez, Saman Zadtootaghaj, José Luis Blanco-Murillo, Sara Atito

TL;DR: 论文提出 ReCoCo（Regional Composition Consistency），将自监督学习中的跨视图一致性从图像级和 patch 级扩展到区域级。它通过在学习到的视觉原型上组合 patch 级预测来表示增强视图间共享的内容，并对这些组合进行对齐，从而无需显式 patch 对应或语义分组即可学习稠密视觉表示。方法可自然融入 teacher-student 自蒸馏框架，且不引入额外可学习参数。

Details

Motivation: 现有自监督方法在稠密特征学习上仍具挑战：patch 级匹配易引入对应不确定性，区域级方法又依赖视觉内容如何分组的假设。论文希望在不估计 patch 对应、也不预设语义分组的情况下，将跨视图一致性扩展到区域级。

Result: 实验表明 ReCoCo 在冻结特征的分割、聚类和区域检索任务上取得显著提升，同时在图像级识别上保持有竞争力表现，缓解了稠密表示与全局表示质量之间的权衡。

Insight: 创新点在于用 patch 级预测在视觉原型上的组合来表达跨视图的区域一致性，避免显式对应和语义分组，并可无缝集成到 teacher-student 自蒸馏中且不增加可学习参数。客观上，其价值在于提供了一种更鲁棒的稠密自监督信号构造方式。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning has produced powerful visual representations, yet learning strong dense features remains challenging. While consistency between augmented views provides strong image-level supervision, extending this principle to local visual content is less straightforward. Existing approaches often match individual patches across views or identify coherent regions, introducing uncertainties in correspondence estimation and assumptions about how visual content should be grouped. We introduce ReCoCo (Regional Composition Consistency), a principled approach that extends cross-view consistency to the regional level without requiring such correspondences or semantic grouping. ReCoCo represents the content shared between augmented views through compositions of their patch-level predictions over learned visual prototypes. By aligning these compositions, the model learns from regions containing multiple visual concepts without requiring them to be separated beforehand. ReCoCo integrates naturally with teacher-student self-distillation and introduces no additional learned parameters. Extensive experiments demonstrate substantial improvements in frozen-feature segmentation, clustering, and regional retrieval while maintaining competitive image-level recognition, alleviating the trade-off between dense and global representation quality.

TL;DR: 论文研究动作条件语义预测作为自动驾驶世界模型时，预测是否真正依赖自车动作。作者比较特征级和输入级动作条件模型与无动作输入模型，并通过测试时动作干预和标准分割指标量化动作依赖性，在合成环境、Cityscapes和BDD100K上验证。结果表明，执行动作带来的准确率提升有限，但错误动作会导致显著性能下降，且真实数据上特征级条件比输入级条件更依赖动作。

Details

Motivation: 现有自动驾驶世界模型评估主要关注预测保真度和规划性能，较少验证预测是否真正依赖条件动作，难以判断模型是否有效利用动作信息。论文旨在系统评估动作条件语义预测中的动作依赖性。

Result: 执行动作仅使中期mIoU最多提升2.5点（Cityscapes）和0.6点（BDD100K），而错误动作最多降低23.2点和7.0点，甚至低于无动作输入模型。真实数据上，特征级条件在相似准确率下对动作的依赖约为输入级的1.6倍；合成环境中两者依赖相同。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于提出基于测试时动作干预和标准分割指标的动作依赖性评估协议，并设置无动作输入对照，区分准确率增益与动作依赖强度。客观来看，该工作揭示了不同动作条件方式和数据环境下世界模型利用动作信息的差异，为动作条件世界模型提供了可借鉴的评估方法。

Abstract: World models are increasingly used in autonomous driving to predict how a scene evolves under the ego vehicle’s actions, supporting counterfactual reasoning and model-based planning. However, existing evaluations focus on prediction fidelity and planning performance, and rarely verify that the predictions depend on the conditioning action. In this paper, we study action dependence in action-conditioned semantic forecasting, which predicts future semantic segmentation from past segmentations and the ego action. We compare a forecaster with feature-wise and input-level action conditioning against an identical model without action input, and measure action dependence with test-time action interventions scored by standard segmentation metrics. The evaluation is validated in a synthetic environment with known action-dependent dynamics and applied to Cityscapes and BDD100K, both within each dataset and across the two vehicle platforms. Experiments show that the executed action improves mid-term mIoU by at most 2.5 points on Cityscapes and 0.6 points on BDD100K, whereas a wrong action reduces it by up to 23.2 and 7.0 points, below the model without action input. On real data, feature-wise conditioning depends 1.6 times more on the action than input-level conditioning at similar accuracy, whereas both depend on it equally in the synthetic environment, where the history does not determine the action. These results show that an accuracy gain indicates that a driving world model uses its action, but not how strongly its forecasts depend on it. Code is available at https://github.com/binayksingh/does-action-matter.

Abstract: Human communication involves highly coordinated multimodal signals such as speech and co-speech gestures. Existing approaches for generating both modalities commonly follow a cascaded pipeline, where speech is generated first and subsequently used to drive gesture generation; speech generation is therefore independent of gestures. We introduce Gaspard, a unified speech–gesture generation model based on continuous autoregressive generation with next-step flow-matching predictions, and trained on Seamless Interaction, a large-scale multimodal dataset. The heterogeneous temporal properties of speech and gesture motivate modality-specific prediction heads and decoupled classifier-free guidance over the two generation streams. This guidance rebalances the influence of speech and gesture at inference time, improving expressivity while mitigating dynamics collapse in continuous autoregressive gesture generation. Details

Motivation: 低比特量化对资源受限视觉部署很重要，但压缩到4比特以下常导致精度崩溃；现有混合精度恢复策略缺乏对崩溃位置和恢复边界的机制性理解。

Result: 在ResNet和EfficientNet等标准CNN的ImageNet-1k 5万张验证集上，仅保留不到5%的关键层激活为8比特即可恢复最多92%的精度损失；但在ARM/x86 CPU上，运行时比特解包会带来1.48倍到21.5倍执行减速，取决于batch size。

Insight: 创新点在于系统刻画sub-4-bit量化崩溃的非线性进展并定位关键层，提出选择性INT4/INT8与浮点尺度的多步恢复瀑布，同时结合真实CPU部署评估，指出仅看存储压缩不足，还需关注解包开销和硬件吞吐。

Abstract: Deep vision models operating in resource-constrained environments depend heavily on low-bit quantization, yet compressing weights below 4 bits frequently induces catastrophic accuracy collapse. While mixed-precision recovery strategies aim to rescue accuracy by selectively retaining high-precision parameters, the fundamental mechanics of where and why these models collapse remain poorly understood. In this study, we systematically analyze the anatomy of quantization collapse across standard Vision CNNs (including ResNet and EfficientNet backbones) evaluated on the full 50,000-image ImageNet-1k validation set. First, we map the exact progression of degradation, demonstrating that collapse is highly non-linear and concentrated in late-stage pointwise projections and depthwise layers. Second, we introduce a structured, multi-step recovery waterfall that evaluates selective integer precision (INT4/INT8) and selective floating-point scales. We show that keeping less than 5% of critical layer activations in 8-bit precision recovers up to 92% of the accuracy lost during aggressive sub-4-bit compression. Finally, we bridge the hardware reality gap by evaluating deployment performance on standard ARM and x86 CPU architectures. We show that while low-bit quantization reduces memory storage requirements, standard runtime software bit-unpacking introduces a 1.48x to 21.5x execution slowdown depending on batch size, highlighting a critical trade-off between model footprint and actual edge throughput. Our findings provide a practical blueprint for navigating the limits of sub-4-bit deployment without sacrificing hardware performance.

[140] SpecLook: Speculating Where to Look for Fast and Reliable Agentic MLLMs cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Xi Ding, Jiawei Zhang

TL;DR: 论文针对agentic多模态大模型（MLLM）”用图像思考”时串行循环慢、答案无可靠性评分的问题，提出免训练的”推测-验证”框架SpecLook：不再推测答案，而是推测agent的下一观测（glimpse），由小模型草拟观测并重新作答，先校验答案是否稳定且置信，再让大模型无工具地验证并纠正观测，最后融合答案；仍缺证据的问题才进入一次前向，SpecLook-Fast则可跳过此步，使任何问题都不必跑循环。

Details

Motivation: 现有agentic MLLM的zoom-in工具调用构成串行循环，速度慢且答案没有可靠性分数；此前对token、推理步或答案的推测仍保留循环，而答案推测还会接受”自信的错误”。作者发现循环大多只多加一步zoom-in，小模型在多数split上已领先agent。

Result: 在V*、HR-Bench、POPE上每个split均优于所有基线；SpecLook平均准确率87.2%和86.2%，相对DeepEyes-7B和Thyme-RL分别加速7.7倍和4.7倍，SpecLook-Fast加速9.9倍和6.3倍。

Insight: 创新在于把推测对象从答案换成”可验证、可纠正、并可用于检验答案”的下一观测，并设计小模型草拟→大模型无工具校验纠正→答案融合→必要时单次前向的三级免训练框架；此外trace可在无标签下为每个答案给出可靠性评分，SpecLook-Fast进一步跳过一次前向。

Abstract: Agentic multimodal large language models (MLLMs) that think with images interleave reasoning with zoom-in tool calls. However, this sequential loop is slow, and its answers carry no reliability score. Prior speculation of tokens, reasoning steps, or answers keeps the loop, and answer speculation also accepts confident errors. We find that the loop mostly adds one zoom-in step, with which a small model is ahead of the agent on most splits. Our key insight is to speculate the agent’s next observation, which, unlike a drafted answer, can be verified, corrected, and used to test the answer. We propose SpecLook, a training-free speculate-and-verify framework with three levels. A small model drafts a glimpse and re-answers from it, and the question ends if the answer is unchanged and confident. Otherwise, the agent’s large model verifies and corrects the glimpse without tools, and their answers are fused. Only questions that still lack evidence reach the agent, in one forward pass that SpecLook-Fast skips, so no question runs the loop. The trace scores every answer’s reliability without labels. Extensive experiments on V*, HR-Bench, and POPE show that both are more accurate than every baseline on every split. SpecLook reaches 87.2% and 86.2% mean accuracy with 7.7x and 4.7x speedups over the DeepEyes-7B and Thyme-RL agents, and SpecLook-Fast reaches 9.9x and 6.3x.

[141] BOS Summary: The First Token Matters cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.CVPDF

Hui Zhu, Saleh Zare Zade, Rafi Ibn Sultan, Deng Pan, Arun Ross

TL;DR: 论文指出大型视觉语言模型和语言模型中BOS token常作为注意力汇接收过多注意力，但在因果掩码下其表示不携带输入信息。提出BOS Summary，一种免训练的推理时方法，在预填充阶段仅对单个Transformer层的BOS查询行放松因果约束，使其聚合全部视觉和文本输入token形成上下文表示，且不增加参数、模块或解码开销。实验表明该方法在多个基准上提升性能并优于近期sink干预方法。

Details

Motivation: 现有模型存在注意力汇现象，BOS token等位置获得过多注意力，但标准因果掩码下BOS查询只关注自身，导致后续token反复访问的表示缺乏输入信息，浪费了持续注意力路径。论文旨在重新利用这一路径，让BOS携带输入上下文以提升模型推理能力。

Result: 在多个VLM和LLM家族及八个多模态和纯文本基准上，BOS Summary优于直接提示和思维链提示，并在多步推理任务上超越近期sink干预方法。机制分析显示该方法削弱了BOS的汇主导地位，将注意力重新分配给承载内容的提示token。

Insight: 创新点在于免训练、推理时仅修改单个Transformer层中BOS查询行的因果掩码，使BOS聚合全局输入信息，无额外参数或解码开销；同时揭示改变汇携带的内容能重塑后续层的注意力分配，为利用注意力汇提供简单有效的思路。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) and language models (LLMs) often exhibit attention sinks, whereby particular positions, such as the beginning-of-sequence (BOS) token, receive a disproportionate share of attention. Under standard causal masking, however, the BOS query attends only to itself, so the representation that later tokens repeatedly access carries no information about the input. We introduce BOS Summary, a training-free inference-time method that repurposes this persistent pathway to carry input context. At a single transformer layer during prefill, BOS Summary relaxes the causal constraint for the BOS query row alone, letting BOS aggregate all visual and textual input tokens into a self-derived context representation while every other token retains standard causal attention. The enriched representation then remains accessible to the model throughout decoding, with no additional parameters, auxiliary modules, or decoding-time overhead. Across multiple VLM and LLM families and eight multimodal and text-only benchmarks, BOS Summary improves over direct and Chain-of-Thought prompting and outperforms recent sink-intervention methods on multi-step reasoning tasks. A mechanistic analysis shows that making BOS input-specific also weakens its sink dominance and redistributes attention toward content-bearing prompt tokens, indicating that changing what a sink carries reshapes how later layers allocate attention.

Details

Motivation: 现有自动化智能体设计多依赖聚合适应度引导进化搜索，掩盖了实例级成功与失败信息，难以精细指导架构重组。论文旨在利用执行级证据提升LLM智能体架构搜索的效果。

Result: 在六个推理与编程设置上，使用开放权重优化器和执行器模型，GRAFT取得82.0%的平均测试准确率，超过最强基线9.8个百分点。受控消融表明，在初始化、搜索规则、修复策略和评估预算固定时，加入执行证据仍能提升测试准确率。

Insight: 创新点在于将父本特定执行证据用于语义交叉与集成变异，并复用评估阶段的有界成功/失败样本作为反馈，避免额外执行和参考答案泄露。客观上，该方法把粗粒度适应度选择与细粒度执行反馈结合，为进化式智能体设计提供了更有效的搜索信号。

Abstract: Automated agent design searches for executable architectures that improve LLM-based agent performance, with evolutionary methods using language-model crossover to recombine promising mechanisms for task decomposition, model coordination, and verification. This search, however, is typically guided by aggregate fitness alone, which hides the instance-level successes and failures behind each score. We introduce Execution Guided Genetic Recombination for Agent Design (GRAFT), which uses aggregate fitness for selection and survival and parent-specific execution evidence to guide semantic crossover with integrated mutation. Its feedback harness reuses bounded samples of successful and failed runs from fitness evaluation, withholding reference answers and requiring no additional task executions. We evaluate GRAFT across six reasoning and programming settings using open-weight optimizer and executor models. It achieves a mean test accuracy of 82.0%, exceeding the strongest baseline by 9.8% points. Controlled ablations show that adding execution evidence improves test accuracy with initialization, search rules, repair policies, and evaluation budgets held fixed. Our results support the value of parent-specific execution evidence for evolutionary agent design.

Abstract: Can AI agents design robots that work in the real world? Simulation alone is not enough: under public part data, none of 15 initial passing designs crossed the real course, so an agent learns the simulator from measurements it requests and videos of real runs. Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) can run on commodity hardware and support privacy-preserving, on-premises clinical deployment, but their medical explanations are often short and poorly structured compared with large language models. Xingyang Li, Tianlang Zhao, Song Han, Ligeng Zhu

Details

Motivation: PTQ 的最优配方因模型和精度而异，专家需为每个新模型重新设计，耗时且常次优；直接用 LLM 智能体迭代设计又面临单次评估需数十 GPU 小时、跨代码库方法移植困难等问题。

Result: 在四个推理模型上，QDA 于 W4A16、W4A8、W3A16 分别比最强公开配方平均提升 0.9、1.4、2.2 个百分点，每次搜索用 64 GPU 小时；在 320B MoE 模型 GLM-5.3-Flash 的 NVFP4 W4A4 上恢复 FP8 模型 98.3% 的准确率，优于最佳厂商检查点的 96.9%，且所有检查点可直接运行于现有服务内核、无推理开销。

Insight: 创新点在于用智能体闭环搜索每模型/精度的量化配方，并以未见推理轨迹上的输出 KL 散度做分钟级快速评分，再用长生成检查捕捉重复循环等失败；同时提供相互兼容且适配新架构的公共 PTQ 方法库，兼顾自动化、评估效率和部署可用性。

Abstract: Post-training quantization is the standard way to serve large language models at low cost. However, the best quantization recipe differs across models and precisions. As a result, experts must redesign it for every new model, which is time-consuming and often sub-optimal. Fortunately, an LLM agent can take over this design by refining the recipe over rounds of building and evaluating candidates, so that more rounds lead to better recipes. However, evaluating one quantized reasoning model takes tens of GPU-hours, and combining methods from different codebases means porting each to the new architecture by hand. We present QDA, a system in which an agent designs the recipe for each model and precision. To make evaluation fast, QDA scores each candidate in minutes by its output KL divergence from the original model on reasoning traces that the agent never sees, and checks promising ones with long generations to catch failures such as repetition loops. To make building fast, QDA provides a library of public PTQ methods that are compatible with one another and with new model architectures. Across four reasoning models, QDA improves accuracy over the strongest public recipe by 0.9, 1.4 and 2.2 percentage points on average at W4A16, W4A8 and W3A16, using 64 GPU-hours per search. On the 320B-parameter MoE model GLM-5.3-Flash at NVFP4 W4A4, it recovers 98.3% of the FP8 model’s accuracy, against 96.9% for the best vendor checkpoint. Every QDA checkpoint also runs on existing serving kernels with no inference overhead. We will release the library, the evaluator and the harness.

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is a powerful paradigm for improving language-model agents on long-horizon tasks, yet its conventional formulation relies on teacher logits that text-only black- box models do not expose. Details

Motivation: 现有 latent reasoner 通常使用预设的固定 latent horizon 进行推理，但论文发现更长 horizon 并不必然带来更好推理效果，甚至可能损害性能，因此需要重新思考固定 horizon latent inference 是否合理。

Result: 在多个代表性 latent CoT 推理器和多个算术基准上，该方法将推理准确率最多提升 7.2 个百分点，同时减少最多 29.0% 的 latent 计算量，且无需重新训练 LLM。

Abstract: Existing latent reasoners typically run for a preset latent horizon during inference. Intuitively, a longer horizon should provide more opportunity for reasoning. However, in this work, we find that extending latent Chain-of-Thought (CoT) to the full preset horizon may in fact degrade reasoning performance. We observe that latent CoT evolves through distinct functional stages, with earlier computation forming frontier reasoning states, while later computation mainly reorganizes existing candidates and can weaken gold candidate support. These findings motivate a rethink of fixed-horizon latent inference. To address this, we characterize the reasoning benefits of latent CoT by realized and remaining constructive progress. Empirically, we find that higher realized constructive progress corresponds to higher current reasoning accuracy, while more remaining progress corresponds to larger future gains from continued latent CoT. To this end, we introduce a lightweight method that estimates the remaining reasoning progress and adaptively adjusts the latent horizon during inference. Details

Abstract: Recent studies report that video diffusion models “know more than they show”: physical properties decode better from their activations than from their inputs or outputs. But a diffusion model computes its clean-sample estimate from those same activations, so such a comparison measures how easily a readout extracts the information. Whether the model hides anything is a separate question. We set out to find hidden physical knowledge in kinematic text-to-motion diffusion models, where the physics of every intermediate estimate can be computed exactly, and recorded every layer along the sampling trajectories of MDM (two checkpoints) and MotionDiffuse. Against the usual baselines the layers do look ahead of the model’s own estimate on balance and flight. With every readout tuned the same way, the estimate closes the gap: for balance and flight, adding a layer gains at most 0.01, a size that a positive control shows we would detect. The fall rate of jumps is the one clear exception. Early in denoising, MDM’s layers add about 0.08 in $R^2$ (up to 0.11 in a refit) that the estimate does not yet show, and the gain fades as sampling proceeds; on MotionDiffuse it is small and not robust. The estimate also flags floating samples without training, but aborting them saves under a tenth of the compute. In a video model, plausibility that linear probes miss in Wan 2.1’s clean latent becomes readable after one fixed nonlinearity.

[149] Transferring an analysis of human brain organization to language models cs.LG | cs.CLPDF

Donghyeok Lee, Sangwon Yu, Sangkwon Park, Hye Youn Park, Munyoung Chang

TL;DR: 论文将用于刻画人脑皮层组织的扩散映射嵌入方法迁移到语言模型分析中，比较基础模型与推理训练模型，发现推理能力提升伴随局部连续层区域的表征重组。将这些区域的权重移植到指令微调模型中，可平均提升数学推理表现，并对宿主原有数学能力干扰较低。

Details

Motivation: 论文关注语言模型推理能力提升是否伴随类似人脑皮层功能系统分化的区域重组，并试图定位可迁移的推理相关区域。

Result: 在三个模型家族中，梯度剖面大体保留但出现局部连续层变化；区域权重移植平均提升数学推理，重叠越高增益越大，且比非该区域移植保留更多已解问题；中间训练阶段移植也有帮助，并在医学模型中找到可迁移区域。

Insight: 创新点在于跨学科借鉴脑组织分析中的扩散映射嵌入来研究语言模型层间组织，识别连续层“推理区域”，并通过权重嫁接验证其可迁移性和低干扰性。

Abstract: During human cortical development, functional systems become increasingly differentiated. We asked whether improved reasoning in language models involves a comparable regional reorganization. Applying diffusion-map embedding, used to characterize cortical organization, to matched base and reasoning-trained models from three families, we found broadly preserved gradient profiles with localized changes within a contiguous layer span. Grafting weights from these spans into instruction-tuned hosts improved mathematical reasoning on average, with gains rising with region overlap, while retaining more previously solved problems than grafts elsewhere. Across training, the span formed gradually during the early rise in performance, and grafts from intermediate stages helped. Grafts shifted most hosts partly towards their donors’ behaviour and sometimes improved on problems the donors failed; the same analysis located a transferable span in a medical model. Together, these findings identify a reasoning-related region whose weights improve reasoning with low interference in the host’s existing mathematical competence.

[150] Openhagen Worlds: Calibrated Environments for Measuring and Training Scientific Discovery Agents cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Alexander Boesgaard Lorup

TL;DR: 论文提出 Openhagen Worlds，一套包含 27 个程序化生成环境、178 个难度等级的基准与训练套件，用于测量和训练科学发现智能体。智能体需在预算内设计实验，并提交答案，由确定性验证器对照隐藏真值检查；每个观测都明确给出完整评分规则。该套件分为面向小模型训练的 92 个核心等级和包含多阶段实验战役的 86 个前沿等级。

Details

Motivation: 现有科学发现基准难以把“设计信息量高的实验并从结果推理”的能力与记忆、未明示评分规则或捷径策略区分开，因此需要可校准、可训练、可防止捷径的环境来更纯净地评估科学发现智能体。

Result: 在 84 个留出的前沿实例上，DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 默认推理设置（每次调用 16,384 token）解出 32 个，最大推理设置（最多 393,216 token）解出 57 个，脚本 oracle 为 79 个；八阶段战役剩余提升空间最大。On-policy expert iteration 使 9B 模型在已训练家族的核心等级成功率从 0.090 提升到 0.148，部分来自更好的动作格式，且在 GPQA Diamond 上无明显损失。

Insight: 创新点在于通过 oracle 成功率下限、非演绎策略成功率上限和捷径策略上限来校准难度等级，并明确完整评分规则、使用隐藏真值确定性验证，从而减少基准污染和捷径；同时划分核心训练层与多阶段前沿层，兼顾小模型训练和前沿能力测量。客观看，其价值主要在基准构建方法学：把实验推理能力与召回/规则不透明/捷径分离，并提供可训练的分层环境。

Abstract: Agents that do science must choose informative experiments and reason from their results, but most discovery benchmarks cannot separate that skill from recall, unstated scoring rules or shortcut policies. We introduce Openhagen Worlds, a suite of 27 procedurally generated environments (some with finite truth spaces), from classic puzzles of experimental reasoning to simulator-grounded physics and chemistry, in which an agent runs experiments under a budget and commits an answer that a deterministic verifier checks against a hidden truth. Every observation states the complete scoring rule, and each of the 178 difficulty levels is calibrated: it is admitted only if, on 64 development instances, a scripted oracle succeeds on at least 0.75, the strongest plausible non-deductive policy on at most 0.25 and each gated shortcut on at most 0.35. The 92 core levels span all 27 families and are sized to train small models; the 86 frontier rungs extend the 15 discovery worlds with harder questions and with campaigns of three, five and eight or more committed stages on one hidden system. On the same 84 withheld frontier instances, one per family and rung, DeepSeek V4.1 Flash solves 32 at its default reasoning effort with 16,384 tokens per call and 57 at its maximum effort with up to 393,216 tokens per call (oracle: 79); the headroom that remains is largest in eight-stage campaigns. On-policy expert iteration gives a 9B model modest gains: its core-tier success on trained families rises from 0.090 to 0.148, partly through better action format, with no detectable loss on GPQA Diamond. A hosted service plays the core tier’s withheld instances.

[151] Synthesizing hyperspectral Data using generative Models to train spectral Unmixing Methods for low-cost Crop Residue Cover Mapping cs.LG | cs.CV | eess.SPPDF

Ege Artan, Shuo Chen, Andrew Peng, Sammuel Aldrich Karya, Kyaw Thiha

TL;DR: 论文面向FINCH高光谱卫星在作物残茬覆盖制图中的光谱解混难题，提出一种将插值与丰度映射估计解耦的框架：先用条件生成器合成高光谱数据，再训练解混模型。作者设计了GD-Streamline和TCVAE两种生成器，以及MLP和FNO两种解混模型，并指出生成器必须严格引入光谱物理先验，否则会出现模式崩溃、幻觉和分布外解混性能。

Details

Motivation: FINCH的光谱范围缺乏标准指数，需依赖复杂数据驱动解混模型；但真实高光谱地面真值数据稀疏且昂贵，难以提供密集且分布良好的数据流形。论文希望更充分利用现有真值数据，并通过引入光谱物理先验来人工扩展数据集。

Result: 摘要未给出具体定量指标或benchmark对比结果。作者报告的核心发现是：若不在解耦框架中严格整合和引入光谱先验，生成模型会出现域/模式崩溃和幻觉，导致近似数据流形失真，最终解混性能不理想且处于分布外。

Insight: 创新点在于用中间条件数据生成器解耦插值与丰度映射估计，使解混流程能够纳入模型本身不易直接利用的光谱物理先验，并将合成数据视为总体风险的Monte Carlo近似。客观来看，论文的价值还包括系统比较两类生成器与两类解混模型，并强调生成式数据增强在高光谱解混中必须受物理约束，否则会放大分布偏移。

Abstract: The spectral range of the Field Imaging Nanosatellite for Crop residue Hyperspectral Mapping (FINCH), developed by the University of Toronto Aerospace Team’s Space System Division, poses significant challenges for hyperspectral unmixing to determine crop residue cover fractional abundances. The severe lack of standard indices necessitates the use of complex, data-driven unmixing models. Complex unmixing models require dense, well-distributed manifolds, whereas ground-truth datasets are sparse and expensive. To better leverage the information content in existing ground-truth datasets, this study presents a framework that decouples interpolation and abundance-mapping estimation via an intermediary conditional data generator, contrasting with traditional single-model unmixing pipelines. This decoupling enables the inclusion of physical prior knowledge about spectra, which is not natively accessible to unmixing models, thereby artificially expanding the dataset to serve as a Monte Carlo approximation of the population risk. To test this hypothesis, we have proposed two generator models: a 1D Conditional U-Net with Conformer Layers (GD-Streamline) and a Dual-Path Transformer Conditional Variational AutoEncoder (TCVAE), and two unmixing models: Multi-Layered Perceptrons (MLP) and Fourier Neural Operators (FNO). The results of the study indicate a need for rigorous integration and introduction of spectral priors within the scope of the proposed decoupling framework to prevent domain and mode collapse and hallucination by the generator models; otherwise, this leads to falsely approximated data manifolds, ultimately resulting in unsatisfactory and out-of-distribution unmixing performance.

[152] AdaS-ViT: Test-Time Adaptive Routing for Spiking Vision Transformers with Ultra-High Energy Efficiency cs.NE | cs.AI | cs.CVPDF

Zecheng Hao, Zihan Huang, Shenghao Xie, Jiyuan Zhang, Zhaofei Yu

TL;DR: 论文面向脉冲视觉Transformer（S-ViT）中固定、统一的脉冲发放方案带来的计算冗余问题，提出测试时自适应路由架构AdaS-ViT。其核心包括AdaE-IF、S-GSA和S-LGU，通过注意力分数将显著区域路由到细粒度脉冲模式，其余区域采用低能耗粗粒度模式，并在token和通道粒度上选择性地发放脉冲。论文还使用渐进式时空课程学习稳定脉冲精度与路由掩码的协同适配，在多种基准上实现有竞争力的性能与超高能效。

Details

Motivation: 现有S-ViT在时间、空间和通道维度上大多采用固定且统一的脉冲发放方案，导致计算冗余；论文希望能在测试时联合三个维度自适应调整脉冲发放，以在保持性能的同时显著降低能耗。

Result: 在覆盖不同数据规模和模态的多个基准上，AdaS-ViT取得有竞争力的性能；在ImageNet-1k上每张图像仅消耗1.02至2.45毫焦耳，准确率-能耗比达到32倍至72倍，进一步推进了能效边界。

Insight: 创新点在于基于每个脉冲token的注意力分数进行测试时自适应路由，将显著区域分配细粒度脉冲模式、其余区域分配粗粒度节能模式；并结合S-GSA与S-LGU实现token和通道级选择性脉冲发放，以及用渐进式时空课程学习稳定路由与脉冲精度的联合适配。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) promise event-driven and energy-efficient vision, yet existing spiking vision transformers (S-ViTs) largely confine spiking tokens to fixed and uniform firing schemes along the temporal, spatial and channel dimensions, whereas suppressing the computational redundancy thus incurred calls for adaptive firing mechanisms that operate jointly across all three axes at test time. To this end, we propose the Adaptive Efficient Integrate-and-Fire (AdaE-IF) model, which consults the attention score of each spiking token and routes salient regions to fine-grained firing patterns while assigning energy-frugal coarse patterns to the remaining regions. Building on AdaE-IF, we further introduce Spiking Gated Self-Attention (S-GSA) and Spiking Local-Gated Unit (S-LGU), which enforce selective spike emission at token and channel granularity. We integrate the aforementioned mechanisms and modules into AdaS-ViT, a test-time adaptive-routing architecture trained with a progressive spatio-temporal curriculum that stabilizes the co-adaptation of firing precision and routing masks. Extensive experiments across benchmarks spanning diverse data scales and modalities demonstrate that AdaS-ViT consistently achieves highly competitive performance. Notably, on ImageNet-1k it consumes only 1.02 to 2.45 millijoules per image and attains an accuracy-per-energy ratio of 32$\times$ to 72$\times$, operating in an economical inference regime and thereby pushing the energy-efficiency boundary further.
