[153] RageVLA: Augmentation-Trained Perception and Action for State-of-the-Art Robustness on LIBERO cs.RO | cs.CVPDF

Amrit Lal Singh, Smarak Kanjilal

TL;DR: 论文提出RageVLA，通过数据增强（对LIBERO演示进行物体移动旋转、环境迁移和指令改写）和策略架构解耦（基于冻结DINOv3特征的感知引擎输出物体列表及位置、夹爪注视编码，结合TinyCLIP嵌入，由从头训练的流匹配扩散Transformer执行动作），显著提升了视觉-语言-动作模型在LIBERO-PRO上的鲁棒性。

Details

Motivation: 现有VLA模型在LIBERO上表现饱和，但在LIBERO-PRO上遇到物体移动或新指令时性能急剧下降，作者认为问题不在于模型规模，而在于动作策略将“做什么”与训练时的物体位置、场景和指令措辞耦合在一起，因此需要解耦。

Result: RageVLA（0.21B参数）在LIBERO-PRO上取得新SOTA，得分0.72（最佳已发表模型为0.53），更大变体达0.73；在LIBERO-Object上，打乱物体位置输入时成功率从0.965降至0.215，目标物体位移28cm时成功率0.865（π0.5仅0.105）。

Insight: 创新点在于通过数据增强规则和感知-动作解耦架构（将场景表示为物体列表及位置、夹爪注视编码等结构化信息，与图像/指令嵌入分离）来切断策略对训练分布的依赖，使模型基于感知而非记忆位置行动，提升了泛化性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models look saturated on LIBERO, yet most of them fall apart on LIBERO-PRO as soon as an object moves or the instruction asks for something new. We argue that what is missing is not scale but decoupling. In these policies, what to do is tied to where things were during training, to the scene around them and to the wording of the instruction. We cut these ties in two places. In the data, LIBERO demonstrations are re-arranged by general rules: every object and fixture is moved and rotated, tasks are carried into other environments and onto other surfaces, and every instruction is paraphrased. In the policy, a perception engine on frozen DINOv3 features, trained on this augmented data, hands the action model the scene in decoupled form: a list of objects with their positions, and a compact code of what the gripper is looking at, next to image and instruction embeddings from TinyCLIP. A flow-matching diffusion transformer, trained from scratch, acts on this view. We call this combination of Robust Augmentation and Grounded Encoding RageVLA. With 0.21 billion parameters, RageVLA sets a new state of the art on LIBERO-PRO among VLA policies, scoring 0.72 against 0.53 for the best published model, and a larger variant reaches 0.73. Two further tests on LIBERO-Object show that the policy acts on what it perceives. When the object positions it is given are shuffled while the camera images stay correct, its success falls from 0.965 to 0.215. When the target object is displaced by 28 cm, it still succeeds in 0.865 of the episodes, where $π_{0.5}$, run through the same harness, succeeds in 0.105.

[154] WAM-Policy: World Action Models with On-Policy Distillation for Quadrupedal Loco-Manipulation cs.RO | cs.CVPDF

Junlin Long, Xu Deng, Yiran Wang, Yue Yang, Yang Zhao

TL;DR: 论文提出 WAM-Policy，将面向机械臂的世界动作模型（WAM）转化为无臂四足机器人的语言条件移动操作策略。方法分两阶段：先做跨形态策略适配与在线自蒸馏（OPSD）加速，再用任务特定强化学习微调教师并通过多教师在线策略蒸馏（M-OPD）合并为少步学生。最终在 QLM-Bench 和真实 Unitree Go2 Air 上验证了策略有效性。

Details

Motivation: 现有无臂四足机器人可完成三腿行走、前腿操作的移动操作，但缺乏能遵循语言指令的策略；现有 WAM 虽适合语言条件操作，却主要面向机械臂且推理采样慢，难以直接用于四足平台。

Result: 在 QLM-Bench 的 10 个仿真任务、1000 条无遥操作合成演示上，迁移策略达到 77.0%，OPSD 学生保持 76.7% 且采样快 14 倍；任务特定 RL 与 M-OPD 将性能提升至 85.0%，并将 OPSD 学生部署到真实 Unitree Go2 Air。

Insight: 创新点在于跨形态适配机械臂 WAM 到四足移动操作，并用在线自蒸馏获得少步高效学生；进一步用 RL 专业化教师和多教师在线策略蒸馏融合能力。客观看，其价值主要在将语言条件世界动作模型、跨形态迁移、策略蒸馏与 RL 结合成可部署的高效四足移动操作流程。

Abstract: Arm-free quadrupeds can perform loco-manipulation, walking on three legs while manipulating with a front leg, yet no existing policy for it follows language instructions. World action models (WAMs) have emerged as effective language-conditioned manipulation policies, but are pretrained for robot arms and sample slowly. WAM-Policy turns one WAM into a quadrupedal loco-manipulation policy via two stages. The first, Cross-Morphology Policy Adaptation and Acceleration, adapts the arm-centric WAM to quadrupeds and distills the transferred policy into a student with one video and two action steps by on-policy self-distillation (OPSD). This student, however, is bounded by its teacher, which only imitates demonstrations. The second stage, Task-Specific Improvement and Merging, therefore tunes the teacher by reinforcement learning (RL) and merges the slow specialists into one few-step student by multi-teacher on-policy distillation (M-OPD). On QLM-Bench, ten simulated tasks with 1,000 demonstrations synthesized without teleoperation, the transferred policy reaches 77.0%, and the OPSD student keeps 76.7% while sampling 14 times faster. Task-specific RL and M-OPD lift it to 85.0%. We also deploy the OPSD student on a real Unitree Go2 Air. WAM-Policy thus achieves a stronger quadrupedal policy at a fraction of the inference cost.

[155] Meaning Reaches Object Choice but Not Completion: Where Is a VLA Vulnerable to Landmark Synonyms? cs.RO | cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Ryoichi Kurihara, Kota Sunamoto

TL;DR: 论文研究 VLA 策略对指令改写的脆弱性，以 GR00T N1.7 在两个仅 landmark 词不同的 LIBERO-Spatial 任务上区分“选错物体”和“任务未完成”两类失败，并通过在视觉语言模型与动作生成器之间的连接器前后替换/遮蔽 token 向量及微调来定位脆弱点。结论是指令语义能传到物体选择，但不能保证任务完成；鲁棒性需闭环评估，并分别评分选择与完成。

Details

Motivation: 现有 VLA 对改写敏感，但仅看成功率无法判断失败是物体选择错误还是动作完成失败，也无法指导在策略何处干预。论文要定位 landmark 同义词导致的脆弱发生在 VLA 的哪个环节。

Result: 未在训练指令中出现的同义词仍能把策略引向正确碗，但某任务闭环完成率仅 67.6%，原词为 100%；连接器前粘贴 landmark 向量几乎总能翻转选择，连接器后单独粘贴 landmark、其余指令或图像 token 很少改变选择，任意两者组合才会。用多样化 landmark 词微调可修复同义词完成率，且只训练连接器或将多样化微调策略的连接器复制到原指令策略即可；开环起始帧评分会漏掉连接器外权重的修复作用。

Insight: 创新在于把改写鲁棒性拆成“物体选择”和“任务完成”并分别评估，结合连接器前后干预定位语义到动作的传递瓶颈。客观看，其价值在于揭示 VLA 中语义选择与动作完成解耦、连接器是重要但非唯一干预点，并强调闭环执行评估的重要性。

Abstract: VLA policies are fragile to rewording. Success rates alone cannot say whether a policy fails to choose the object or to complete the task, nor where in the policy to intervene. We study the released GR00T N1.7 on two LIBERO-Spatial tasks differing only in the landmark word telling which of two bowls to take. Synonyms absent from the training instructions send the policy to the bowl they name, unlike made-up words, yet complete 67.6% of one task’s closed-loop episodes, against the original word’s 100%. Before the connector between the vision-language model and the action generator, replacing only the landmark tokens’ vectors with the other landmark’s (pasting) sends nearly every episode to the other bowl. After the connector, pasting at the landmark, the remaining instruction, or the image tokens alone rarely moves the choice, but any two do. Hiding the landmark tokens from the action generator after the connector still lowers completion for the synonyms that fall short. Finetuning on diversified landmark words repairs that one task’s completion for synonyms absent from finetuning; finetuning on the original instructions does not. Training only the connector on the diversified words suffices, as does copying the connector of a policy fully finetuned on them into one finetuned on the original instructions. An open-loop score on starting frames misses that weights outside the connector also carry part of the repair. For now, on this evidence, robustness to rewording should be judged in closed loop, with actions executed, intervening on both sides of the connector, scoring choice and completion separately.

[156] Ego2X: Contact-aware Transfer from Egocentric Human Data to Any Robot cs.RO | cs.CVPDF

Xinke Lyu, Bowen Yang, Zhicheng Wang

TL;DR: 论文提出 Ego2X，一个以物体为中心的框架，将单目第一人称人类视频转换为可在灵巧手和平行夹爪机器人上物理执行的数据。其核心是 CORE 接触中心物体交互表示，记录物体表面接触锚点、相对力、抓取模式和交互状态，并用于校正手物漂移、引导逆运动学实现接触以及保持被持物体。

Details

Motivation: 具身模型依赖真实交互数据，但在物理机器人上采集慢且昂贵；现有自我中心到机器人流程主要按视觉外观判断保真度，常导致手指悬空、夹爪抓空等物理不可执行问题。论文认为应关注物体如何运动以及驱动运动的接触。

Result: 在 HOT3D 上，接触驱动校正将接触中手部深度误差降低 33.7%；在 EgoDex 上实现 62.9% 的标注接触，超过官方 HuRo 流程的 27.9%；在 RoboTwin 2.0 上仅用 Ego2X 片段后训练 X-VLA，即将 200 个配对场景任务成功率从零样本 40.5% 提升至 67.0%。

Insight: 创新点在于用 CORE 以物体表面接触锚点、相对力、抓取模式和交互状态统一表示接触，并将接触约束贯穿手物漂移校正、逆运动学重定向和机械臂合成中的物体保持。客观看，其价值在于把自我中心数据重定向的评价从视觉相似转向物理接触可执行性。

Abstract: Embodied models depend on real interaction data, yet collecting it on physical robots is slow and labor-intensive. Recent ego-to-robot pipelines retarget first-person view human videos to robot data via transferring hand and arm motion. They judge fidelity mainly by visual appearance. However, trajectories often fail in terms of physics: fingers hover beside objects, and grippers close on empty space. We argue that how the object moves and the contacts that drive the motion count. Thus, we present Ego2X, an object-centric framework that converts monocular egocentric video into physically executable robot data across dexterous hands and parallel grippers. Ego2X centers on CORE (Contact-centric Object-interaction REpresentation), which records each contact on the object by its surface anchor, as well as relative force, grasp mode, and interaction state. Throughout the pipeline, CORE corrects hand-object drift via object-relative constraints, steers inverse kinematics toward contacts the robot must realize, and preserves held objects during arm erasure and robot compositing. On HOT3D, contact-driven correction reduces in-contact hand depth error by 33.7%, while on EgoDex, contact-driven retargeting realizes 62.9% of annotated contacts, surpassing the official HuRo pipeline (27.9%). In RoboTwin 2.0, post-training X-VLA on Ego2X episodes alone, without simulator demonstrations, raises task success rate from 40.5% zero-shot to 67.0% across 200 paired scenes. We will publicly release our conversion pipeline and CORE-annotated dataset for dexterous hands and parallel grippers.

[157] Towards Pose-Robust Few-Shot Vision-Language-Action Models cs.RO | cs.AI | cs.CVPDF

Wenhao Li, Zhongyi Han, Yuhao Wei, Liang Han, Yilong Yin

TL;DR: 论文关注少样本微调的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型对相机与机器人基座位姿变化的脆弱性，指出相机被碰歪或基座重新对接本质上都是相机-基座外参发生漂移。作者提出 WACO 框架：利用正运动学得到的机械臂作为度量标定目标、静态场景作为世界锚点，从单帧 RGB-D 图像中无真值外参地估计相机与基座的偏移，将图像规范化到标定视角、状态/动作/起始位姿规范化到标定基座坐标系，并在自身估计误差下微调原策略。同时提出首个带分级相机、基座及复合偏移的 RGB VLA 基准 LIBERO-PoseShift。

Details

Motivation: 少样本示教微调的 VLA 策略会隐含假设相机与机器人基座在数据采集后保持原位，一旦三脚架被碰或基座移动几厘米就会抓错位置；现有方法缺少对这类位姿漂移的系统建模与评测基准。

Result: 在 LIBERO-PoseShift、LIBERO-Plus 相机轨道及 UR5e 真实机器人四项任务上，WACO 均优于标准少样本微调与已有视角鲁棒方法，仿真偏移场景成功率提升 13.2 个百分点、真机提升 36.7 个百分点，分别恢复相对真值 oracle 差距的 90% 与 87%，在无扰动训练数据、无测试时标定的方法中达到 SOTA。

Insight: 创新点在于把相机移动与基座移动统一为相机-基座外参变化这一现象，并巧妙复用机器人自身（正运动学机械臂作标定物、静态场景作世界锚点）完成自标定与规范化，无需真值外参或额外数据；同时引入分级位姿偏移基准填补评测空白，且仅在估计器误差下微调原策略、不改变模型结构，工程可迁移性强。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a vision-language-action (VLA) model on a few dozen demonstrations is the standard way to put a manipulation policy on a robot, yet the resulting policy silently assumes that the camera and the robot base stay exactly where they were during data collection: a bumped tripod or a base re-docked a few centimetres away makes it reach for the wrong place. We show that both failures are one phenomenon, a change of the camera-to-base extrinsic that the policy has memorised, and that the robot carries everything needed to undo it. We introduce WACO, a world-anchored canonicalization framework for VLA models: the arm, placed by forward kinematics at the measured joint angles, serves as a metric calibration target, and the static scene serves as the world anchor that tells a moved camera from a moved robot. From a single RGB-D frame, WACO estimates both shifts without ground-truth extrinsics or extra data, canonicalises the image to the calibration viewpoint and the state, action and start pose to the calibration base frame, and fine-tunes the unmodified policy under the artefacts of its own estimator. We also introduce LIBERO-PoseShift, the first benchmark for RGB VLAs with graded camera, base and compound shifts. Across LIBERO-PoseShift, the LIBERO-Plus camera track and four real-world tasks on a UR5e robot, WACO consistently outperforms the standard few-shot recipe and prior viewpoint-robust methods, raising shifted success by 13.2 points in simulation and by 36.7 points on the real robot, recovering 90% of the simulation gap to a ground-truth oracle and 87% of the real-robot gap, and establishing a new state of the art among methods that use no perturbed training data, with no test-time calibration. Project page: https://peacelwh.github.io/WACO/.

Details

Motivation: VLA 策略通过感知-动作闭环控制具身智能体，对抗补丁会破坏动作预测并进一步影响后续观测；现有防御多依赖策略适配或辅助模型，需要额外训练或推理开销。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies control embodied agents through a perception-action loop grounded in physical interaction, where adversarial patches can corrupt action predictions and thereby alter subsequent observations. To mitigate these threats, many existing defenses rely on policy adaptation or auxiliary models, but require additional training or inference. We introduce RECLAIM, a training-free, inference-time defense that uses a frozen VLA’s own attention response to local visual interventions to guide purification during closed-loop embodied manipulation. Our key insight is that attention changes induced by local edits provide feedback for choosing where and how to purify as the robot interacts with its environment. RECLAIM combines this feedback with robot geometry to refine patch localization and select local treatments, then revalidates them as the agent’s embodied actions change the observed scene. We evaluate RECLAIM against patches generated by four adversarial attack methods across multiple manipulation task suites under frozen VLA control. Under adversarial patch attacks, RECLAIM succeeds in at least one rollout on 95% of the evaluated tasks and raises rollout success on LIBERO-Object from 0% without defense to 70.0%. Its mean decision time is only 548.9 ms per control step. These results highlight policy-guided visual purification as an effective approach to defending embodied agents against adversarial patches and recovering closed-loop task execution.

TL;DR: 论文提出统一机器人基础模型Z-sight，将视觉-语言-动作（VLA）与生成式潜在世界建模结合。它在VLM感知当前画面的原生视觉token空间中直接预测未来，并通过mixture-of-transformers耦合预见专家和连续动作专家，推理时联合去噪未来空间token与动作，无需像素重建或外部编码器。

Abstract: We introduce $Z$-sight, a unified robot foundation model that couples vision-language-action (VLA) and generative latent world modeling. Recent latent-space policies avoid pixel generation, yet typically align intermediate features or rely on frozen target encoders, decoupling future prediction from the VLM’s native feature space. $Z$-sight instead predicts the future directly in the visual token space in which its VLM perceives the present. Coupling a foresight expert and a continuous action expert with the VLM in a mixture-of-transformers, $Z$-sight jointly denoises future spatial tokens and actions at inference without pixel reconstruction or external encoders. [160] torchtest2: One-Call Sanity Checks for PyTorch Models,Evaluated on Real and Unseen Architectures cs.SE | cs.CVPDF

Grzegorz Gwardys

TL;DR: 论文提出 torchtest2，一个开源库，可对 PyTorch 模型在单个 batch 上用一次调用运行 18 项健全性检查，并能自动还原所触碰的 PyTorch 状态，还通过插件适配不同模型家族。作者以变异分析等方式，在 torchvision、timm、Hugging Face transformers 的 103 个模型、600 个随机架构和 20 个手写真实 bug 上进行了评估，并与原版 torchtest 检查、单 batch 过拟合、smoke test 和训练监控等方法对比，同时验证了工具在未见架构上的泛化能力。

Details

Motivation: 深度学习代码中许多 bug 是静默的，例如层未被调用、优化器在分类头替换前创建、循环层把 batch 当作时间维、注意力 mask 被忽略等。这些问题不会报错，loss 仍会下降，往往要耗费数天 GPU 时间后才暴露；现有检查工具覆盖面与可移植性不足。

Result: 在冻结工具后，对 191 个从未见过的架构家族模型测试，torchtest2 检出 78% 的注入 bug（95% CI 75–80），显著高于原版 torchtest 的 40% 和训练监控的 48%；在真实模型上，其 batch 独立性检查发现了 timm GCViT 中耦合同一 batch 图像的 bug，同时也暴露出误报。方法存在边界：Stack Overflow 复现的 28 个 PyTorch bug 仅 3 个、TrainCheck 的 18 个静默训练错误仅 3 个在其可及范围内，中位检查耗时 1.0 秒。

Insight: 创新点在于将多种静默 bug 的健全性检查整合为单次调用、单 batch、可自动状态还原且可插拔适配的实现，并系统性地用突变分析、随机架构和真实 bug 构建评测基准来量化检出能力；此外，作者如实报告误报和在真实数据集上的检出上限，为预训练模型测试器的能力边界提供了客观参照。

Abstract: Many bugs in deep learning code are silent: a layer that is never called, an optimizer created before the classification head was replaced, a recurrent layer that treats the batch as time, or an ignored attention mask. The code runs, the loss decreases somewhat, and the defect surfaces, if ever, after days of GPU time. We present torchtest2, an open-source library that runs 18 sanity checks on a single batch with one call, restores the PyTorch state it touches, and adapts to model families through plugins. We evaluate it by mutation analysis with 17 classes of silent bugs on 103 models from torchvision, timm and Hugging Face transformers, on 600 random architectures and on 20 hand-written realistic bugs, against the checks of the original torchtest, overfitting a single batch, a smoke test and a training monitor in the style of AUTOTRAINER and DeepDiagnosis. Every mutant is compared with its healthy counterpart, and intervals come from a bootstrap over models. After freezing the tool, we ran it on 191 models of architecture families it had never seen: counting, for every tool alike, a failing report where the healthy model’s report does not fail, it detected 78% of the injected bugs (95% CI 75-80), against 40% for the original torchtest checks and 48% for the training monitor. On real models its batch-independence check found a bug in timm’s GCViT that couples the images of a batch. Real models also exposed false alarms that neither the tool’s own test suite nor random architectures had revealed. Two independent collections of real silent bugs mark the limits of the approach: only 3 of 28 reproduced PyTorch bugs from Stack Overflow and 3 of the 18 silent training errors released with TrainCheck are within reach of a pre-training model tester. A median check takes 1.0 s. We release the library, the benchmark and all raw results.

Lingzhe Zhang, Tong Jia, Chiming Duan, Minghua He, Leyi Pan

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了大语言模型强化学习运维（LLM RLOps）相关方法，关注RL训练全生命周期中的效率与可靠性问题。作者将现有技术归纳为训练设置、经验收集、奖励计算、策略优化和模型评估五个阶段，并梳理各阶段的运维关注点、管理技术与评估证据。最后总结了可观测性、跨阶段协同、泛化RLOps、多次运行管理和递归自我改进等开放挑战与未来方向。

Motivation: 随着RL被广泛用于提升LLM的推理、指令遵循和任务求解能力，规模化RL训练暴露出分布式执行、反馈计算和学习动态等方面的失败问题，可能浪费算力、拖慢训练并削弱模型改进。现有LLM RLOps技术分散在不同研究领域，缺乏对其运维角色和相互作用的系统综合。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于从运维视角系统化梳理LLM强化学习训练生命周期，将分散技术按五阶段分类并分析其交互。其客观价值在于把RLOps的关注范围从单次训练运行扩展到多样训练设置、共享基础设施和连续改进周期，为后续研究提供结构化框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become increasingly important for improving the reasoning, instruction-following, and task-solving capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, scaling RL training exposes failures in distributed execution, feedback computation, and learning dynamics that can waste computational resources, slow training, and undermine model improvements. These challenges have motivated a growing body of techniques for LLM RLOps, which manages the efficiency and reliability of operations throughout the LLM reinforcement learning training lifecycle. Despite growing interest, these techniques remain scattered across research areas, with limited synthesis of their operational roles and interactions. To bridge this gap, we present a systematic survey of LLM RLOps methods. We organize existing approaches into five stages: Training Setup, Experience Collection, Reward Computation, Policy Optimization, and Model Evaluation. Within each stage, we examine operational concerns, management techniques, and evaluation evidence. Building on this taxonomy, we identify open challenges and future directions in training process observability, cross-stage coordination, generalizable RLOps, multi-run management, and RLOps for recursive self-improvement. These directions extend operational management from individual training runs to diverse training settings, shared infrastructure, and successive improvement cycles. We maintain a GitHub repository indexing relevant papers and open resources at https://github.com/zhanglingzhe0820/Awesome-RLOps.

[162] Option-GRPO: Hierarchical RL Against AI Slop in Code Generation for Unseen SDKs cs.SE | cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Prashank Kadam

TL;DR: 论文提出 Option-GRPO，一种分层强化学习框架，仅利用未见过 SDK 的源代码，将代码 LLM 适配到新 SDK，并同时优化功能正确性与代码优雅性。其核心是把 SDK 源码中的可复用代码模式建模为分层“代码选项”，作为课程学习单元和策略动作空间，再用 GRPO 和执行奖励逐层学习 SDK 惯用法、组合模式并生成完整程序。

Details

Motivation: LLM 生成的代码常出现冗长、冗余、非惯用的 AI slop，虽能通过功能检查却膨胀代码库；在训练截止后发布的新 SDK 上缺乏惯用先验和组合理解，问题更严重。论文希望无需人工使用示例或测试用例，仅从 SDK 源码学习，同时兼顾正确性和简洁性。

Result: 在 12 个未见 SDK 和 32B 参数基座模型上，Option-GRPO 达到 61.4% pass@1，比 flat GRPO 高 8.7 个点，比 ExploraCoder 高 11.2 个点，比 ReCode 高 6.3 个点；相较 flat GRPO，生成方案 API 调用少 34.7%、代码短 27%，且正确率更高。

Insight: 创新点在于从 SDK 源码中提取组合式分层代码选项，将其同时用于课程学习和高效策略优化，并给出半马尔可夫决策过程建模、简洁性/API 效率界以及信用分配改进分析。可借鉴之处是把代码模式层级化作为 RL 动作空间与课程结构，以执行反馈自动学习新 SDK 惯用法并抑制冗余生成。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly produce AI slop in generated code: verbose, redundant, non-idiomatic implementations that pass functional checks but bloat codebases. The problem compounds on SDKs released after the model’s training cutoff, where the model has neither idiomatic priors nor compositional understanding. We present Option-GRPO, a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework that adapts a code LLM to an unseen SDK from its source code alone, without human-written usage examples or test cases, and that explicitly optimizes for both functional correctness and code elegance. Our key insight is that SDK source code contains compositional structure that defines a hierarchy of reusable code patterns (code options) which simultaneously serve as the building blocks of a curriculum and the action space for efficient policy optimization. We show that code options induce a valid semi-Markov decision process, give conditional bounds under which shorter option description length implies more concise and, in expectation, more API-efficient programs, and analyze under stated assumptions how the option hierarchy improves credit assignment by a factor of $\sqrt{T/K}$. Using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with execution-based rewards at each curriculum level, Option-GRPO discovers SDK idioms, composes them into patterns, and assembles patterns into complete programs. Experiments on 12 unseen SDKs with a 32B-parameter base model demonstrate 61.4% pass@1, outperforming flat GRPO by 8.7 points, ExploraCoder by 11.2 points, and ReCode by 6.3 points. Option-GRPO produces solutions that use 34.7% fewer API calls and are 27% shorter than flat-GRPO outputs while maintaining higher correctness, directly attacking the slop problem.

[163] Streaming Generative Video Compression with Embedding Alignment and In-Context Propagation eess.IV | cs.CV | cs.MMPDF

Zhitao Wang, Hengyu Man, Han Liu, Wenrui Li, Debin Zhao

TL;DR: 论文提出 SGVC，一种面向超低码率的流式生成视频压缩框架。它将预训练潜视频 DiT 改造成因果像素域细化器，通过嵌入对齐把像素编解码器特征投影到 DiT 输入空间，并用轻量重建路径绕过 VAE 解码器。方法还引入上下文传播，跨帧复用逐层 K/V 状态以利用历史生成上下文，从而在保持因果解码的同时提升时序一致性。

Details

Motivation: 超低码率下保持视频感知质量十分困难；现有基于视频 DiT 的生成式视频压缩依赖计算昂贵的 VAE 解码器，且多帧联合细化会引入额外缓冲延迟。论文旨在降低计算开销并实现无片段缓冲的流式因果解码。

Result: 实验表明 SGVC 具有有竞争力的率-感知性能，在 DISTS 指标上相对 S²VC 平均取得 71.97% 的 BD-rate 节省。

Insight: 创新点包括将预训练潜视频 DiT 适配为因果像素域细化器、通过嵌入对齐弥合编解码器嵌入与 DiT 预训练嵌入的表示差距、用轻量重建路径绕过 VAE 解码器，以及复用逐层 K/V 状态实现上下文传播。客观来看，其核心价值在于兼顾生成先验、低延迟流式解码和跨帧时序一致性。

Abstract: Video compression at ultra-low bitrates presents substantial challenges in preserving perceptual quality. Although pretrained video diffusion transformers (DiTs) provide powerful generative priors, existing generative video compression (GVC) methods built on them typically rely on computationally expensive VAE decoders to reconstruct video frames. Moreover, jointly refining multiple frames to exploit the inherent spatiotemporal modeling capabilities of video DiTs introduces additional buffering delay. To address these challenges, we propose SGVC, a streaming generative video compression framework that adapts a pretrained latent video DiT into a causal pixel-space refiner. Specifically, SGVC projects decoded features from a contextual pixel codec directly into the DiT input embedding space. To bridge the representation gap between codec-derived embeddings and pretrained DiT embeddings, we introduce Embedding Alignment, which aligns the projected embeddings with input embeddings extracted from the original frames during training. The refined DiT hidden states are then mapped directly to the pixel domain through a lightweight reconstruction path, bypassing the pretrained VAE decoder. To further exploit the spatiotemporal priors of the video DiT while maintaining causal decoding, we introduce In-Context Propagation, which reuses layer-wise K/V states across frames to exploit historical generative context, improving temporal consistency without requiring clip-wise buffering. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SGVC achieves competitive rate-perception performance, with average BD-rate savings of 71.97% over S$^2$VC in terms of DISTS. Motivation: 传统AET通常采用体素网格的体积表示，在测量角度有限、电子剂量低等真实成像挑战下不够鲁棒，且难以自然融入原子级物理先验。论文希望以更符合原子本质的表示直接重建原子结构。

Abstract: Atomic electron tomography (AET) computationally reconstructs the 3D atomic structure of materials from a series of 2D projection measurements acquired at different tilt angles. Inspired by Gaussian splatting techniques from computer vision, we introduce an atom-centric parameterization for modeling AET samples. Instead of a conventional volumetric representation with a grid of voxels, we model the sample as a list of atoms, where each atom is a 3D Gaussian characterized by learned location, scale, and amplitude. The Gaussian model is a natural fit for atoms that allows us to easily incorporate prior information grounded in physics; we impose isotropy of atoms, a minimum interatomic distance, and realistic atomic radii. We show in simulation that our method is more robust than volumetric baselines to real-world imaging challenges, including a limited number of measurements and low electron dose. We further show that our method yields a more physically plausible reconstruction of an experimental 23,000 atom iron-platinum nanoparticle than a previously published volumetric reconstruction. Beyond tomography, our representation is a promising tool for any atomic resolution imaging modality (e.g. multislice electron ptychography).
